## Genuine skip: Spotify (2026-09-13)

Investigated live per the "verify, don't guess" discipline — `lifeatspotify.com/jobs` is a Next.js SPA with no jobs embedded in its `__NEXT_DATA__` (client-fetched), so the real API was found by pulling the page's own JS chunks rather than assuming an ATS from the branding: `pages/jobs-*.js` imports a helper module (chunk `741-*.js`) whose `searchJobs()` function calls a first-party WordPress REST API, `https://api.lifeatspotify.com/wp-json/animal/v1/job/search` — not a third-party ATS at all, an in-house "Animal" plugin, same class of finding as trivago's in-house Divi plugin. The endpoint is a clean, unauthenticated GET returning the full JSON job pool directly (`result[]` with `id`/`text`/`main_category`/`sub_category`/`locations[]`/`job_type`), no pagination params needed.

Pulled the entire unfiltered global pool (no keyword/location params applied): **62 total open positions worldwide**, and the complete distinct-location set across every job is `Boston, Ho Chi Minh, Istanbul, Jakarta, London, Los Angeles, New York, San Francisco, Seoul, Singapore, Stockholm, Taiwan, Taiwan (Home Mix), Toronto, United States of America (Home Mix)` — zero jobs, and zero location values, name India in any form (no city, no "Remote - India", no "India (Home Mix)"). This is the same disposition class as Euroclear/CVS Health/Temenos/TripAdvisor's Gurgaon-office case, but a level further than TripAdvisor: Spotify's own `allFilters.locations` facet (surfaced separately in the page's `__NEXT_DATA__`) doesn't even list a defined-but-empty India office — there's no India entity in the data at all, confirmed against the full 62-job pool rather than a filtered subset. Spotify has no India office and does not appear to hire India-based remote engineering roles through its official pipeline.

No fetcher, registry entry, config section, or seen-jobs file created. Worth a from-scratch recheck later if Spotify opens an India office/entity or starts posting India-eligible remote roles.

## Agoda (Booking Holdings) added (2026-09-13)

Investigated live rather than assumed. The branded `careersatagoda.com` domain is Cloudflare-managed-challenge-gated (HTTP 403 "Just a moment..." even with a browser User-Agent) — but the real ATS was found via a plain web search of the company's own apply links: a standard public Greenhouse board at `job-boards.greenhouse.io/agoda`. Confirmed directly against `boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/agoda/jobs?content=true`: fully open to plain HTTP, no challenge, no Playwright anywhere — same easy REST-API shape as Razorpay/Groww/Pinterest/Instacart/TripAdvisor already in this repo.

The full global board is ~291 jobs, cached once in-module like every other Greenhouse integration here. India presence is small but real: 8 jobs mention a Gurgaon/Gurugram/India location, mostly HR/ops/WFM roles plus two genuine "Staff Software Engineer - Backend" postings. One of those two ("Staff Software Engineer - Backend-Heavy Full Stack (Gurugram Based)", job 7431306) explicitly lists C# as one of its accepted BFF-layer languages (alongside Kotlin/Java) — a real, if loosely-gated, `.NET / C#` primary-skill match, not a Walmart-style irrelevant boilerplate list. Location quirks handled the same way as Razorpay: some India postings show the bare "IND" country code or no country word at all ("Gurugram, IND | Gurugram") rather than "India" — normalized via the same recognized-city whitelist (never blindly, so ambiguous multi-country combos like "Gurgaon, Bangkok, Pune & Kuala Lumpur" are correctly left unmatched by `is_india_job()`). "Gurgaon/ Pune, India" is correctly dropped by `exclude_locations` due to the embedded "Pune" substring, consistent with the conservative Pune-exclusion policy applied everywhere else.

Live validation (`--validate --companies agoda` and a direct `matcher.find_matching_jobs` call against `agoda_fetcher`): 291 jobs fetched, 1 genuine match (`[.NET / C#]` tag, Gurugram location), no Pune/Kochi/Chandigarh/Chennai/Tamil Nadu leakage in the matched result, near-miss log shows correct `[exclude]`/`[title family]`/`[skill]` tags on the other 7 India-flavored postings. No `require_tech_in_description` added — not warranted at this volume.

## Airbnb added (2026-09-13)

Verified live rather than guessed Greenhouse (Airbnb's careers page has no third-party ATS branding at all). `careers.airbnb.com/positions/` is WordPress with the FacetWP plugin. The obvious-looking `wp-json/facetwp/v1/refresh` REST route (visible in `FWP_JSON.ajaxurl`) is a dead end — it returns an empty `[]` for every payload tried. Reading the plugin's own `front.min.js` showed why: since `FWP.template === "wp"`, the real AJAX call POSTs `{"action":"facetwp_refresh","data":{...}}` as JSON straight back to the listing page URL itself (`https://careers.airbnb.com/positions/`), not the REST route — the response's `template` field is the entire re-rendered page HTML, with job rows in `ul.job-list li`. Filtering server-side on the `where_you_work: ["india"]` facet option returns a small, stable India pool (8 open roles at verification time, out of 164 global), so `airbnb_fetcher.py` caches the full India set once and ignores keyword (added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS`).

No genuine posting-date field exists: the detail page's JSON-LD `datePublished` is the render/crawl timestamp, confirmed identical-to-"now" across three different job IDs fetched seconds apart — not the job's real post date. `posting_date` is left as `""`, same convention already used by `progresssoftware_fetcher.py`.

Live validation (`--validate --companies airbnb` and a direct `run_company.run_company_pipeline` call against a throwaway seen-state path with a no-op notifier, so nothing was sent and `seen_jobs_airbnb.json` was never touched): 8 India jobs fetched, correctly categorized in the near-miss log (`[exclude]` for Manager/Supervisor/Lead titles, `[title family]` for two more, `[broad-only]` for a "Senior Software Engineer(AI/ML), Trust" role whose only AI/ML terms are the broad `Python`/`machine learning` signal with no hard `primary_skills` term, `[10+ yoe]` for a Payments role requiring 10+ years). 0 current matches — a true reflection of Airbnb's small India engineering pool on this date, not a fetcher defect; the pipeline is wired correctly and will alert the moment a qualifying `.NET/C#` or `LangChain`/`RAG`-flavored India posting appears. No Pune/Kochi/Chandigarh/Chennai/Tamil Nadu leakage (only Bangalore/Gurugram locations seen). No `require_tech_in_description` added — too little verified volume to justify it.

## Genuine skip (re-confirmed): Yatra (2026-09-13)

Re-investigated on explicit request rather than trusting the Wave 16 (2026-09-07) finding at face value. `www.yatra.com` itself is Akamai-fingerprint-gated at the connection layer — plain `curl`/`requests` against `/careers` and even `/robots.txt` hang and reset (HTTP/2 stream errors, then a full timeout under HTTP/1.1) the same way Honeywell's Oracle HCM CE tenant does, so the branded page can't be probed directly without a real browser. Instead probed every ATS vendor pattern this repo knows by guessing tenant slugs directly, the same discipline used for the OYO skip: `boards.greenhouse.io/yatra` and its `job-boards.greenhouse.io/yatra` redirect target both 404, `jobs.lever.co/yatra` 404s, `yatra.wd3.myworkdayjobs.com` 406s (no such tenant), `yatraonline.darwinbox.in` redirects to a bare `/user/login` (the standard Darwinbox "tenant doesn't exist" fallback, not a real board), and `yatra.zohorecruit.in` redirects to a `/careers` path that itself renders "Page does not exist." No live ATS tenant resolves under any vendor this repo has previously integrated.

This corroborates, rather than overturns, the Wave 16 finding: Yatra's own careers presence is a static lead-capture/resume-drop form with hardcoded dropdowns and no job-list API behind it (confirmed there via Playwright), and the branded domain's bot-gating means even a from-scratch vendor-guessing pass finds nothing live to build against. No fetcher, registry entry, config section, or `seen_jobs_yatra.json` created — same disposition as SpiceJet/OYO from the same wave. Worth a from-scratch recheck only if Yatra migrates its careers presence onto a real third-party ATS in the future.

## DoorDash added (2026-09-13)

Investigated live rather than guessed. `careersatdoordash.com` is Cloudflare-managed-challenge-gated (HTTP 403 `cf-mitigated: challenge`), but the underlying ATS is Greenhouse — found the real tenant token by web-searching live `careersatdoordash.com/jobs/...` URLs rather than assuming the obvious slug. DoorDash's *global* board (`boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/doordashusa/jobs`, 456 reqs) has **zero genuine India locations** — its only "india"-looking hits are the classic `\bindia\b` false-positive shape, "Indianapolis, IN" (×3), correctly not matched by this repo's word-boundary India check. The real India hiring lives on a completely separate, smaller tenant: `doordashindia` (confirmed live, ~21 reqs), found only via a web search of an actual `careersatdoordash.com` job permalink, not a URL-shape guess. DoorDash's 2022 acquisition of Wolt is Europe/APAC-focused (its own Greenhouse office list has no India entries tied to it) and not relevant here.

`doordash_fetcher.py` follows the same cache-once REST-API pattern as `towerresearch_fetcher.py`/`worldquant_fetcher.py`: `GET .../jobs?content=true` once per process, keyword ignored (added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS`), descriptions already inline in the same payload (added to `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS`). Not added to `_SUPPORTS_LOCATION` (no location query param used), `_NEWEST_FIRST` (sort order not verified), or `_USES_PLAYWRIGHT` (plain HTTP works fine — the Cloudflare gate only affects the branded frontend, never the Greenhouse API itself).

Live validation (`--validate --companies doordash` and a direct `run_company.run_company_pipeline` call against a throwaway seen-state path with a no-op notifier — `seen_jobs_doordash.json` was never touched): 21 India jobs fetched, all genuinely Hyderabad-based (one listing has a source-side typo, "Hyderbad, TG", missing "India" entirely — correctly and silently dropped by `is_india_job()`, not a fetcher bug). Near-miss log confirms every non-engineering title correctly caught (`[exclude]` for two Manager titles, `[title family]` for the rest — HR/Recruiting/Ops/Compensation roles), and the one real engineering posting ("Software Engineer, Data Engineer II") correctly fails as `[broad-only] (skills=Python)` — its JD mentions Python/SQL/Scala/Airflow/Spark but no hard `.NET/C#` or `AI/ML` `primary_skills` term. 0 current matches — a true reflection of this small, Hyderabad-only board's contents on this date, not a fetcher defect. No Pune/Kochi/Chandigarh/Chennai/Tamil Nadu leakage. No `require_tech_in_description` added — far too little verified volume (21 jobs total, 1 engineering) to justify it.

## Genuine skip: Goibibo (2026-09-13)

Goibibo (Ibibo Group Pvt Ltd) is an OTA brand owned by the MakeMyTrip Group, so the first question was whether it's already covered by `makemytrip_fetcher.py`'s existing job pool. Checked live: `careers.makemytrip.com/api/jobs`'s `group_company` field only ever returns `"MakeMyTrip (India) Limited"` or `"Hotelcloud Services Private Limited"` across all 41 current postings — no Goibibo-branded entity appears, so Goibibo jobs are NOT already surfaced through the MMT wrapper and a distinct integration would be needed if feasible.

**Infeasible — exhaustively checked across independent methods and network origins.** `goibibo.com`'s own careers surface (`/careers/`, `/careers/teams`, bare `/`) is unreachable by every method tried: plain `curl` either times out completely after the TLS handshake (30s, 0 bytes — a silent tarpit drop, not a fast reject) or returns a fake `504 Gateway Time-out` body containing a `<script src="/CgGWa1/.../DSpaWm/...">` — a known Akamai Bot Manager JS-challenge-failure page dressed up as a 504, not a real upstream timeout; headless Chromium fails outright with `net::ERR_HTTP2_PROTOCOL_ERROR`; headless Firefox (installed fresh for this check) reaches the server but gets the identical fake-504 challenge page as curl; and Anthropic's own `WebFetch` tool — a completely separate network origin from this sandbox — times out on both `/careers/` and the bare domain root. Four independent client types (curl, Chromium, Firefox, a third-party fetch service) from at least two different network origins all failed to retrieve real content, which is the same "genuinely inaccessible" bar used for prior genuine skips (Euroclear, CVS Health, Temenos) rather than a single-method guess. `api.goibibo.com` was also probed as a possible API host — it returns a genuine Akamai edge "DNS failure" error, i.e. that hostname isn't configured as a real origin at all, not a lead worth pursuing.

No fetcher, registry entry, config.yaml section, or `seen_jobs_goibibo.json` was created — there is no live channel to build against today. If Goibibo's careers site becomes reachable (or migrates to a discoverable ATS subdomain) later, re-check both this bot-gating and whether it's since been folded into the shared MakeMyTrip wrapper before building a dedicated fetcher.

## Grab (2026-09-13): Southeast Asian super-app, custom in-house ATS

Investigated live from scratch rather than assuming a third-party vendor. `grab.careers` (canonical host `www.grab.careers`) is a fully server-rendered, plain-HTML site with no Greenhouse/Lever/Workday/iCIMS/etc. markers anywhere in the page or its bundled JS — a genuinely custom, in-house careers platform (`/phb/app.js...` branding, no recognizable ATS vendor string). No Playwright needed: a plain `requests.get` on `/en/jobs/` returns full server-rendered job cards.

The listing page has real, verified server-side filtering: `?country=India` narrows to India-only postings (confirmed against the full option list, which also includes Cambodia/Indonesia/Malaysia/Philippines/Singapore/Thailand/Vietnam/China/Romania — Grab's actual SEA-plus-GCC footprint), and `?search=<term>` genuinely narrows results server-side too (`search=engineer` returned 3/7 India jobs; a nonsense keyword returned 0). Pagination is `page=N` with a `pagesize` param. Grab does have real India-based engineering hiring — confirmed live, not assumed — but the current India pool is small: **7 total live postings, all in Bangalore** (Embassy Tech Village), no excluded cities.

Job-detail pages embed the full posting as a schema.org `JobPosting` JSON-LD block (`<script id="js-job-posting" type="application/ld+json">`) with both a complete HTML `description` and a `datePosted` field — one clean request per job, no separate API needed. One HTML wrinkle: the raw page source HTML-entity-escapes the `+` in the script's `type` attribute (`application/ld&#x2B;json`), which only matters if matching on the raw attribute string — parsing via BeautifulSoup's own `id` lookup sidesteps it entirely.

Of the 7 India postings, only the two "Senior AI Engineer" listings (Bangalore, Data Science team) pass every filter layer — both genuinely reference LangChain, LangGraph, and generative AI in the JD body, a real `[AI / ML / Python]` match. The other 5 (Solutions Manager/Solution Manager/Solutions Specialist roles in Finance Systems and People Systems — ERP/ServiceNow/Oracle-adjacent, not software engineering) are correctly rejected: 2 by `exclude_terms` ("manager"), 3 by `title_family` (no engineer/developer-shaped title). No .NET/C# postings currently exist in Grab's India pool; the `AI / ML / Python` track is the only one currently represented.

**Registry/config changes:** `_PIPELINE_DATA` (`grab`, alphabetically between `google` and `groww`); not added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` (server-side keyword search genuinely narrows), `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS` (description lives only on the detail page, not the listing), `_SUPPORTS_LOCATION`, `_NEWEST_FIRST`, or `_USES_PLAYWRIGHT` (plain HTTP, no browser). `config.yaml` gained `grab_search` (no `require_tech_in_description` — no verified reason to add it). `seen_jobs_grab.json` created as `[]`. Validated with `python src/run_all.py --validate --companies grab` (ok) and a direct fetch → matcher-function walk (no notifier, no seen-state advancement) over all 7 live India postings: near-miss log tags (`[exclude term]`, `[title family fail]`) fired correctly with no false positives, the 2 genuine matches both got the correct `[AI / ML / Python]` tag with a sane `format_message` rendering, and no Chennai/Pune/Chandigarh/Kochi/Tamil Nadu leakage anywhere in the fetched set. `tests/test_company_registry_runner.py`'s hardcoded registry-count assertion was bumped from 279 to 280 to match the true count after this addition (full suite: 507 passed).

## Genuine skips: SBI, PNB, Bank of Baroda, Canara Bank — Indian PSU banks (2026-09-13)

Investigated all four as a group per an explicit brief to take the "SBI SDE / specialist officer" track seriously rather than assume a skip, since this repo already has four *private*-sector Indian bank fetchers (Axis, HDFC, ICICI, Kotak — all RippleHire/Oracle HCM CE/in-house-SPA tenants with rolling, individually-apply-able reqs). **All four PSU banks were confirmed live, then skipped** — none exposes the kind of continuously-open, individually-applicable job posting this repo's `fetch_jobs()` model needs. The mechanism is structurally different from the private banks, and the difference generalizes to any other Indian PSU bank checked in the future (see "Reusable pattern" below).

**SBI** — official careers portal is `sbi.bank.in/web/careers` (the marketing domain `sbi.co.in/web/careers` and `bank.sbi/web/careers` both 301-redirect here). `/web/careers/current-openings` lists recruitment notices chronologically: Probationary Officers, Junior Associates, Circle Based Officers, Specialist Cadre Officers (SCO — includes real IT-flavored titles: Deputy Manager/Manager (Systems) for networking/cloud/IT-architecture/InfoSec, Digital Platforms/UI-UX, Data Scientist, DBA), Apprentices, and retired-officer re-engagements. Every single one of these — including the SCO/IT ones — is a **PDF advertisement plus an IBPS-registration-portal apply link**, with a fixed application window, a written exam and/or interview, call letters, and a results/joining-schedule cycle. There is no page listing individual open reqs a candidate can browse and apply to directly the way a Greenhouse/Workday/RippleHire board works — "Current Openings" is a notice-board of periodic drives, not a job board.

**PNB** — official portal is `pnb.bank.in/recruitments.aspx` (marketing domain `pnbindia.in/Recruitment.html` redirects to a dead page on the same host, `pnb.bank.in`, confirming this is the live domain). Same shape as SBI: a chronological list of drive notices (Management Trainees, Specialist Officers — including a live 2026 cycle for 30 SO vacancies with an IT sub-stream alongside Law/HR/Marketing — Customer Service Associates, Local Bank Officers, contractual Defence Banking Advisors), each a PDF advertisement with an IBPS-portal apply link and a written-exam-based selection process. The specific 30-SO drive found live during this check (`madeeasy.in`-mirrored PDF, application window 21 Apr–5 May 2026) is IT/Law/HR/Marketing specialist officers selected purely by written exam — exactly the "banking-officer recruitment drive" pattern the brief said not to count, not an SDE job posting.

**Canara Bank** — official portal is `canarabank.bank.in/pages/recruitment` (marketing domain `canarabank.com` now resolves to this same host). Notices found live include IBPS-CRP POs/CSAs/Clerks, an active "Recruitment Project-1/2025 — Engagement of Specialist Officers on contract basis," and past IBPS-CRP-XIV cycles explicitly for "IT Officers." A 2025 Specialist Cadre Officer drive genuinely covers real tech job titles (Application Developer, Cloud Administrator, Cloud Security Analyst, Data Analyst/Engineer/Scientist, DBA, Ethical Hacker/Pen Tester, ETL Specialist, Network/InfoSec Analyst) — the closest of the four to a real SDE-track hire — but it is still delivered as one dated PDF advertisement with a fixed application window and, per every source describing it, a written-exam/interview selection process, not a rolling job board. No mechanism exists on the live site to browse currently-open individual tech reqs outside these periodic drive windows.

**Bank of Baroda** — this one looked genuinely different at first and got the deepest check. `bankofbaroda.bank.in/career/current-opportunities` (marketing domain `bankofbaroda.in/career` redirects here) is **not** a static PDF list — it renders from a `var glblMasterCareerDetails = [...]` JSON array embedded directly in the page HTML (445 records live at check time), with real structured fields (`prfile` title, `fntnName` function/department, `city`, `vacan` vacancy count, `lDate`/`eDate` dates, `cta` detail-page link, `apCta` apply-button HTML) and working Department/Zone/Region/City filters including an explicit "Digital Technology" checkbox — the closest thing to a live, filterable job board any of the four PSU banks has. Filtering that JSON to `fntnName` containing `information technology` or `digital` surfaces real entries: "Recruitment of Professionals on Regular Basis for Information Technology" (350 vacancies), "...in Enterprise Data Management Office Department" (25), "...in Digital Group Department" (139), etc. **But opening the actual detail page for one of these (`.../recruitment-of-professionals-on-regular-basis-in-information-technology-department-17-01`, Advt. No. BOB/HRM/REC/ADVT/2024/08, 177 IT vacancies) confirms it is the identical drive mechanism as the other three banks**, just rendered as a JS-driven card instead of a flat PDF list: a dated advertisement → online application window (closed 27 Jan 2025) → online written exam → call letters (7 Mar 2025) → interview → results (17 May 2025) → joining. No individual job titles like "Software Developer"/"Engineer" are exposed anywhere in the record — only a department name and a vacancy count for the whole drive. So although the *data format* is structurally closer to a job board than SBI/PNB/Canara's PDF lists, the underlying *hiring mechanism* is the same exam-and-interview specialist-recruitment cycle the brief explicitly said not to count, and there is no rolling "apply anytime to this specific SDE req" pathway to fetch.

**Reusable pattern for any future PSU-bank check:** don't stop at "does the careers page have a structured list" (BoB's does, and it's still a skip) — open an actual detail page/PDF for the tech-flavored drive and check whether the *selection process* is exam/interview-based with a fixed application window (skip — this is banking-officer recruitment, whatever the department name says) versus an open-ended "apply now, get screened, get hired" flow with individually dated postings (would be a genuine add). Every nationalized/PSU Indian bank checked so far (SBI, PNB, Bank of Baroda, Canara) routes ALL hiring — including IT/Digital/Specialist-Officer tracks — through the exam/interview drive model, in contrast to the four private banks already in this repo (Axis, HDFC, ICICI, Kotak), which all expose a normal rolling ATS (RippleHire/Oracle HCM CE/in-house SPA) a candidate applies to directly. IBPS-run drives (`ibpsreg.ibps.in`) are the strongest single tell — if the apply link routes through an `ibps.in` registration portal, it's an exam-based recruitment cycle, not a job posting, regardless of what the department/title says.

No fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_*.json` was created for any of the four. If any of these banks ever exposes a rolling, individually-datable job board for its IT/Digital function specifically (as opposed to periodic advertised drives), it would be a genuine candidate to revisit — but none does today.

## Genuine skips: Energy/Industrial PSUs — ONGC, NTPC, Coal India, SAIL, IOC (2026-09-13)

Investigated all five independently and honestly rather than assuming the whole category is infeasible — each one's own official careers/recruitment page was actually fetched (not guessed from prior PSU knowledge). All five turned out to share the exact same structural disposition, described once here and then per-company below.

**Reusable pattern for any future `.gov.in`/PSU/Maharatna-CPSE check (flagging prominently since other PSU-focused agents are running in parallel):** none of these five organizations run anything resembling a corporate ATS (no Workday/Greenhouse/Lever/iCIMS/Oracle HCM/SuccessFactors/Darwinbox/etc. — none of the five career domains reference any known ATS vendor string, and no hidden JSON/XHR/GraphQL endpoint was found behind any of the five "current openings" pages when checked directly). Central-government-linked PSU hiring for the engineer cadre runs on a **periodic, chronologically-listed PDF-notice model**: a "Recruitment Notice"/"Current Opening"/"Career" page lists dated entries, each linking to a standalone PDF advertisement (a "Detailed Advertisement" or "Advt. No. X/2026") for one discrete recruitment drive — Graduate Engineer/Management Trainee intake via GATE score or an in-house Computer Based Test (CBT), doctors, apprentices, fixed-term specialist/advisor engagements, etc. There is no unified searchable job list with individual postings, per-job location/salary fields, or a stable per-job ID/URL — the "listing" unit is the PDF itself, issued perhaps a handful of times a year, not a live job board with continuous postings. This is structurally incompatible with this repo's fetcher model (`fetch_jobs()` returning per-job dicts with `id`/`title`/`location`/`application_url`) — there is no stable per-job record to fetch, dedupe, or link to. Any future PSU/`.gov.in` check should look for this same shape (a "Recruitment Notice"/dated-PDF list) as the first thing to rule in or out, before assuming an ATS exists at all.

A second, independently-confirmed factor: even where a "Computer Science and Information Technology" discipline exists inside a Graduate Engineer Trainee drive (confirmed for IOC, see below), it is one educational-qualification bucket among ~8-10 core engineering disciplines (Chemical, Civil, Mechanical, Electrical, Instrumentation, Mining, Petroleum, Computer Science/IT, etc.) inside a single unified exam/CBT process — not a distinct, recurring "software engineer" job requisition with its own posting cadence. These organizations are refining/power/mining/steel operators whose IT hiring, where it exists at all, is folded into a generalist GATE/CBT-based Graduate Engineer Trainee pipeline alongside core-engineering disciplines, not run as an independent tech-hiring track the way a bank's or GCC's engineering org is.

### ONGC (Oil and Natural Gas Corporation)

`ongcindia.com/web/eng/career/recruitment-notice` is a chronological list (2019–2026) of dated PDF recruitment notices, no searchable job list, no ATS vendor markers, no hidden API found in the page source (only static asset paths matched an `api`/`json` grep, no live endpoint). Currently-listed notices are Director-level appointments, GATE-based Graduate Trainee (GT) recruitment, contract doctor/medical-officer engagements, NAPS/NATS apprenticeships, and asset-level consultant/junior-consultant roles — **no software engineer, IT, or computer-science-track notice was found in the current listing at all**, not even folded into a GT engineering-discipline bucket the way IOC's is. Genuine skip — no live job-listing mechanism exists, and no visible SDE-track hiring to build against even if one did.

### NTPC (National Thermal Power Corporation)

`careers.ntpc.co.in` → `careers.ntpc.co.in/recruitment/` (302 redirect) is a "Notice Board" of dated PDF advertisements (English + Hindi) plus syllabi/interview-schedule attachments — same shape as ONGC, no ATS, no hidden API. Current/recent notices are Engineering Executive Trainee (GATE-based, core power-engineering disciplines: Electrical, Mechanical, C&I), Diploma/Artisan Trainee intake, Finance/HR trainee drives, medical professionals, and nuclear-field specialists (NTPC's nuclear JV). No software engineer or IT-specific recruitment notice is currently visible, and NTPC's Executive Trainee scheme (per its own recruitment-system page) is explicitly split into only Engineering/Finance/HR tracks — a power-generation company whose "Engineering" track means electrical/mechanical/C&I plant engineering, not software. Genuine skip.

### Coal India Limited (CIL)

`coalindia.in/career-cil/jobs-coal-india/` lists dated PDF notifications (e.g. "Recruitment of Management Trainee based on GATE-2027 Score" — 660 vacancies across Civil/Mechanical/Electrical/System E&T/etc. disciplines; 125 Industrial Trainee vacancies across CIL's coalfield subsidiaries) — same PDF-notice shape, no ATS, no live job list, no location/salary fields inline. The one IT-adjacent item found across the whole notice history is a single senior advisory engagement ("Advisor (ICT), CIL" — a fixed-term consultant appointment, not a recurring engineer requisition). No software-engineer-track hiring exists on CIL's own recruitment channel at any cadence; its "System E&T" (Electronics & Telecommunication/Systems) discipline inside the Management Trainee GATE drive is the closest analog and is itself just one bucket in a single periodic exam-based intake, not an independent tech pipeline. Genuine skip.

### SAIL (Steel Authority of India)

`sailcareers.com` (SAIL's dedicated careers portal, separate from `sail.co.in`) shows the same periodic-PDF-notice model, plus plant-specific application portals (Rourkela/Bokaro/ISP, etc.) linked per drive (e.g. "Young Professionals at Bokaro Steel Plant Advt. No BSL/R-YP/2026-01"). Current visible openings are Young Professionals (various disciplines), Management Trainee (Technical), medical consultants/GDMOs, and sports coaches — **no IT/software engineer track appears anywhere**, current or historical, on this portal. SAIL is a pure steel-manufacturing operator; its technical hiring is metallurgical/mechanical/electrical plant engineering, and no page, PDF title, or discipline list found during this check names computer science, IT, or software engineering at all. Genuine skip — the clearest "no SDE track exists" case of the five.

### IOC (Indian Oil Corporation)

`iocl.com` itself 307-redirects for every automated client tried (`curl`, WebFetch) — likely a bot-detection/geo gate on the branded frontend, consistent with the pattern other PSU/Indian-corporate sites in this repo show (Akamai-style edge behavior) — but the actual recruitment PDF, directly linked from search results and fetched successfully (`iocl.com/admin/img/UploadedFiles/LatestJobOpening/Files/DetailedAd14082026.pdf`, `Advt. No.: IOCL/CO-HR/RECTT/2026/01`, dated 14 Aug 2026), is a single 31-page "Recruitment of Executives... through Computer Based Test (CBT) - 2026" advertisement covering Graduate Engineer Grade 'A' intake across ~8 disciplines. **"Computer Science and Information Technology" is explicitly one of the listed disciplines** (accepted degree titles include Computer Engineering, Computer Science and Engineering, Information Technology, and even "Computer Science and Engineering (Artificial Intelligence and Machine Learning)") — so unlike the other four, IOC does have a defined CS/IT hiring bucket. However, this is still not a live job-listing mechanism in this repo's sense: it is one dated CBT drive covering all disciplines together (an exam-based, GATE/CBT-score-driven Graduate Engineer Trainee intake, not distinct per-role postings), advertised via a single PDF with no per-job ID, location-per-posting, or ongoing/rolling cadence — a future recruitment round would be an entirely new PDF/advertisement number, not a new row appearing in a stable job feed. `iocl.com/latest-job-opening` and `iocl.com/careers-overview` (both bot-gated to automated fetches, confirmed via repeated 307s) corroborate the same PDF-notice-board shape seen via search-engine caching of the page. Genuine skip — closest of the five to having an SDE-relevant hiring track, but the mechanism itself is fundamentally incompatible with a job-watcher/fetcher model, not merely undiscovered.

No fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_*.json` was created for any of the five. If any of these PSUs migrates its recruitment presence onto a real applicant-tracking system with individually addressable postings (unlikely given how centralized-government hiring is structured, but not impossible), re-check using the "Recruitment Notice"/PDF-list signal documented above as the first thing to look past.

## Godrej Industries Group added (2026-09-13)

Investigated which Godrej entity has the real, most SDE-relevant careers pipeline. `godrej.com/careers` is a bare landing page that only links out to the two post-July-2024-restructuring group portals:

- **Godrej Enterprises Group** (the industrial/auto/appliances/security side: Aerospace, Locks & Security, Appliances, Furniture, etc.) runs its own custom "CareerWEB" ATS at `careeropportunities.godrejenterprises.com`, linked from `godrejenterprises.com/about-us/careers/openings`. Confirmed live: only 13 open India postings, all sales/manufacturing/procurement/ops roles (e.g. "Manager - Sales and Marketing", "Sr. Executive Busduct Sales & Marketing") — zero SDE-relevant, not worth building a fetcher against.
- **Godrej Industries Group** (GCPL/Godrej Consumer Products, Godrej Properties, Godrej Capital, Godrej Agrovet/Chemicals, Godrej Ventures — everything else, under one shared tenant) runs on **Phenom People CareerConnect** at `careers.godrejindustries.com` — the same platform family as Morningstar/Evernorth/GE Aerospace/GE HealthCare/Cisco/Disney already in this repo. Confirmed live via its own sitemap scan to have genuine, current SDE-track India postings: Full Stack Developer, "Java + Angular/React Fullstack Developer", Senior DevOps Engineer, DevOps/MLOps Engineer (a real posting naming CI/CD for "GenAI/LLM apps", LLMOps, and RAG-app deployment patterns), Senior Executive - Data Engineer, Assistant Manager - Data Scientist, and several "SDE I/II/III/IV" postings. This is the entity onboarded here.

**ATS mechanics**: same shape as Morningstar's tenant — the real `/widgets` search API is session-gated (`PLAY_SESSION` JWT + CSRF cookie) and returns `{"status":"failure"}` to plain `requests`; the server-rendered `/search-results` page's own initial payload is an empty `"jobs":[]` shell (client-side JS fetches the real list after page load, same "landing page, not a real search response" shape flagged for Evernorth). Worked around identically to Morningstar: walk the tenant's own `sitemap.xml` (`/in/en/sitemap.xml` and the business-unit-scoped `/in/en/godrejindustriesgroup/sitemap.xml` are byte-identical, 404 `/job/{id}/{slug}` URLs, no pagination needed) and extract each job page's embedded JSON-LD `JobPosting` block — no auth, no JS execution. Cached once per process (keywords are ignored server-side).

**Two load-bearing bugs found and fixed during live verification** (both would have crashed the pipeline partway through a real sitemap scan, not just cost precision):
1. **`jobLocation` can be a bare list, not a single object.** Most postings have a single `jobLocation: {...}` object, but multi-site postings on this tenant emit `jobLocation` as a `[{...}, {...}]` list instead — a bare `.get("address")` on that raises `AttributeError: 'list' object has no attribute 'get'`, caught live mid-scan (matcher.py's own defensive `except Exception` swallowed it into a `[warn] search fetch failed` line rather than crashing the run, but it silently truncated the cache to whatever had been scanned before the first list-shaped posting). Fixed by normalizing `jobLocation` to a list either way and treating the posting as India-based if any of its locations is.
2. **One JSON-LD `<script>` block on some pages is a bare list, not an object** (a `BreadcrumbList`'s `itemListElement` at the top level, not nested under a `@graph` key) — a bare `d.get("@type")` on that also raises `AttributeError`. Fixed with an `isinstance(d, dict)` guard before the `.get` call.

**Data-quality gotcha, not a bug, defended against anyway**: this tenant inconsistently authors `addressLocality`/`addressRegion` in Devanagari (Hindi) script for some postings and plain English for others — confirmed live on two contemporaneous postings at the identical URL pattern (one "Full Stack Developer" showed `addressLocality: "मुंबई"`, a same-day "Java + Angular/React Fullstack Developer" showed `addressLocality: "Mumbai"`). `addressCountry` was reliably English "India" on every sampled posting, so India detection is unaffected, but a Hindi-authored *excluded* city (e.g. "पुणे") would silently bypass `exclude_locations`'s plain-English substring match. Added a small Devanagari→English transliteration table in the fetcher covering exactly the `default_exclude_locations` city/state names — a genuinely new mitigation pattern for this repo, same "flag and defend, don't just flag" discipline as Eurofins' TN-region-code fix and Persistent's city-regex extraction.

**Live verification (post-fix)**: sitemap scan found 404 total job URLs, 399 parsed as genuine India postings (5 dropped as non-India or malformed). Full `find_matching_jobs` run against the real `godrej_search` config: 399 fetched, 1 genuine match — "Java + Angular/React Fullstack Developer" (Mumbai), tagged `[AI / ML / Python]` on a description literally naming "Knowledge of JAVA, Python, SQL, Heroku" and "Support the integration of AI services (e.g., **Google Generative AI**, text-to-speech...)" — a real primary-skill hit, not a near-miss. Zero excluded-city leakage among the 399 India postings or the 1 match. Near-miss log showed correct `[title family]`/`[exclude]`/`[broad-only]`/`[skill]` reasons throughout (e.g. several genuine "SDE I/II/III/IV" postings filtered as `[skill]` with `skills_found=none` — legitimate, their JDs don't currently name any qualifying .NET/C#/AI/ML term).

**Registry/config changes:**
- `_PIPELINE_DATA`: added `("godrej", "Godrej Industries Group", False)`
- `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS`: added `godrej` (tenant's real search API is session-gated; the fetcher ignores the caller's keyword entirely and serves from a cache-once sitemap scan)
- `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS`: added `godrej` (full description is embedded in the same JSON-LD block already fetched during the sitemap scan, no separate detail call in the normal path — same idiom as `morningstar`)
- `config.yaml`: added `godrej_search` (`max_listings: 400`, `inter_page_delay: 0.15`)
- `seen_jobs_godrej.json` created as `[]`
- `src/godrej_fetcher.py` created

`python src/run_all.py --validate --companies godrej` passes. Godrej Enterprises Group (the industrial arm) was investigated and is not onboarded — see above; a future re-check would only be worthwhile if its 13-posting board grows a real SDE-track presence.

## Bajaj Finance added (2026-09-13)

Investigated which Bajaj entity has the most genuine tech/engineering hiring, per the standing hypothesis that Bajaj Finserv/Finance are more likely than Bajaj Auto to have real software roles. Bajaj Auto was not independently deep-dived once Bajaj Finance's own large, live, working SDE-relevant pipeline was confirmed (a traditional two/three-wheeler manufacturer with no comparable software-hiring surface found during initial checks) — this repo's own precedent (Boeing/PepsiCo/Walmart/etc.) is to verify from scratch rather than assume, but once one entity in a group is confirmed both feasible and genuinely SDE-relevant, that's the one worth shipping first.

Bajaj Finserv's own branded careers page (`bajajfinserv.in/careers-finance`) links to a Zoho Recruit tenant (`bajajfinserv.zohorecruit.in/jobs/Careers`, `company_name: "Bajaj Finance"`) — the same self-hosted ATS product as ITC/Zoho Corp/Yubi/Akasa Air elsewhere in this repo. Parsing its own embedded job-list JSON (same `<input id="jobs" value="[...]">` mechanism as `itc_fetcher.py`) found **exactly one posting, dated 2023** — confirmed live, not a request-shape bug (the tenant, session, and embedded-JSON mechanism all worked correctly; there is simply almost nothing posted there). That same careers page also links to `https://bflcareers.peoplestrong.com/home` (a 404, though the SPA root at `bflcareers.peoplestrong.com/` is live) — this turned out to be Bajaj Finance's **real, actively used** hiring pipeline: **PeopleStrong "Candidate Portal"**, the same Indian HR-tech vendor already onboarded for Larsen & Toubro (`larsentoubro_fetcher.py`) and Aditya Birla Group (`adityabirla_fetcher.py`), a different tenant here. This is the pipeline onboarded.

**ATS mechanics**: identical REST API shape to L&T's tenant on the same vendor, confirmed live and reproducible via plain `requests` with no auth/session/cookies — `POST /api/cp/rest/altone/cp/jobs/v1?offset=<n>&limit=<n>&searchString=<kw>` with the same fixed mostly-null JSON body the real page sends. `searchString` genuinely narrows server-side (literal case-insensitive title substring, same non-tokenized behavior as L&T's tenant): unfiltered = 9,220 total postings; `"software engineer"` = 74; `"senior software engineer"` = 64; `"data engineer"` = 18; `"AI engineer"` = 2; `"developer"` = 7; `"engineer"` alone = 105; `"java"`/`"python"`/`".NET developer"`/`"C# developer"`/`"dot net"`/`"angular"`/`"machine learning engineer"`/`"generative ai engineer"` = 0 right now — genuinely zero current title matches for those terms, not a broken filter (confirmed alive and narrowing via the nonzero counts above). Pagination (`offset`/`limit`) behaves safely: an out-of-range offset against a real pool returns an empty list rather than wrapping to page 1 (same safe-termination family as L&T's tenant), and `limit` silently caps at 99 rows regardless of a larger requested value. Detail fetch uses the identical per-job PeopleStrong API as L&T/Aditya Birla (`GET /api/cp/rest/altone/cp/job/<code>/v2?part=...&isReqId=false`, including the vendor's own "descriprion" typo in the param name, reproduced verbatim because the API requires it exactly) — confirmed live to work with no auth.

**Live verification**: of the 74 current `"software engineer"`-titled postings, 67 are in Pune (this repo's own `exclude_locations` entry) and 7 are in Bengaluru — all 7 sampled live and read as genuine current openings ("Senior Software Engineer" — Salesforce B2B/LSF platform work, ServiceNow development, a "Bajaj Finserv Web" role covering the company's 500M-traffic/30k-webpage consumer platform). Ran the real fetcher through `find_matching_jobs` against the actual `bajaj_search` config: 76 unique jobs fetched across the 10 default keywords, 0 matches — a legitimate result, not a bug (near-miss log shows correct `[broad-only]` reasons, e.g. several Bengaluru "Senior Software Engineer" postings hitting only Azure/Angular with no qualifying primary-skill term, and one genuine `[skill]`/`skills_found=none` case), consistent with the Sprinklr/Fidelity International/D. E. Shaw precedent of "real ATS, real India postings, zero current qualifying matches is a true reflection of the snapshot." All location strings correctly carried "India" as the leading hierarchy segment with no leakage past `exclude_locations`.

**Registry/config changes:**
- `_PIPELINE_DATA`: added `("bajaj", "Bajaj Finance", False)`
- `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS`: **not** added — `searchString` genuinely filters server-side on this tenant (confirmed above), same treatment as `larsentoubro`/`adityabirla`/`mahindra` on the same or a sibling vendor
- `config.yaml`: added `bajaj_search` (`max_listings: 200`, `inter_page_delay: 0.2`)
- `seen_jobs_bajaj.json` created as `[]`
- `src/bajaj_fetcher.py` created

`python src/run_all.py --validate --companies bajaj` passes. Bajaj Finserv's own Zoho Recruit board and Bajaj Auto were investigated and are not onboarded separately — see above; the working PeopleStrong pipeline already covers Bajaj Finance's real hiring surface.

## Genuine skip: RBI (Reserve Bank of India) (2026-09-13)

Investigated live rather than assumed, per the pattern already established by the SBI/PNB/BoB/Canara PSU-bank skips above — RBI runs its own Grade B Officer exam, so it was worth checking specifically for a distinct IT/software/Data-Scientist recruitment track before concluding anything.

RBI's actual careers/recruitment portal is `opportunities.rbi.org.in` (the marketing page at `rbi.org.in` under "Opportunities@RBI" is purely informational and links out to this portal for every real listing). Its "Vacancies" list (`opportunities.rbi.org.in/scripts/vacancies.aspx`) was fetched live and is a **chronological archive of dated notification links**, not a job board: every entry currently listed (Sep 2026 check) is either a Bank's Medical Consultant (BMC) contract engagement/extension at a specific regional office, a Lateral Recruitment of Site Engineers drive, or a "Young Professionals" engagement — each a standalone notification with a fixed application window (e.g. one BMC notice's deadline was "extended up to 03:00 PM on September 24, 2026"), referencing back to an original dated notification for full eligibility/selection details. There is no per-job stable ID, location-per-posting, or apply-anytime flow — clicking an entry opens a notification detail page (`bs_viewcontent.aspx?Id=...`), not a job posting.

RBI's flagship recruitment for the officer cadre, **Grade B (General/DEPR/DSIM streams)** — DSIM (Dept. of Statistics and Information Management) being the closest RBI gets to a quantitative/tech-adjacent specialist stream, alongside DEPR (economic research) — is exam-based: Phase 1 (Prelims) → Phase 2 (Mains) → Interview, released as a single dated notification PDF for ~60 vacancies per cycle, run directly by RBI (not routed through IBPS, unlike SBI/PNB/Canara, but structurally identical in effect: fixed application window, written exam, interview, results). No distinct "Software Developer"/"IT Officer" rolling job feed exists separate from this generalist exam cycle — any IT-flavored hiring at RBI (e.g. IT Officer/specialist recruitment when it runs) is likewise a periodic Grade A/Grade B specialist-post notification advertised the same way as the Medical Consultant and Site Engineer drives seen on the live Vacancies page, not an independently browsable posting.

This matches the reusable pattern documented above for Indian PSU banks and PSU/`.gov.in` bodies generally: a dated-notification archive with fixed-window, exam/interview-based drives, not a stable per-job feed `fetch_jobs()` can dedupe against. Genuine skip — no fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_*.json` was created. Re-check only if RBI ever exposes a rolling, individually-addressable IT/tech job board distinct from its Grade A/B exam notifications.

## Genuine skips: Defense/Space PSUs — ISRO, DRDO, HAL, BEL, BHEL (2026-09-13)

Investigated all five independently and honestly rather than assuming the whole category is infeasible (same discipline as the SBI/PNB/BoB/Canara, ONGC/NTPC/CIL/SAIL/IOC, and RBI skips above, run by other agents in this same parallel batch — see those sections for the general PSU pattern this confirms again). Every one of the five careers/recruitment pages was actually fetched live, not guessed from prior knowledge of how Indian government hiring works.

**Reusable pattern, confirmed a third time on a different PSU sector (flagging prominently for any other parallel PSU-focused agent):** all five are Recruitment & Assessment Centre (DRDO's RAC) or CHRC/HR-cell-run **periodic, dated-notification** organizations — GATE score, an in-house written/computer-based test, or walk-in interviews for JRF/RA/Apprentice/Trainee/Specialist-Officer-equivalent posts — not a standing job-req board. Two nuances worth calling out that weren't hit by the banking/energy-PSU batches, both discovered by going one level deeper than "does the page have an API":

1. **A modern-looking SPA frontend on a PSU site can still be backed by a real, discoverable JSON API — and it can still not be fetchable job data.** HAL's `hal-india.co.in` is an Angular SPA (not a plain PDF-notice page like the other four); grepping its shipped `main.*.js` bundle for `baseUrl`/`apiUrl` string literals near `career`/`job` tokens turned up a genuine WordPress REST backend at `https://hal-india.co.in/backend/wp-json/hal/v1/career` (`POST` with an empty `FormData` body — confirmed live, returns real structured JSON: `id`, `division_id`, `division`, `title`, `floated_date`, `activeupto`, and a detail endpoint `/career_detail` with `description` + linked PDF `file[]` array). This is worth remembering as a technique for any future `.gov.in`/PSU SPA: don't stop at "the page needs JS to render" — pull the bundle and grep for `baseUrl`/`apiUrl`/`postApi(` call sites next to domain-relevant keywords before concluding there's no API. In HAL's case the API is real but still the wrong shape for this repo: there is **no `location` field anywhere in the schema** (every record's "division" is an internal HAL business unit like "CHRC-Talent Acquisition Vertical" or "Accessories Division, Lucknow", not a city), `job_url`/`job_url_info` are present in the schema but empty on every entry checked, and the content itself is dominated by recruitment-*process* updates (written-test result lists, interview shortlists, call-letter/admit-card announcements) for periodic GATE-based Engineer/Design/Management Trainee and Deputy-Manager-grade drives — not standing open job postings with a title matchable against `title_family` and a JD body to run skill checks against. A structured API existing is necessary but not sufficient; the data behind it still has to look like a job board.
2. **A PSU-domain 403 can be a simple Referer/header check, not a hard WAF gate — retry with browser-like headers before writing a domain off.** `careers.bhel.in/index.jsp` returns HTTP 403 to a bare `curl`/`requests` GET with no `Referer`, but returns a normal HTTP 200 page the moment a `Referer: https://www.bhel.com/recruitment` header (plus a standard browser `User-Agent`/`Accept`) is added — a shallow anti-hotlinking check, not Akamai/Cloudflare-style bot detection. Worth trying at any other PSU/`.gov.in` domain that 403s on the first plain request before assuming it's genuinely inaccessible.

### ISRO (Indian Space Research Organisation)

`isro.gov.in/Careers.html` is a static bulletin-board page linking to per-center notice pages (`ICRB_Recruitment11.html`, `LPSCRecruitment13.html`, `NRSCRecruitment2026_1.html`, `SACRecruitment21.html`, `URSCRecruitment12.html`, and similar for HSFC/ISTRAC/NESAC/NSIL/PRL/SDSC — one recruiting center's notice per HTML page), plus a "centre-wise Current Openings" and an "all Current Openings" consolidated view — both plain HTML, not an API or structured feed. No ATS vendor markers anywhere, no hidden JSON/XHR endpoint behind either "Current Openings" view. Each notice is a periodic recruitment-drive advertisement (e.g. "ISRO:ICRB:01(A-JPA):2026") pointing to a PDF advertisement and application form — Scientist/Engineer recruitment at ISRO is GATE-score-based (the "ICRB" — ISRO Centralised Recruitment Board — process), a single annual/periodic cycle covering all engineering disciplines together, not a rolling per-req software-engineer job feed. Genuine skip — no fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_isro.json` created.

### DRDO (Defence Research and Development Organisation)

`drdo.gov.in/drdo/en/offerings/vacancies` is the same bulletin-board shape: a chronological list of dated vacancy notices, each its own page (e.g. `rci-hyderabad-invites-application-eligible-candidates-position-ra-jrf`, `lrde-bengaluru-invites-eligible-candidates-walk-interview-post-junior-research`), and every notice page links out to a PDF "Advertisement and Application Form" (confirmed live, e.g. `/drdo/sites/default/files/vacancy/advtRCI17082026.pdf`) rather than exposing any structured job data on the page itself. Current notices are overwhelmingly Junior Research Fellow (JRF)/Research Associate (RA) engagements and walk-in-interview positions at individual DRDO labs (RCI Hyderabad, LRDE Bengaluru, NPOL Kochi, CVRDE Chennai, DEBEL Bengaluru, etc.) — academic/research-fellowship-style short-term engagements advertised per-lab, not standing software-engineer job requisitions. DRDO's permanent Scientist cadre is hired through its own centralized RAC (Recruitment & Assessment Centre) process — GATE-score-based, same shape as ISRO's ICRB — which was also confirmed via a linked notice ("Recruitment Assessment Centre (RAC), DRDO invites application for appointment...") to be a periodic, single-cycle drive, not a rolling job list. No ATS, no API — every one of the ~15 currently-listed notices resolves to a bare content page plus a linked PDF. Genuine skip — no fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_drdo.json` created.

### HAL (Hindustan Aeronautics Limited)

See the "reusable pattern" nuance #1 above for the full technical finding — HAL is the one company of these five with a genuine, live, discoverable JSON API (`hal-india.co.in/backend/wp-json/hal/v1/career`, WordPress REST behind an Angular SPA frontend), not just static PDF-linked HTML. It was still ruled infeasible: the live feed (8 current entries at check time) is a mix of interview-shortlist announcements ("CANDIDATES SHORTLISTED FOR INTERVIEW FOR THE POST OF DEPUTY MANAGER FINANCE GRADE-III"), written-test qualified-candidate lists, admit-card/call-letter downloads for a "SELECTION OF DESIGN TRAINEES/MANAGEMENT TRAINEES - 2026" GATE-based drive, and fixed-term Visiting Consultant (doctor) engagements — no field distinguishes an open, individually-applicable software-engineer requisition from these process-update announcements, there is no location field to run Layer 1 against, and no title in the feed is shaped like a job posting `title_family` could match (they're notice headlines, e.g. "List of Provisionally qualified candidates in Written Test different Trades/Disciplines for Engagement of Tenure basis Personnel..."). HAL's own engineer hiring (Design Trainee/Management Trainee/Deputy Manager grades) is GATE-score or written-test based, run by its "CHRC-Talent Acquisition Vertical," and this API is the results/status feed for those periodic drives, not a job board. Genuine skip — no fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_hal.json` created, though the API shape (`POST /backend/wp-json/hal/v1/career` with empty `FormData`, `POST /backend/wp-json/hal/v1/career_detail` with `id` for detail) is documented here in case HAL ever adds a location field and separates open reqs from process-update notices.

### BEL (Bharat Electronics Limited)

`bel-india.in/job-notifications/` is a WordPress page (confirmed via the page's `gmpg.org/xfn/11` profile link, a standard WordPress signature) listing individual recruitment drives as `<h2>` headings (e.g. "Recruitment of Senior Engineer - Fixed Term Engineer - Export Manufacturing - SBU", "Recruitment of Deputy Engineer/ E-II (FTE) posts for NS(S and CS) SBU", "WALK-IN SELECTION (WRITTEN TEST) FOR THE ENGAGEMENT OF SHORT TERM APPRENTICESHIP TRAINEE (ITI)"), each followed by nothing but a "click here to view detailed advertisement" PDF link (`bel-india.in/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/...pdf`). No `wp-json` career/job custom-post-type route was found (checked `wp/v2/career`, `wp/v2/vacancy`, `wp/v2/job` and plural variants — all `rest_no_route`), confirming this page really is just a styled list of PDF links, not a WordPress custom-post-type feed with an accessible REST route the way HAL's Angular app has. Even the Senior Engineer/Deputy Engineer drives that do name real engineering-track posts are individual fixed-term/FTE recruitment advertisements with a written-test-and-interview selection process and a fixed application window, not standing job requisitions. Genuine skip — no fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_bel.json` created.

### BHEL (Bharat Heavy Electricals Limited)

`careers.bhel.in/index.jsp` (see reusable-pattern nuance #2 above for the 403/Referer finding) is a legacy JSP portal listing "Current Openings" as a set of periodic-drive links: "Recruitment of Engineer Trainees - GATE 2017/2018" (GATE-score-based Engineer Trainee intake, the closest BHEL gets to a recurring engineer pipeline), "Artisan Recruitment – 2025" (skilled-trades, Computer Based Exam plus Skill Test, with its own candidate-login portal for admit cards/score cards — not software engineering), "Recruitment of Experienced Engineering Professionals On Lateral Basis For Electronics Division (EDN), Bengaluru" (a lateral-hire drive, but delivered as a single "Apply Online"/"Detailed Advertisement" PDF pairing with a fixed window, not a rolling req), and "Recruitment of Project Directors at AGM/GM Level" (senior leadership, one-off). No structured job list or API was found behind any of these — every "Current Opening" is a link to either a PDF advertisement or the site's own exam/admit-card candidate-login system, never a per-job posting with a location and stable ID. BHEL's Electronics Division Bengaluru lateral-hire drive is the single closest thing to a real SDE-adjacent opening across all five PSUs checked in this batch, but it is exam/interview-drive-based with a fixed application window like every other notice on the page, not an "apply anytime" requisition — the same disposition as Canara Bank's Specialist Cadre Officer drive in the banking-PSU skip above. Genuine skip — no fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_bhel.json` created.

No fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_*.json` was created for any of the five. If any of these organizations migrates to a real rolling ATS with individually addressable, location-tagged postings (unlikely given how centralized defense/space-PSU hiring is structured today, but not impossible), re-check using the "dated PDF notice list" / "process-update feed with no location field" signals documented above as the first thing to look past.

## Genuine skips: IT/Software PSUs — NIC, C-DAC, IRCTC/CRIS (2026-09-13)

Investigated all three independently and honestly, per an explicit brief noting these are *actual software/IT organizations* (unlike the generic PSUs skipped above) and might therefore have a more genuine SDE hiring track. They don't — but one of the three (C-DAC) came close enough to be worth documenting in detail, since it's a real Angular SPA with a real backend API, not just a PDF-notice board like the rest of this PSU wave.

**NIC (National Informatics Centre)** — the branded `nic.in` domain and its dedicated `recruitment.nic.in` subdomain are both currently serving a static "WEB HOSTING SERVICES OF NIC ... This Website is under Maintenance" placeholder (confirmed live via both `curl` and WebFetch, from two different network origins) — no page content of any kind is reachable at the top level right now. The actual recruitment mechanism was found via web search rather than the (down) portal itself: NIC's current live drive is "Recruitment for the posts of Scientist-'B'/Scientist-'C'/Scientist-'D' and Scientific/Technical Assistant-'A'" (Ref. No. NIC/SCB/2026/1 and related, hosted as PDFs directly under `recruitment.nic.in/DetailedSciB.pdf` etc. even while the portal's own index page 404s/is down), on a **Direct Recruitment basis but selected purely by GATE score** (2024/2025/2026 valid scores) shortlisted into interviews at a ratio of 1:5 — registration happens on a one-off exam-registration sub-portal (`examinationservices.nic.in/recsys2026/...`), not a job board. This is the exact same shape as the GATE-based Graduate Engineer Trainee drives already skipped for ONGC/NTPC/Coal India/SAIL/IOC above: one dated advertisement number covering a large batch of vacancies across disciplines, a fixed application window, an exam-score-based shortlist, and no per-job ID/location/stable posting to fetch. Genuine skip.

**IRCTC (Indian Railway Catering and Tourism Corporation)** — official recruitment page is `irctc.com/recruitment.php` → "New Openings" (`irctc.com/new-openings.php`), fetched live and inspected in full (not sampled). It is a flat table of dated PDF/DOCX download links, not a job-listing API: current entries are Tourism Monitor and Hospitality Monitor notifications (multiple railway zones — North/South/West/East/South-Central), a Rajbhasha (Official Language) Consultant requirement, an IT-infrastructure consultant advertisement, and apprentice-engagement notices — **zero software-engineer/developer-titled postings appear anywhere in the current or historical list**, and there is no ATS vendor, hidden JSON/XHR endpoint, or searchable listing behind the page (view-source confirms plain server-rendered HTML with `<a href="assets/images/....pdf">` download links only). IRCTC's own tech/software work is in fact built and run by its sister PSU, CRIS (see below) — IRCTC itself does not appear to run an independent software-engineering hiring track at all right now. Genuine skip.

**CRIS (Centre for Railway Information Systems)** — this is the entity that actually does IRCTC/Indian Railways' software engineering (an autonomous society under the Ministry of Railways, the IT backbone for railway reservation/freight/signalling systems), so it was checked as the real candidate behind the IRCTC lead, not a redundant afterthought. `cris.org.in`'s Career page (`loadpage?page=indexcareer`) was fetched live: it is a tabbed archive of PDF recruitment notifications — "Software Professionals Recruitment" (the closest-named track) currently shows GATE-2023-based ASE (Assistant Software Engineer) select-panel PDFs from 2019–2020 (stale, not a current cycle) — plus separate tabs for Gazetted/Non-Gazetted Deputation roles (filled by transferring existing Indian Railways officers, not open hiring), Project Assistants (retired-professional re-engagements), Project Officers, and Consultants. CRIS's only genuine "software engineer" hiring track (ASE, GATE-score-based) is run as an infrequent, dated recruitment drive with its own selection-panel PDF cycle (the most recent panel found was published December 2020 — no live 2026 ASE cycle visible on the page at all), not a rolling job board; every other tab is either deputation-only (not open external hiring) or short-term contractual engagement. Genuine skip.

**Reusable pattern confirmed, extending the note above:** even where the organization's own mission is literally "build government software" (CRIS) or "run India's e-governance IT" (NIC), that doesn't change the hiring *mechanism* — both still route their core technical cadre through a periodic, GATE-score-gated Direct Recruitment drive advertised as a dated PDF, identical in shape to the GATE-based Graduate Engineer Trainee cycles at the energy/industrial PSUs. **C-DAC is the one partial exception worth flagging for other agents:** unlike every other organization in this PSU wave, C-DAC's `careers.cdac.in` is a genuine standalone Angular single-page application with a real backend REST-ish API on the same origin (verified live — distinct `/advt-1`, `/job/{id}`, `/post_details/{id}`, `/centres`, `/disciplines`, etc. routes that are NOT SPA-fallback 200s; `/centres` alone correctly 401s with `{"error":"No token provided."}` for an authenticated candidate-facing route). However: (1) every data-bearing GET/POST response (confirmed on `/advt-1`) is client-side AES-encrypted with CryptoJS using a passphrase-derived key hardcoded in the SPA's own bundle (`Zm.key`) — the same reverse-engineering shape as the ICICI Bank AES-encrypted API already in this repo (Wave 15), a real engineering path forward if the recruitment model itself justified it; and (2) it doesn't — C-DAC's own hiring under this portal is explicitly run as periodic "JIT (Just-In-Time) Recruitment Cycles" for fixed-term contractual Project Engineer/Project Manager posts (the live cycle found via web search, "JIT June 2026," opened 1 June and closed 20 June 2026 — already closed as of this check), i.e. the same fixed-window batch-drive model as every other org in this wave, just implemented as a modern SPA+API instead of a PDF table. A future recheck should decrypt `/advt-1` (AES key `ufc4684325?wfd45vf^asd23fdds&*sd(kvggv4654vig`, OpenSSL-compatible CryptoJS passphrase scheme, `pycryptodome` already a repo dependency from the ICICI integration) to confirm whether a JIT cycle is ever left continuously open across multiple months rather than closing after ~3 weeks — if so, this would be the first Indian PSU in the entire PSU wave worth building a fetcher against.

**Reusable pattern for other `.gov.in`/PSU-focused agents running in parallel:** don't assume "no ATS" from the branded marketing domain being down or gated — NIC's own web-hosting infrastructure being in maintenance did not stop its GATE-recruitment PDFs from being independently reachable and discoverable via search. And don't stop investigating at "there's a real SPA/API" (C-DAC) — a modern frontend can still wrap the identical periodic-exam-drive hiring model as a PDF-notice board; check the *underlying recruitment cadence* (continuously rolling vs. fixed-window batch cycle), not just the technical sophistication of the portal serving it.

No fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_*.json` was created for any of the three. Re-check NIC/CRIS if either ever exposes a rolling non-exam-based tech job board; re-check C-DAC specifically if a future JIT cycle is observed staying open longer than a few weeks, or if `careers.cdac.in`'s `/advt-1` payload (decryptable with the key documented above) shows individually-dated, always-refreshing postings rather than one batch drive at a time.

## Genuine skip: Tata Motors (2026-09-13)

Investigated live rather than assumed, despite Tata Motors being one of the largest Tata Group companies and this repo already tracking TCS. The homepage's own careers link resolves to `careers.tatamotors.com/search/`, a **SAP SuccessFactors J2W classic** portal (`<tr class="data-row">` server-rendered rows, `j2w`/`successfactors` markers in the page source) — same ATS family as Mastek/Nomura/Capgemini/Dover/YASH already in this repo, `?startrow=N` pagination (25/page).

The `q=` keyword param is ignored server-side on this tenant (confirmed: `q=software`, `q=developer`, and `q=IT` each return different-looking but similarly-sized result sets built from the same underlying pool, not narrowed matches — the classic "keyword is a no-op" shape already documented for Nomura/YASH). Paged the entire unfiltered board (`sortColumn=referencedate&sortDirection=desc`, walked `startrow` until a page repeated zero new IDs): **144 total open positions company-wide**, and every single title is a manufacturing/automotive-engineering, quality, plant-production, purchase, sales, or logistics role (`Senior Manager (L5) - Engineer Crash Simulation, Crash & Safety`, `SMT Wiring harness, circuit and simulation_EV`, `Module Lead -Engine mounting`, `Senior Manager - Powertrain Production (Machine Shop)`, etc.) — **zero titles anywhere in the full pool match a software-engineering shape** (no "Software Engineer," "Developer," "Programmer," ".NET," "Full Stack," or similar). The closest hit, "SM Connected Car Cloud Architecture," is still a Senior Manager-level architecture role that `matching.exclude_terms` would drop as a manager title regardless. Location breakdown of the full pool is also skewed toward the excluded Pune facility (97 of 144 postings across "Pune, IN"/"Pune, MH, IN"/"Pune, India" variants).

This is a car manufacturer's official hiring portal doing exactly what it says — hiring automotive/manufacturing engineers, not software engineers — not a bot-protection or access problem. No fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_tatamotors.json` was created. Worth a from-scratch recheck only if Tata Motors opens a distinct software/digital-engineering hiring track (e.g. a connected-vehicle or EV-software GCC) on this same portal or a separate one.

No fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_tatasteel.json` was created.
