## Amadeus added (2026-09-13)

User-prompted alongside Travelport and Vrbo, since this repo already tracks Sabre — a competing GDS/travel-tech infrastructure vendor. Investigated live rather than assumed: `amadeus.com/en/careers/overview`'s "View all jobs" button links to `amadeus.wd502.myworkdayjobs.com/jobs` — Workday, tenant `amadeus`, site id `jobs` (confirmed by reading the page's own `window.workday = {tenant: "amadeus", siteId: "jobs", ...}` bootstrap script rather than guessing a conventional branded site name — the obvious guesses `AmadeusJobs`/`AmadeusCareers`/`ExternalCareers`/`Careers` all 404 against this tenant, same "URL match isn't full verification" lesson as Novartis's underscored site code).

`amadeus_fetcher.py` follows the exact same shape as `sabre_fetcher.py`: `POST /wday/cxs/amadeus/jobs/jobs` with the standard cross-tenant India WID (`c4f78be1a8f14da0ab49ce1162348a5e`, same GUID Sabre/Barclays/Citi use) applied via `appliedFacets.locationCountry`, and `GET /wday/cxs/amadeus/jobs{externalPath}` for the JSON detail API (`jobPostingInfo.jobDescription`/`startDate`). Amadeus has real Bengaluru (~4,000 staff) and Pune (~140 staff) engineering centres per the company's own site. Same "N Locations" collapsing behavior as Maersk/Sabre — 2 of the 7 India-faceted results show `locationsText` like "2 Locations" instead of a city name; safe to relabel as India since the country facet was already applied server-side, same fallback code as `sabre_fetcher.py`.

Live validation (`--validate --companies amadeus`, and a direct `run_company.run_company_pipeline` call against a throwaway seen-state path with a no-op notifier under a Python 3.12 venv — this repo's dataclass `slots=True` usage requires 3.10+, the sandbox's default `python3` was 3.9.6 — `seen_jobs_amadeus.json` was never touched): 7 India jobs fetched (Bangalore x5, Pune x1, plus one "2 Locations" resolved to India), all genuinely Bengaluru/Pune. One real `.NET` match: "System Architect(SAFe .NET) - Hospitality - OnProperty" (R34942, Bangalore) — explicitly names .NET in the title and JD. The one genuine Pune posting ("Manager Data Engineer") is correctly dropped by `default_exclude_locations`'s standing Pune exclusion, consistent with the conservative Pune policy applied everywhere else in this repo — not a fetcher bug. No Kochi/Chandigarh/Chennai/Tamil Nadu leakage either. No `require_tech_in_description` added — too little verified volume (7 jobs) to justify it, same call made for Airbnb/Agoda/DoorDash.

## Genuine skip: Travelport (2026-09-13)

Investigated live. `www.travelport.com/careers` is a Next.js site whose actual ATS backend is Oracle HCM Cloud/Fusion Recruiting Cloud (`ejzg.fa.us6.oraclecloud.com`, `CandidateExperience` site `CX_1` — found via a `preload`/`og:image`-style embedded URL in the page's own head tags, same discovery method as the Chubb/Amex/BNY Mellon Oracle HCM CE tenants already in this repo). The public REST API (`hcmRestApi/resources/latest/recruitingCEJobRequisitions`, `expand=requisitionList.workLocation,...`) is fully open to plain unauthenticated HTTP — no bot gating, no Playwright needed, a genuinely feasible ATS integration in isolation.

**Skipped for zero current India volume, not infeasibility.** The tenant's entire global job pool is tiny: `TotalJobsCount: 21` across every siteNumber tried (`CX_1` through `CX_5` all return the identical 21-job pool — the site-number parameter appears to be effectively ignored by this tenant). Pulling the full unfiltered `requisitionList` and inspecting every posting's location directly (not relying on a possibly-broken facet, per the Micron/Verizon/Lowe's lesson) shows the complete distinct-country set is Philippines, United Kingdom, Romania, Egypt, Mexico, Australia, Ireland, and United Arab Emirates — zero India, and the role mix is overwhelmingly customer-support/finance/ITSM-analyst, not software engineering (one "Jr Prompt designer" in Romania is the closest AI-adjacent title in the entire pool). Third-party aggregators (Naukri/Indeed) list a handful of India-tagged "Travelport" postings, but none of them resolve to a live requisition in this tenant's own API — consistent with stale/expired aggregator listings, not a second live channel this fetcher is missing.

No fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_travelport.json` was created — there is nothing live to alert on today even though the ATS itself is fully reachable and well-understood. Worth a from-scratch recheck later if Travelport's global hiring picks back up (the small pool size is consistent with the company's known post-acquisition headcount reductions) — the Oracle HCM CE integration pattern documented here (site `CX_1`, `expand=requisitionList.workLocation,requisitionList.secondaryLocations`) is ready to reuse immediately if India postings appear.

## Genuine skip: Vrbo (2026-09-13)

Investigated per the task's own hint to check `expedia_fetcher.py`'s existing job pool first, since Vrbo is a wholly-owned Expedia Group brand — same investigation shape that correctly identified Cleartrip as folded into Flipkart's TurboHire pool. Confirmed live: Vrbo has no separate careers domain, ATS tenant, or job-search API of its own. `vrbo.com/careers` doesn't resolve to a distinct careers experience (HTTP 429 from Vrbo's own edge, and no separate careers subdomain exists in DNS/search results); every real Vrbo-branded requisition found (via web search of live `careers.expediagroup.com/job/...-vrbo/...` URLs, e.g. "Associate Account Manager - Vrbo" R-107398, "Machine Learning Scientist III - VRBO Pricing" R-105582) uses the exact same `R-NNNNNN` requisition-ID namespace and lives on the exact same `careers.expediagroup.com` WordPress + Appcast Job Search plugin pipeline that `expedia_fetcher.py` already fetches in full on every run — Vrbo postings are simply Expedia Group requisitions whose title/JD happen to name the Vrbo brand, not a separate job pool, org, or endpoint.

Ran `expedia_fetcher.fetch_jobs('', 'India')` live against the existing fetcher: 11 current India postings, none currently Vrbo-branded (Expedia's own India engineering hiring at this snapshot is under the Expedia/generic brand, not Vrbo specifically) — but any future Vrbo-branded India requisition would already be surfaced through the same `filter[country]=India` call `expedia_fetcher.py` makes today, with no code change needed. Building a separate `vrbo_fetcher.py` against this same pool would just re-fetch identical `R-`-numbered jobs under a second company label and risk double-alerting the same posting via two different `seen_jobs_*.json` files, the exact failure mode already documented and avoided for Cleartrip/Flipkart.

No fetcher, registry entry, `config.yaml` section, or `seen_jobs_vrbo.json` was created — Vrbo's own engineering reqs are already reachable (and already covered by matching) through the existing `expedia` company entry. Worth a from-scratch recheck only if Vrbo is ever spun out onto its own distinct careers site/ATS.

## Pharma majors added (2026-09-13): Johnson & Johnson, Roche, Sanofi — 3 more Workday tenants, 2 real ATS shapes never seen before at this scale

User-prompted onboarding of three major pharma companies alongside the existing Novartis/AstraZeneca/Pfizer/GE HealthCare pharma-GCC pipelines. All three investigated live and independently (real POST requests against each tenant's actual CXS API, no guessed URLs beyond the initial tenant/site discovery) rather than assumed from the shared "pharma majors run Workday" prior — that prior turned out correct for all three, but the *shape* of the usable location facet differed company to company, which is the real finding here.

**Johnson & Johnson (`jnj`)** — Workday at `jj.wd5.myworkdayjobs.com`, tenant `jj`, site `JJ` (found via a plain web search, not guessed). **Sanofi (`sanofi`)** — Workday at `sanofi.wd3.myworkdayjobs.com`, tenant `sanofi`, site `SanofiCareers`. **Roche (`roche`)** — Workday at `roche.wd3.myworkdayjobs.com`, tenant `roche`, site `roche-ext`.

**New facet-shape finding: two of three tenants have no country-level location facet at all.** Sanofi's `locationMainGroup` nests the familiar `locationCountry` facet with the standard cross-tenant India WID (`c4f78be1a8f14da0ab49ce1162348a5e`, shared with Novartis/Wells Fargo/Fidelity/Citi/Northern Trust/MUFG/Target/Shell/Pfizer) — the easy case. J&J's and Roche's `locationMainGroup`, however, nest only a flat `locations` facet of individual city/state values with no country grouping whatsoever — the same shape already documented for Genpact ("no usable location facet"), except unlike Genpact, both J&J and Roche DO expose usable per-city WIDs; there's just no single India-country WID to pass. Fix: scanned the full raw facet-value list for both tenants, hand-picked every genuine India city/state entry (17 for J&J: Ahmedabad, Aurangabad, Bangalore, Chandigarh, Chennai, Delhi, Gurgaon, Hyderabad, Jaipur, Kanpur, Kolkata, Ludhiana, Mumbai [2 separate WIDs for "Mumbai, India" and "Mumbai, Maharashtra, India"], Patna, Penjerla, Pune, Raipur; 6 for Roche: Bangalore, Chennai, Hyderabad, a bare "India" facet value, Mumbai, Pune), explicitly excluding the classic false-positive entries a naive "india" substring scan over the raw facet list would also match (J&J: Fort Wayne/Mooresville/Valparaiso/Warsaw "Indiana, US" cities, "Indiana (Any City)", "Indianapolis, Indiana, United States"; Roche: "Indianapolis"), and passed the whole list as `appliedFacets.locations` (not `locationCountry`). Confirmed live that this correctly narrows to an India-only pool with zero leakage (every returned `locationsText` value spot-checked against the full result set for both tenants).

**Pagination wraparound bug confirmed live and load-bearing on exactly one of the three tenants.** Sanofi's tenant replays page 1's first job ID verbatim once `offset` passes the true total (same bug class as UBS/MUFG/Nvidia/Pfizer/Walmart/Novartis) — confirmed directly (offset=80 of a 72-job India pool returned the identical page-1 first ID), so the page1-first-ID memo guard is load-bearing there, not just defensive insurance. J&J's and Roche's tenants were both tested through their real last page (J&J: offset=80/100/120 of 94; Roche: offset=40/60 of 50) and correctly returned an empty array with no wraparound — the same guard is still applied to both as cheap defensive insurance (consistent with every other Workday fetcher in this repo), even though it wasn't observed to be load-bearing there in this specific snapshot.

**Real SDE-relevant India engineering presence varies sharply across the three, despite all three being the same "pharma major, Workday" archetype.** Roche has the strongest current signal: a real Hyderabad "Applied & Agentic AI Systems" team posting "Fullstack Data Engineer," "Fullstack AI Quality Engineer," "AI Solution Architect," and "Regulatory AI & Automation Specialist" roles — live validation found 3 genuine `[AI / ML / Python]` matches (2x "Fullstack Data Engineer - Applied & Agentic AI Systems", 1x "Software Developer Regulatory Affairs and China Clinical Development"). J&J has a real Hyderabad software/platform-engineering GCC ("Forward Deployed Engineer," "RD Soft Eng," "Product Engineer" titles) but 0 current matches in live validation — every candidate title either fails `title_family` (generic "Eng"/"Mgr" abbreviated titles not in the phrase list, same class of gap as the existing Disney "Mgr"/"Dir" abbreviation and DAZN/Novartis title-shape gaps already flagged in this playbook), fails `exclude_terms` ("Manager"/"Technology Manager"), fails on broad-only skills (Azure mentioned with no hard primary-skill term), or fails the 10+ YOE filter — a real, current fact about this snapshot's exact title/description shapes, not a fetcher defect. Sanofi has the weakest current signal: its India Hub pool (72 jobs) is dominated by scientific writing, data/business analytics, and regulatory-affairs roles; `searchText="software engineer"` returns only 1 hit ("Principal Stats Programmer") and `searchText="generative ai"` returns 0 — live validation found 0 matches, again a true reflection of the current posting pool (same disposition already established for Airbnb/DoorDash's low-volume India boards) rather than a bug.

**Pune-exclusion policy applied consistently, not special-cased for these pharma GCCs.** Roche in particular has real, live Pune-based technical roles today (a P&C Business Partner and two Solution Architect/Identity-and-Access-Management postings under "Roche Digital Technology") — confirmed these are silently and correctly dropped by the existing `default_exclude_locations` anchor (which does include `"Pune"`, verified directly against `config.yaml` before writing any fetcher code) via `matcher.is_india_job()`/`exclude_locations`, the same behavior applied to every other company in this repo (e.g. the existing Persistent Systems Pune-exclusion precedent). No custom per-company override was added; `roche_search`/`jnj_search`/`sanofi_search` all use the shared `*default_exclude_locations` anchor like every other company.

`require_tech_in_description` (Layer 4) was **not** enabled for any of the three — consistent with the playbook's "do not add by default" guidance and the relatively low per-company volume (22–30 fetched jobs per company in a `--validate`-scale sample).

Live validation (`--validate --companies jnj,roche,sanofi`, all `ok`; then a direct `matcher.find_matching_jobs` call against each fetcher module with `known_ids=set()` and no notifier/seen-state involvement — `seen_jobs_jnj.json`/`seen_jobs_roche.json`/`seen_jobs_sanofi.json` were never touched):
- **J&J**: 22 unique jobs fetched, 0 matches — near-miss log shows correct `[title family]`/`[exclude]`/`[broad-only]`/`[10+ yoe]` tags on all 22, no Pune/Kochi/Chandigarh/Chennai/Tamil Nadu leakage (only Bangalore/Gurgaon/Hyderabad/PENJERLA/Aurangabad locations appeared in this sample).
- **Roche**: 30 unique jobs fetched, 3 genuine `[AI / ML / Python]` matches (all Hyderabad, India), near-miss log shows correct `[title family]`/`[exclude]`/`[broad-only]`/`[skill]` tags on the rest, no Pune/Kochi/Chandigarh/Chennai/Tamil Nadu leakage in the matched set (raw fetch independently confirmed 3 real live Pune postings exist and were correctly excluded before reaching the near-miss/match output).
- **Sanofi**: 11 unique jobs fetched, 0 matches — near-miss log shows correct `[title family]`/`[exclude]` tags on all 11, no excluded-city leakage.

All three added as full pipelines: `src/jnj_fetcher.py`, `src/roche_fetcher.py`, `src/sanofi_fetcher.py`; registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA` (none needed `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS`/`_SUPPORTS_LOCATION`/`_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS`/`_NEWEST_FIRST`/`_USES_PLAYWRIGHT` membership — plain REST API, per-keyword narrowing genuinely works, descriptions come from a separate detail-API call); `jnj_search`/`roche_search`/`sanofi_search` added to `config.yaml`; `seen_jobs_jnj.json`/`seen_jobs_roche.json`/`seen_jobs_sanofi.json` created empty.

## Emirates, Qatar Airways, Singapore Airlines (2026-09-13): one added, two genuine skips

User-prompted follow-on to the existing airline pipelines (Air India, Akasa Air, IndiGo, United Airlines, Delta, Lufthansa): three major foreign carriers with no India-based flag office, investigated independently for whether any run an India tech/GCC hiring pipeline (as opposed to airport/ground-ops hiring, which this repo has never targeted). All three were fetched live from their real careers backends before any add/skip decision — none were guessed from precedent.

**Emirates — genuine skip.** `emiratesgroupcareers.com`'s own `search.js` bundle reveals a plain, unauthenticated JSON REST endpoint (`GET /api/v1/jobs/`, `"system":"Avature"` in every record) that returns the Emirates Group's **entire live global board in one unfiltered call** — confirmed live that `keywords`/`country`/`page` query params are all silently ignored (91 jobs returned regardless), and the sibling `/api/v1/jobs-unique-props` endpoint independently confirms 91 is the true total, not a page-1 slice. Of those 91: only 2 are India-based ("Aircraft Technician - TRV", Trivandrum; "Customer Sales & Service Consultant", Mumbai — both airport/ground-ops, not engineering), and the 12 "Information Technology"-category postings that exist company-wide are all non-India (Dubai HQ). A web cross-check for an Emirates India tech/GCC hub turned up marketing/recruiting-drive noise (in-person hiring events, aggregator sites showing stale/duplicate counts) but no actual dedicated India IT office. **Current, verifiable fact, not a fetcher limitation**: Emirates has zero India-based IT/engineering postings today. No fetcher, registry entry, config section, or `seen_jobs_emirates.json` was created.

**Singapore Airlines — genuine skip.** `careers.singaporeair.com/sia/` is classic-theme SAP SuccessFactors J2W (same family as Air India/Nomura/Capgemini/SAP Labs/Mastek — real server-rendered `<tr class="data-row">` HTML, no session/CSRF dance). The tenant exposes exactly three job-family categories (Ground Professionals, Pilots, Cabin Crew) — no Engineering/IT category at all in that structure. A separate marketing microsite, `techcareers.singaporeair.com.sg`, links to a distinct SuccessFactors search view (`/sia/search/?...&optionsFacetsDD_department=Information+Technology`) confirming a real 17-job IT department exists. Walked every page of all three job-family categories (69 jobs total) AND the dedicated IT-department facet (17 jobs) with plain HTTP scraping — **every single one of the combined 86 postings' `jobLocation` field reads "SG"**; zero India-tagged postings anywhere on the tenant. SIA's "Tech Careers" branding refers to its Singapore-based tech organisation, not an India GCC — there is currently no India tech hiring surface to build a pipeline against. No fetcher, registry entry, config section, or `seen_jobs_singaporeairlines.json` was created.

**Qatar Airways — added.** `careers.qatarairways.com` is an Avature portal (`avacdn.net` CSP references, `avature.portal.*` meta tags). The React-driven "SearchJobs" wizard UI initially looked like it would need Playwright or a CSRF/session dance (per-page-load-obfuscated internal field tokens, the same complexity class as the SAP SuccessFactors "Unify" theme) — but that assumption was wrong. The rendered page's own "Next Page" link exposes a plain, unauthenticated, fully server-rendered target: `GET /global/SearchJobs/?jobRecordsPerPage=6&jobOffset=N` — confirmed live via a raw `curl`/`requests` call returning byte-identical content to what Playwright renders. `jobRecordsPerPage` is accepted but ignored (server always returns exactly 6/page); no keyword or location query params exist on this endpoint at all, so (like Siemens/MetLife/BNP Paribas) the whole ~189-job global board is walked and cached once per process, with India filtered client-side. Individual `JobDetail` pages are **also** plain server-rendered HTML (no Playwright needed there either) — full JD lives in a `<div id="section1__content">` block. Confirmed real India presence: Qatar Airways runs a genuine, non-trivial IT/corporate-functions development centre in **Ahmedabad, India** (~13 of 188 fetched postings), including live "Software Engineer - Java", "Senior Software Engineer - Angular", and "Data Engineer - Power BI Developer" listings (cross-checked against a live web search before committing to building the fetcher, not assumed).

**Bug avoided, same class as United Airlines/PolicyBazaar:** the JobDetail page's own JSON-LD `datePosted` field is unreliable on this tenant — confirmed live that job 27007 ("Software Engineer - Java") shows `datePosted: 2026-04-08` in its JSON-LD while the search-listing page's own "Posting date:" field for the same job correctly shows `2026-09-13`. Since `matcher.py` unconditionally overwrites `job["posting_date"]` with any non-empty date `fetch_job_description` returns, `qatarairways_fetcher.fetch_job_description` deliberately always returns `""` for the date, preserving the accurate date already captured in `fetch_jobs()`.

Live validation (`--validate --companies qatarairways`: `ok`; then `run_company.run_company_pipeline` against a throwaway temp seen-state path with an injected no-op notifier — `seen_jobs_qatarairways.json` was never touched): 188 unique jobs fetched (of 189 reported — a 1-job overlap from live pagination drift during the walk, not a bug), 13 India (Ahmedabad) postings identified, 0 current matches. Near-miss log confirms every rejection is genuine: 9 non-engineering titles correctly caught by `[title family]`/`[exclude]` (Finance/Tax/Talent Acquisition/Customer Service/Quality roles), "Software Engineer - Java - Ahmedabad, India" correctly dropped `[skill]` (its JD is generic engineering boilerplate — Architect/Design/Documentation/code-review language — with zero mention of Java, .NET, C#, or any AI/ML term despite the title), and "Data Engineer - Power BI Developer - Ahmedabad, India" correctly dropped `[broad-only]` (mentions Python, a broad-only term that never qualifies alone). No Pune/Kochi/Chandigarh/Chennai/Tamil Nadu leakage. `require_tech_in_description` was **not** enabled — consistent with playbook default and the small (13-job) India sample size.

Added: `src/qatarairways_fetcher.py`; registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA` and `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` (keyword/location params don't exist on this endpoint — whole board is cached); `qatarairways_search` added to `config.yaml`; `seen_jobs_qatarairways.json` created empty.

## British Airways / IAG and American Airlines (2026-09-13): one genuine skip, one added via a third-party GCC marketplace

Two more foreign carriers investigated independently, per the standing instruction to check honestly for real India tech/GCC hiring rather than airport/ground-ops-only presence. Both were fetched live from their real backends (careers.ba.com, IAG's own GBS/Tech portals, jobs.aa.com, and third-party leads) before any add/skip decision.

**British Airways / IAG — genuine skip, all three known channels checked.** `careers.ba.com` is TalentBrew (org id `22348`) — same vendor/HTML shape already documented for Boeing/Disney/AstraZeneca (`tbcdn.talentbrew.com` CDN, `search-results__job-*` classes, a `/search-jobs/results` ajax endpoint). BA's **entire public global board is only 29 open jobs today**, confirmed by hitting that ajax endpoint directly (`data-total-results="29"`) — and the tenant's only populated country facet is `United Kingdom` (`data-count="29"`, i.e. every single current posting is UK-based; the country-facet dropdown has no India option at all). BA's own site mentions an India presence only in blog/hub content ("20 Years of CallBA: Building Careers in Customer Care" — a customer-service/BPO centre, not engineering) with no live job listings behind it. Two sibling IAG (International Airlines Group, BA's parent) recruiting properties were also checked, not just assumed dead: **IAG GBS** (`iaggbs.wd103.myworkdayjobs.com`, Workday) has exactly 5 open jobs, all Krakow, Poland, all Finance/HR/Business-Ops — zero India, zero engineering. **IAG Tech** (`careers.smartrecruiters.com/IAGGBS1/iag-tech-careers`) is a dead tenant — the branded URL 302-redirects straight to `jobs.smartrecruiters.com`'s bare root with no company context, the same "dead SmartRecruiters tenant" failure shape already documented for OYO. **Current, verifiable fact, not a fetcher limitation**: neither British Airways nor its IAG parent has any India-based software engineering hiring surface today, across all three of their known recruiting channels. No fetcher, registry entry, config section, or `seen_jobs_britishairways.json` was created.

**American Airlines — added, via a third-party GCC marketplace, not AA's own ATS.** `jobs.aa.com` is SAP SuccessFactors "Job2Web Unify" (`j2w.searchResultsUnify.min.js`, `companyId: 'americairP'` — the same `/services/recruiting/v1/jobs` POST-with-CSRF shape already documented for Standard Chartered/Wipro/HCLTech). That tenant's entire public board was walked in full (99 jobs worldwide, confirmed by paging every result) and has **zero India-tagged postings** — American's own direct ATS is not the India channel. The real one is **Talent500** (a third-party GCC/tech-hiring marketplace, new vendor for this repo), reached via American's own "AA Tech Hub India" marketing microsite (`talent500.com/aatechhubindia`) linking to a dedicated company-scoped board at `talent500.com/jobs/aatechhubindia/` — 100% Hyderabad, India, **65 open jobs** at onboarding time, genuine software engineering demand (Java/Spring Boot/Microservices, Angular/React/.NET/Azure/AWS, Machine Learning, Python/PySpark for a data-engineering manager role).

**Finding the real API took two wrong turns, both worth flagging:** (1) `prod-warmachine.talent500.co/api/v5/jobs/search/` (the `jobSearchV5` endpoint referenced in the same JS bundle) silently ignores `company_slug` for ANY value including the real one, and instead falls back to an unrelated small pool from a different Talent500 client ("ANSR", a GCC-consulting firm) — a "looks like it should work" trap that returns HTTP 200 with plausible-looking but wrong data. (2) The correct endpoint is `v3/jobs/search/` (the `jobSearch` key, not `jobSearchV5`), found by pulling apart the React SPA's actual webpack chunk (`companyJobsPage.*.js`) rather than guessing from the more prominently-named sibling. `search_term` genuinely narrows server-side on `v3` (confirmed: "engineer" → 38 of 65 India jobs; a nonsense token → 0), and `size` accepts values well above the frontend's own default page size of 7 (confirmed `size=100` returns the full 65 in one call) — the fetcher still caches the whole small pool once per process and ignores the caller's keyword/location, same discipline as Lufthansa/Boeing.

**Second real bug found and fixed during live validation:** one job in the search results (`T500-20999`, "Principal Solution Architect - Java Fullstack") carries `status: "open"` in the list response but `is_active: false` — its detail endpoint (`GET /api/jobs/{id}/`, note: unversioned, NOT `/api/v3/jobs/{id}/` which 404s) returns HTTP 204 with an empty body for this ID, which crashed a naive `r.json()` call. Fixed two ways: `_fill_cache` now skips any job with `is_active is False` before caching it as a detail-fetch target, and `fetch_job_description` defensively treats a 204/empty body as an unavailable description (returns `("", "")`, which `matcher.py` correctly drops to the near-miss log) rather than crashing, in case a job goes inactive between cache fill and description fetch. Job detail pages themselves render full HTML descriptions in the `description` JSON field via this same plain, unauthenticated `GET /api/jobs/{id}/` call — the friendly public page (`talent500.com/jobs/aatechhubindia/{slug}/`) only renders content client-side via React (confirmed: a plain `requests.get` on it returns an empty SPA shell), so the fetcher calls the JSON detail API directly instead of needing Playwright, keyed by a module-level `application_url -> job id` map built during the cache fill.

Live validation (`--validate --companies americanairlines`: `ok`; then `matcher.find_matching_jobs` called directly against the fetcher with no notifier/seen-state involved): 64 unique jobs fetched (65 minus the one inactive `is_active: false` job filtered out), all Hyderabad/India, zero non-India location leakage and zero Pune/Kochi/Chandigarh/Chennai/Tamil Nadu leakage. **0 current alerts**, but confirmed as a genuine, pre-existing `title_family` gap and not a fetcher defect: every AA Tech Hub India title uses a "{level} {role}, IT {domain}" order ("Engineer, IT Software", "Sr Engineer, IT Software", "Engineer, IT Machine Learning") that never matches `title_family`'s phrase list (which expects "software engineer"/"machine learning engineer" word order) — same "flag, don't silently patch" class as the Disney/DAZN/PepsiCo/Nutanix title-shape gaps already in this table, a global-config change affecting all 292+ companies and deliberately not made as part of this single-company onboarding. Confirmed by temporarily bypassing only the title-family gate in a throwaway check (not shipped): **14 of the 64 jobs have genuine hard-skill matches** — 13 `.NET / C#` (via Entity Framework/.NET/C#-adjacent terms already present in `matching.skills`/`primary_skills`) and 1 `AI / ML / Python` ("Engineer, IT Machine Learning") — real, hireable engineering roles this pipeline will start surfacing the moment the title-family precision pass (already flagged as overdue elsewhere in this file) is done.

Added: `src/americanairlines_fetcher.py`; registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA` and `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` (cache-once, ignores keyword/location); `americanairlines_search` added to `config.yaml`; `seen_jobs_americanairlines.json` created empty. British Airways/IAG: no files created (genuine skip, three channels checked).

## Pharma/medtech majors (2026-09-13): Merck (MSD), GSK, Abbott, Medtronic — all 4 onboarded, mixed current match volume

Investigated all four as a batch. All four turned out to be genuinely feasible with plain `requests` (no Playwright needed for any of them) and all four have a real India tech/GCC presence, not just field-sales/R&D — but current live match counts range from 4 (MSD) down to 0 (GSK), which is a fact about each company's live posting snapshot today, not a sign any of them was wrongly added (same class of finding as Novartis/Pfizer/Sanofi elsewhere in this file).

**Merck/MSD disambiguation (per the task's own warning, confirmed live):** "Merck" outside the US/Canada usually means Merck KGaA (`careers.merckgroup.com`, a Phenom People + SuccessFactors site, unrelated German company) — not investigated further once the US-based Merck & Co.'s international "MSD" brand (`jobs.msd.com`) confirmed real SDE/AI hiring. MSD's public site is a Phenom People frontend, but the real search/apply backend is Workday (`msd.wd5.myworkdayjobs.com`, tenant `msd`, site `SearchJobs` — the guessable `MSD`/`MSDCareers`/`MSDJobs`/`MSD_Careers` site codes all 404 with errorCode S21, same "site-code needs independent discovery" lesson as Novartis). No flat country facet — 10 hardcoded India office WIDs used in `appliedFacets.locations` (Hyderabad HITEC City Raidurg is the primary GCC tech campus). `searchText` is a genuine full-text match against JD body (Novartis/SAP Labs pattern).

**Abbott:** Workday (`abbott.wd5.myworkdayjobs.com`, tenant `abbott`, site `abbottcareers`) — found via the "Check application status" link on `www.abbott.com/en-us/careers`, a separate `www.jobs.abbott` marketing domain proxies the same tenant. Standard flat capitalised `Location_Country` facet with the usual cross-tenant India WID (168 of a 2000-job global cap). `searchText` is a loose/noisy full-text OR-of-words match (not narrowed on).

**GSK:** Phenom People (`jobs.gsk.com`) — same "`/widgets` search API returns `{"status":"failure"}` to plain HTTP" limitation as Morningstar/Godrej's Phenom tenants. Used the identical Morningstar-style workaround: walk the sitemap index (`jobs.gsk.com/sitemap.xml` → two child sitemaps, ~688 live job URLs total) and read each job page's JSON-LD `JobPosting` block directly (`addressCountry`/`title`/`datePosted`/`description`) — no browser needed. Two specific GSK software-engineering postings ("Apprentice — Software Engineer (AI)", "Tech Innovation Engineer") turned up via web search as evidence GSK does hire for these roles in Bengaluru, but both had already closed by investigation time (their job-ID URLs 404/redirect to the homepage with no JobPosting JSON-LD — same "closed posting, no distinguishing error state" behavior documented for Infosys).

**Medtronic:** Workday (`medtronic.wd1.myworkdayjobs.com`, tenant `medtronic`, site `medtroniccareers`) — `jobs.medtronic.com` redirects straight here with no frontend skin at all, the simplest of the four to identify. No country-level facet on this tenant (only `jobFamilyGroup`/`remoteType`/`timeType`/`workerSubType`/`locationMainGroup`, all city-level) — fetches globally per keyword and filters India client-side via `locationsText`. `searchText` is a genuine server-side full-text filter (confirmed: `""`→1139 total, `"software engineer"`→241, `"python"`→81).

**Real bug found and fixed during live validation — Medtronic client-side India filter matched "Indiana":** `matcher.py`'s own `is_india_job()` does only a plain `"india" in location.lower()` substring check (not word-boundary-aware) — this is exactly the false-positive class already documented for PayPal/FactSet/DAZN ("Indianapolis"/"Indiana"/"US - Indiana"), and the first version of `medtronic_fetcher.py` shipped without that tenant-specific guard, since the tenant has no country facet to lean on. Live validation caught it immediately: a genuine US posting, "Engineering Tech V - CNC Programming 1st Shift" in **Warsaw, Indiana, United States of America** (job `R76802`), passed the fetcher's location check and reached the near-miss log (filtered only by `title_family`, coincidentally — it would NOT have been filtered by location). Fixed by adding a `\bindia\b` word-boundary regex guard in the fetcher itself, the same fix pattern as PayPal/FactSet/DAZN. Re-validated after the fix: zero "Indiana"/"Indianapolis" false positives across 60 fetched jobs, all 3 real matches still genuine Hyderabad postings. **Lesson reinforced: any fetcher relying on `matcher.py`'s plain-substring `is_india_job()` for a tenant with real US locations containing the literal substring "india" (Indiana, Indianapolis) must add its own word-boundary guard — this is not automatically safe just because the location string "looks like" it says the country.**

Live validation (`--validate --companies <slug>` for all four: `ok`; then `run_company.run_company_pipeline` called directly against each real fetcher with a temporary seen-state file and a no-op notify function that always returns `False`, so nothing was sent and no real `seen_jobs_*.json` was touched or advanced):

| Company | Fetched | Matched | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| MSD (merck) | 40 | **4** | 2× `[.NET / C#]` (Full-Stack, Azure Full-Stack Developer), 2× `[AI / ML / Python]` (AI Engineering — explicit GenAI/RAG/LLM JD; Senior Specialist Software Engineering) — all genuine Hyderabad postings, tags correct, no location leakage |
| Abbott | 31 | 0 | Real India software titles exist (Cloud Software Engineer II, DevOps Engineer III, Staff Platform Engineer) but none currently name a primary .NET/C#/AI/ML/Python term in the JD body (Staff Platform Engineer's stack is Azure/Kubernetes/Terraform — all broad-only) — a true fact about this snapshot, all locations correctly say "India", zero leakage |
| GSK | 10 | 0 | Full current India pool (10 jobs) is non-technical (Medical Writer/Quality/Sales/Site Engineering) — GSK's Bengaluru GCC tech pipeline is real but between bursts right now, zero leakage |
| Medtronic | 60 (435 pre-fix) | **3** | All 3 are genuine Hyderabad (Nanakramguda) `[.NET / C#]` matches (Enterprise Software Engineer II ×2, Senior Enterprise Software Engineer) — confirmed live that one of these mentions both C# and Python explicitly in the JD body; zero leakage after the Indiana fix |

None of the four got `require_tech_in_description` — every one has genuine, specific engineering titles (not generic IT-services level-bands), so Layer 3 alone is the right precision level, consistent with every other direct-employer pharma/GCC integration in this file (Novartis, Pfizer, GE HealthCare, Sanofi, Roche, J&J).

Added: `src/merck_fetcher.py`, `src/abbott_fetcher.py`, `src/gsk_fetcher.py`, `src/medtronic_fetcher.py`; all four registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA` (slugs `merck`/`abbott`/`gsk`/`medtronic`, all `strict=False`); `gsk` additionally added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` and `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS` (cache-once sitemap scan, same as Morningstar/Godrej); `merck_search`/`abbott_search`/`gsk_search`/`medtronic_search` added to `config.yaml`; `seen_jobs_merck.json`/`seen_jobs_abbott.json`/`seen_jobs_gsk.json`/`seen_jobs_medtronic.json` created empty. No genuine skips in this batch — all four were feasible.

## Allstate (2026-09-13): Workday GCC, feasible and onboarded — a new offset-wraparound tenant, and a genuine current-zero-match snapshot

Investigated Allstate (insurance, reported Bengaluru/Pune India GCC) as a candidate new company. `allstate.com/careers` redirects to `allstatecorporation.com/careers.aspx`, whose page links reveal the real ATS directly: `https://allstate.wd5.myworkdayjobs.com/allstate_careers/...` — Workday, same integration shape as Wells Fargo/Marsh McLennan/Mastercard/Citi in this repo. Confirmed with plain `requests.post` against `/wday/cxs/allstate/allstate_careers/jobs`, no Playwright needed.

**India presence confirmed NOT Pune-only** — the task's stated concern up front, since Pune is on the global `exclude_locations` list. Live facet probe (`Country` facet, India WID `c4f78be1a8f14da0ab49ce1162348a5e` — the same cross-tenant GUID seen at Wells Fargo/Citi, just under a differently-named facet key here) returned exactly 7 India jobs total, split across genuine Bengaluru postings (`"Ind – Blr Sez 1 (3Rd, 6Th & 7Th Floor)"`, `"Ind – Blr Sez 2 (All Other Floors)"` — 4 jobs) and Pune postings (`"Ind – Pune Sez 1 ..."`, plus one bare `"Pune"` — 3 jobs). Allstate India's own JD boilerplate confirms this is a real, two-city GCC set up in 2012 ("offices in Bengaluru and Pune"), not a structural Pune-only zero — onboarded per the playbook's standing rule that Bengaluru presence makes a company eligible regardless of any co-located Pune headcount (the global `exclude_locations` list drops the Pune-specific postings at match time, same as every other multi-city GCC in this repo).

**New location-string edge case found and handled:** none of Allstate's own India location strings ever say the word "India" — they say `"Ind – Blr Sez ..."`, `"Ind – Pune Sez ..."`, or even bare `"Pune"`. A first-draft safety-net filter (`"india" not in loc.lower() and not loc.lower().startswith("ind")`) wrongly dropped the bare-`"Pune"` posting (`R31859`, "Software Engineer Consultant II") since `"pune"` neither contains `"india"` nor starts with `"ind"` — caught immediately in live validation (6 of 7 known jobs returned instead of 7). Fixed by trusting the `Country` facet WID as the sole India gate (its result count exactly matches the known city breakdown) and only using the location string for cosmetic `", India"` normalization for `matcher.py`'s `is_india_job()`, not as a second filter — the same lesson as Persistent's `jobUrl`-slug-parsing and Invesco's city-token-list workarounds elsewhere in this file, a different specific mechanism.

**New wraparound-pagination tenant found:** Allstate's Workday tenant ignores `offset` entirely — `offset=0/7/10/20/50/100` all returned the identical first page — the same bug class as UBS/MUFG/Nvidia/PepsiCo/Salesforce documented above, now at one more tenant. Live validation caught this the expensive way: a direct `find_matching_jobs` call against the initial (non-cached) fetcher implementation hung for 3+ minutes because `matcher.py`'s generic pagination loop kept re-fetching the same 7-job page across ~29 offset increments per keyword × 10 default keywords, never reaching the natural "empty page" termination condition. Fixed with the same cache-once pattern as Deutsche Bank/Persistent/PepsiCo: `_fill_cache()` makes one unfiltered request (`searchText=""`, India facet applied) and `fetch_jobs()` slices the cached list locally by `start`/`num`, terminating correctly the moment `start` exceeds the true pool size. `allstate` added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` accordingly (searchText genuinely narrows on this tenant, but the whole pool is small enough that ignoring it for cache-once robustness costs nothing). Separately hit and fixed a `limit=50` → HTTP 400 on this tenant (Workday's per-tenant page-size cap here is ≤20, unlike Wells Fargo's higher ceiling) — cache fill uses `limit=20`, enough to cover the entire current 7-job pool in one page; a `[warn]` log fires if the API's own `total` field ever exceeds what one page returns, since `offset` cannot be used to reach a second page on this tenant.

**Current match count is a genuine 0, not a bug** — same class as AIG/Swiss Re/ANZ. Of the 7 India jobs, `exclude_locations` (global "Pune" entry) silently drops the 3 Pune postings first; of the remaining 4 Bengaluru jobs, 3 fail `title_family` (Vended Application Lead Consultant, SOC Analyst - Senior Consultant, Customer Service Associate III — none are engineering-titled) and the 4th ("Data Scientist Sr Consultant II") also fails `title_family` since its JD describes a generic "Big Data" analytics/ETL role with no named Python/ML framework or .NET/C# term — a `primary_skills` miss even if title matching were loosened. Verified live via `run_company`-equivalent direct `find_matching_jobs` call (temporary in-memory `known_ids`, no notifier invoked, `seen_jobs_allstate.json` untouched): `total_fetched=7`, `matched=0`, near-miss log shows exactly the 4 non-Pune jobs tagged `[title family]`, zero location leakage (no Pune/Chennai/Tamil Nadu/etc. string in any fetched or matched result).

Added: `src/allstate_fetcher.py`; registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA` (slug `allstate`, `strict=False`) and `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS`; `allstate_search` added to `config.yaml` (no `require_tech_in_description` — too small a pool and too few genuine engineering titles right now to justify Layer 4); `seen_jobs_allstate.json` created empty. Not a genuine skip — feasible ATS, genuine multi-city (non-Pune-only) India presence, onboarded despite a current 0-match snapshot.

## Ferguson and MKS Instruments (2026-09-13): both onboarded, one real Workday pagination bug fixed live

Two newer/smaller GCC-style India presences, investigated as a pair. Both turned out genuinely feasible with plain `requests` (no Playwright), both have a real, current, small India board — same "onboard pre-emptively, small board is still worth it" precedent as Zurich Insurance elsewhere in this file, except both of these already have live matches today, not zero.

**Ferguson — added via Talent500, not Ferguson's own ATS.** Ferguson's own corporate careers site is a genuine Workday tenant (`ferguson.wd1.myworkdayjobs.com/Ferguson_Experienced`), but it is US-only: the `Location_Country` facet only ever returns "United States of America", and a full-text search for "India"/"Bengaluru" returns zero real hits (the only "India" string matches were "Indianapolis, IN" substring false positives — the exact false-positive class already documented for Medtronic/PayPal/FactSet/DAZN). Ferguson's Bengaluru Global Capability Center (launched June 2025 in partnership with ANSR, per contemporaneous press coverage) posts its own openings on a completely separate channel: **Talent500** — the same third-party GCC/tech-hiring marketplace already integrated in this repo for American Airlines' "AA Tech Hub India" (`americanairlines_fetcher.py`). Ferguson's company slug on Talent500 is simply `ferguson` (confirmed live via `company.slug == "ferguson"` on every search hit and `talent500.com/jobs/ferguson/...` URLs). The API shape is identical to AA's integration (same vendor, same `v3/jobs/search/` + `/api/jobs/{id}/` endpoints — see `americanairlines_fetcher.py`'s docstring for the full discovery story of that vendor's `v3`-vs-`v5` search-endpoint trap) — `ferguson_fetcher.py` reuses the pattern with `_COMPANY_SLUG = "ferguson"`. Confirmed live: 13 total jobs, **100% genuinely Bengaluru, India** (`country.name == "India"` on every hit), spanning real software-engineering titles (Lead Cloud Engineer - Azure, Senior QA Engineer, Senior Full Stack Software Engineer, Lead Business Analyst, etc.). Fetcher caches the whole small pool once per process and ignores keyword/location, same discipline as AA Tech Hub India.

**MKS Instruments — added via Workday, but with a real, live-discovered pagination bug fixed before shipping.** MKS's branded `mks.com/careers` is an unrelated SAP Commerce/Spartacus Angular shell with almost no server-rendered content — not the real board. The real one was found via a separate India-specific landing page, `mks-india-careers.webflow.io` (referencing "600+ professionals across five India office locations": engineering/IT in Bengaluru, digital in Gurugram, R&D in Manesar), whose "View Open Roles" buttons point at a genuine Workday tenant: `mksinst.wd1.myworkdayjobs.com`, tenant `mksinst`, site `MKSCareersEMEA` (an EMEA-named site that, per the facet WID itself, also covers India — the same cross-tenant India GUID, `c4f78be1a8f14da0ab49ce1162348a5e`, already used by Wells Fargo/Citi-family tenants in this repo). Confirmed 25 real India jobs with the `locationCountry` facet applied, including genuine software roles ("Senior Software Engineer I", "Technical Lead - AI/ML") alongside MKS's real mechanical/chemistry/IT-ops mix (consistent with a photonics/semiconductor-equipment manufacturer's actual India engineering footprint, not a coverage error).

**Real bug found and fixed during live validation — this tenant's pagination is unreliable in three different ways simultaneously, and the first version of the fetcher hung production-shaped code for several minutes before the fix:**
1. `limit` is hard-capped at 20 — 21+ returns a bare HTTP 400 with no body.
2. For any keyword whose true result count is ≤ 20 (fits on one page), `offset` is silently ignored entirely: `offset=0`, `offset=20`, `offset=40` for `searchText="software engineer"` (true total 8) all returned the *identical* 8-job first page, with `total: 8` reported correctly every single time. Since `jobPostings` is never empty, a naive per-keyword pagination loop (matcher.py's own loop only terminates on an empty page or the `max_listings` cap) spins re-fetching the same page until `max_listings` (200) — confirmed live: the very first validation run hung for several minutes on exactly this, one keyword at a time, before being killed and diagnosed.
3. For the full unfiltered pool (`searchText=""`, true total 25, genuinely more than one page): `offset=0` correctly returns page 1 (20 jobs, `total: 25`); `offset=20` correctly returns page 2 (5 new jobs) but the same response reports `total: 0` (the Accenture-style "`total` resets on later pages" quirk already in this file's bug table); `offset=40` (past the true total) *wraps back around* and re-returns page 1's exact contents with `total: 25` again — the same "pagination wraps past total" class already documented for UBS/MUFG/Nvidia/Pfizer/Walmart.

None of `total`, `offset`, or "is `jobPostings` empty" is trustworthy alone on this tenant, so rather than patch matcher.py's generic pagination with yet another tenant-specific special case, `mks_fetcher.py` was rewritten to sidestep the problem entirely: it caches the ~25-job India pool once per process with a single unfiltered `searchText=""` sweep, walking pages until a page produces zero previously-unseen job IDs (the one reliable "this wrapped around, stop" signal found), hard-capped at 20 pages as defense-in-depth, and ignores the caller's keyword/location — matcher.py's own client-side dedup covers the narrowing server-side `searchText` would otherwise have provided. Same "small board, cache once" discipline as Ferguson/AA Tech Hub India/Lufthansa/Boeing. Re-validated after the fix: completes in seconds (not minutes), 25 fetched, 1 genuine match, zero location leakage.

Other MKS quirks handled: every direct posting's `locationsText` already starts with the literal token "India" (e.g. "India Bangalore Whitefield", "India Gurgaon", "India Manesar") — a controlled facet string, not a free-text US city name, so no Indiana/Indianapolis substring risk here despite the tenant having no explicit country-code field on each posting. Some postings show ambiguous "2 Locations"/"3 Locations" instead of a named city (Workday's standard multi-site-req collapsing, same shape as Maersk/SimCorp) — since the `locationCountry` facet is already applied server-side, these are safely normalized to plain "India" rather than dropped.

Live validation (`--validate --companies ferguson,mks`: both `ok`; then `run_company.run_company_pipeline` called directly against each real fetcher with a temporary seen-state file and a no-op notify function returning `False`, so nothing was sent and no real `seen_jobs_*.json` was touched or advanced):

| Company | Fetched | Matched | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Ferguson | 13 | 0 | Genuine current snapshot: near-misses span `[title family]` (QA/Network/Business Analyst titles), `[10+ yoe]`, `[skill]` (two Software Engineer titles whose JDs name no primary skill), and `[broad-only]` (SQL Server/Azure/TypeScript-only Full Stack/Cloud Engineer roles) — no location leakage, all 13 genuinely Bengaluru, India |
| MKS Instruments | 25 | **1** | 1× `[.NET / C#]` ("Senior Software Engineer I", Bengaluru Whitefield) — genuine match, correct tag; near-misses are real (SAP/Chemist/Sourcing/Territory-Manager roles correctly excluded by title family or `exclude_terms`, one Senior Engineer II correctly dropped for no matching skill in a verified 4760-char description); zero location leakage, all locations say "India ..." |

Neither got `require_tech_in_description` — both have genuine, specific engineering titles (not generic IT-services level-bands), so Layer 3 alone is the right precision level, consistent with every other direct-employer/GCC integration in this file.

Added: `src/ferguson_fetcher.py`, `src/mks_fetcher.py`; both registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA` (slugs `ferguson`/`mks`, `strict=False`) and `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` (both are cache-once, small-pool fetchers that ignore keyword/location); `ferguson_search`/`mks_search` added to `config.yaml`; `seen_jobs_ferguson.json`/`seen_jobs_mks.json` created empty. No genuine skips in this pair — both were feasible, and both have live matches or a clean current-zero explanation, not a coverage gap.

## Procter & Gamble (added) and Unilever (genuine skip) — CPG/FMCG GCC follow-up (2026-09-13)

User-prompted follow-on to the existing CPG/retail peers (PepsiCo, Target, Walmart): investigated both P&G and Unilever independently for a real India tech/GCC hiring surface, per the standing instruction not to assume from precedent.

**Procter & Gamble — added.** `pgcareers.com` is a Phenom People CareerConnect tenant (`cdn.phenompeople.com/CareerConnectResources/PGBPGNGLOBAL/...`, `id="_PCM"` on `<html>` — same platform fingerprint already documented for GSK/Godrej/Morningstar), confirmed live via a plain `curl` on the homepage (redirects 303 to `/in/en` with a `PHPPPE_ACT` Phenom session cookie). Its `/api/apply/v2/jobs` endpoint rejects plain requests (`{"status":"failure","errorCode":null,"errorMsg":"Tenant not identified"}`), so this fetcher uses the same sitemap-scan workaround already proven for GSK: `pgcareers.com/sitemap.xml` 303-redirects to `/global/en/sitemap_index.xml`, which lists two child sitemaps covering ~841 URLs total, ~140 of them real `/global/en/job/{reqId}/{slug}` postings (the rest are static/marketing pages, filtered by URL shape). Each job page's `JobPosting` JSON-LD block (`jobLocation.address.addressCountry`) gives location/title/date/description directly — confirmed clean and consistent, same as GSK.

**Real bug found and worked around during investigation, not just theoretical: pgcareers.com's CloudFront-backed WAF blanket-blocks the `/job/` path after a parallel burst.** A first attempt to scan the ~140-job pool with 20-way parallel `curl` requests got every job (confirmed via the `"addressCountry":"India"` substring) correctly counted once, but every *subsequent* `/global/en/job/*` request from the same IP then started returning a plain-text CloudFront `403 ERROR ... Request blocked` page (not JSON, not Phenom's own 403) for several minutes, while the homepage kept returning 200 — a temporary rate-limit block, not a permanent WAF signature mismatch (confirmed it self-cleared after ~10 minutes with no header/UA changes). `pg_fetcher.py`'s `_fill_cache` therefore walks job pages strictly **sequentially** with a `0.3s` inter-request delay, never in parallel, and treats a non-200 status on an individual job page as "skip this one" rather than a fatal error, so one WAF hiccup can't zero out a run.

Confirmed live: 28 of ~140 currently open global postings are India-based (Mumbai/Bangalore GCC), and — unlike the FMCG-majority pattern this task predicted as "likely" for both companies — a genuine, current slate of software-engineering titles exists: "Senior Data Engineer" (×2), "DevOps Engineer", "Information Security Engineer", "Smart Automations Platform Engineer", "Director-IT-Solution-Architecture", "Data Scientist", plus "Digital Product Manager" (×2) and IT internships, alongside the expected Sales/Marketing/R&D/Finance/HR/Legal majority. `require_tech_in_description` was **not** enabled — titles are specific, genuine engineering titles (not generic IT-services level-bands), consistent with every other direct-employer GCC integration in this file.

Live validation (`--validate --companies pg`: `ok`; then `run_company.run_company_pipeline` called directly with a temporary seen-state path and a no-op `notify_func` that always returns `False` — `seen_jobs_pg.json` was never touched or advanced, `delivery_failed: True`/`state_updated: False` in the returned counters confirm this): **27 unique India jobs fetched** (one fewer than the 28 found during initial investigation minutes earlier — real-time churn on this tenant, not a fetcher discrepancy), **1 genuine match** — the second "Senior Data Engineer" posting (`R000158486`, Nanakramguda/Hyderabad) — with its sibling `R000151006` (same title, different req) correctly dropped by the `[10+ yoe]` experience-floor filter. Near-miss log confirms every other rejection is genuine: `[exclude]` correctly caught Manager-titled and Internship postings (Key Account Manager, Medical Manager, Communications Manager, Digital Product Manager ×2, Manager-HR, IT Internship ×2); `[title family]` correctly caught non-engineering titles (Information Security Engineer, Director IT Solution Architecture, Data Scientist, Scientist, Legal Counsel, R&D Director/Packaging Leader, Media Planner, Territory Sales/Business Executive, Graphic Specialist, event-program names); `[broad-only]` correctly dropped "Smart Automations Platform Engineer" (Azure only) and "DevOps Engineer" (Azure/Python only, no primary-skill term). All locations across the fetched set are Mumbai or Nanakramguda/Hyderabad — zero Pune/Kochi/Chandigarh/Chennai/Tamil Nadu/Lucknow/Kolkata leakage.

Added: `src/pg_fetcher.py`; registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA` (slug `pg`, `strict=False`) and both `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS`/`_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS` (cache-once sitemap scan, same as GSK/Godrej/Morningstar); `pg_search` added to `config.yaml`; `seen_jobs_pg.json` created empty.

**Unilever — genuine skip, HUL disambiguation confirmed live per the task's own warning.** Checked directly whether Hindustan Unilever (India's actual operating entity) runs a separate careers/ATS from global Unilever, rather than assuming: `hul.co.in/careers/` and `www.hul.co.in/careers/` both 301-redirect straight to `careers.unilever.com/en/india` — HUL has **no separate ATS or tech-hub site**; India hiring runs entirely through the one global Unilever tenant. (`www.unilever.com/careers/` itself 403s to a plain `curl`/browser-UA request — Akamai-fronted — but that's just the marketing shell in front of the same backend, not a second ATS; `careers.unilever.com` is unaffected by that Akamai wall and returns clean 200s.)

That tenant is TalentBrew (`tbcdn.talentbrew.com` CDN, `search-results__job-*`/`global-job-list__title` markup, org id `34155` — same vendor already documented for Boeing/Disney/AstraZeneca/British Airways), confirmed via its own `sitemap.xml` which advertises a canonical India-country facet URL: `careers.unilever.com/en/location/india-jobs/34155/1269750/2` (facetTerm `1269750` = India, facetType `2` = country — the identical facet-ID convention already seen on Boeing's tenant, a different org but the same TalentBrew vendor default). Confirmed live that this tenant's free-text `?k=`/`?l=` keyword/location query params are **both silently ignored** (`/en/search-jobs?k=engineer&l=India` returns the same 53-job count and the same non-India-heavy result set — Thailand/US/Australia/Philippines/South Africa locations — regardless of the `l=India` param), the same "location facet page is the only reliable India-only surface" finding already documented for Boeing; pagination on the facet page is a simple path-appended page number (`.../1269750/2/2`, `.../1269750/2/3`), unlike Boeing's undocumented-POST dead end.

**Walked the India facet to its real, complete end — all 3 pages, not just page 1 — and independently cross-checked the total:** `data-total-results="18"` on every page, and all 18 titles were enumerated: Assistant Finance Manager (×3), Assistant Manager – HR Talent and Capability (Beauty & Well-Being India), Group P&L Manager, Territory Sales Officer (×2), Sr. Key Account Executive, Sales Executive, Assistant Factory Engineer, Director Market Operations - Procurement (Europe, Africa, PTAB) and VMD, Legal Associate, Senior Designer, Design Manager/Sr. Design Manager, Senior Logistics Executive, Finance Executive, Sr Finance Executive - PACE. **Zero of the 18 are software/tech engineering roles** — "Assistant Factory Engineer" is a manufacturing/plant-engineering title, not SDE, and none of the 18 would pass `title_family` even before any skill check. This is a stronger case than a "0 current matches" GCC (e.g. GSK/Abbott elsewhere in this file, which have real engineering titles in their pool that just don't currently pass a skill gate) — Unilever's complete, fully-enumerated India pool has no engineering-titled candidate at all today. **Current, verifiable fact, not a fetcher limitation**: Unilever/HUL has no live India software-engineering hiring surface on its only real careers backend right now. No fetcher, registry entry, config section, or `seen_jobs_unilever.json` was created.

## Cybersecurity majors (2026-09-13): Palo Alto Networks, CrowdStrike, Fortinet, Zscaler, Okta added; CyberArk a genuine skip (folded into Palo Alto Networks post-acquisition)

User-prompted batch from a lower-confidence research pass (India GCC details not independently confirmed going in). All 6 were investigated live and independently — real DevTools/curl probing of each company's actual careers backend, no ATS guessed from precedent — per the standing "a URL-shape guess is not verification" lesson. Result: 5 of 6 added, all with genuine India software-engineering signal; 1 genuine skip for a specific, current, structural reason (not a "couldn't find it").

**Palo Alto Networks (`paloaltonetworks`) — added, a new ATS vendor shape for this repo.** `jobs.paloaltonetworks.com` is Radancy/TalentBrew — the same vendor family already documented for Boeing/Disney/Unilever/British Airways elsewhere in this file, confirmed via `tbcdn.talentbrew.com`/`radancy` strings in the page source and the identical `en/location/india-jobs/{orgId}/1269750/2` facet-URL convention (facet term `1269750` = India, shared across orgs) — but themed with a different CSS class family ("section29"/"section30") than those companies' "search-results__job-*" theme, so it wasn't recognized as the same vendor from URL shape alone. Fully server-rendered HTML, no JS/XHR needed at all. The real backing ATS behind "Apply" is Workday (`paloaltonetworks.wd5.myworkdayjobs.com`), but the public search/listing surface never touches that API. The site's separate keyword-search path (`/en/search-jobs/{keyword}/...`) returns the GLOBAL pool, not India-scoped — confirmed by testing "software engineer" and finding US/UK/Israel results mixed in — so only the India location-facet listing is used, cached once (`~130` jobs across pagination). Detail pages embed a full schema.org `JobPosting` JSON-LD block with `description`/`datePosted` — no separate detail API call needed. Live validation: 130 unique jobs fetched, **9 genuine matches** (Sr. Staff Engineer Software Full Stack, Staff Machine Learning Engineer, Senior IT Software Engineer, Staff Engineer Software, Senior Staff Software Engineer ×2, Application Engineer, IT Software Engineer, Senior Staff IT Software Engineer (AI & SAP) — mix of `.NET / C#` and `AI / ML / Python` tags, several genuinely hybrid), correct `[exclude]`/`[title family]`/`[broad-only]`/`[skill]`/`[10+ yoe]` near-miss tags on the rest, no Pune/Kochi/Chandigarh/Chennai/Tamil Nadu leakage (only Bangalore/Mumbai/New Delhi/a bare "500081" pincode location appeared).

**CrowdStrike (`crowdstrike`) — added, standard Workday tenant.** `crowdstrike.wd5.myworkdayjobs.com`, tenant `crowdstrike`, site `crowdstrikecareers` — found via the real "Apply"-linked URL on crowdstrike.com/en-us/careers/, not guessed. India locationCountry WID is the same cross-tenant shared GUID (`c4f78be1a8f14da0ab49ce1162348a5e`) already documented for Wells Fargo/Novartis/Fidelity/Citi/Sanofi/etc. Tenant returns HTTP 400 above `limit=20` — fetcher clamps defensively (Shell lesson). `searchText` genuinely narrows server-side. Live validation: 36 unique jobs fetched, **1 genuine match** ("Lead AI Engineer" — `AI / ML / Python`), correct near-miss tagging on the rest (several real "Engineer II/III"/"Sr. Engineer" titles fail `title_family`'s phrase list — the same known abbreviated-title precision gap already flagged repeatedly in this file, not a new bug), no excluded-city leakage (Bangalore/Pune/Mumbai/Delhi/Remote only).

**Fortinet (`fortinet`) — added, Oracle HCM Cloud CE, a new India-filtering approach.** `edel.fa.us2.oraclecloud.com`, site `CX_2001`, found via the fortinet.com/corporate/careers page's own outbound network request, not guessed. This tenant's location facet only ever surfaces its top-10-by-volume countries (US/Canada/Germany/Japan/France/...) — India's small share never appears there, the same "no usable location facet" shape already documented for Genpact/WTW. Unlike those two, though, every requisition here carries a reliable `PrimaryLocationCountry` ISO code (e.g. `"IN"`) alongside the free-text `PrimaryLocation` — filtering on the ISO code instead of a location substring sidesteps the classic "Indianapolis" false-positive trap entirely (confirmed live: a naive `"india" in PrimaryLocation.lower()` scan would have wrongly kept 3 genuine "Indianapolis, IN, United States" US postings). The ~930-job global pool is cached once via 5 requests at `limit=200`/page. Live validation: 30 unique India jobs fetched (of 930 global, zero leakage), **0 matches** — a true reflection of the current pool, not a bug: near-miss tags are all correct (`[title family]`/`[exclude]`/`[broad-only]`), and the only two real engineering-titled candidates ("Staff Software Development Engineer", "Staff Devops Engineer") fail on `[broad-only]` (Azure/Python only, no primary-skill term) — same disposition already established for J&J/Sanofi elsewhere in this file (real fetched jobs, correct filtering, genuinely zero current hard-skill matches).

**Zscaler (`zscaler`) — added, Greenhouse with a new location-string shape.** `boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/zscaler/jobs` — same cache-once/ignore-keywords Greenhouse family as MongoDB/Okta/Glean/Groww/Razorpay/AlphaSense. New quirk not previously seen in this repo: `location.name` uses the bare 3-letter ISO code `"IND"` (e.g. `"Bangalore, IND"`), never the word "India" — a naive substring scan would catch only the one posting whose location literally says "Remote - India" and would ALSO false-positive on "Remote - Indiana, USA" (present in this exact board, confirming the risk is real, not theoretical — "indiana" contains "india" as a substring). Fixed with a word-boundary regex matching only the standalone `IND` token (`\bIND\b`, which never matches inside "Indiana"/"Indianapolis" since a word char follows immediately, unlike the free-standing abbreviation), replacing it with `"India"`. Live validation: 78 unique India jobs fetched once normalized (of 371 global, confirmed zero Indiana/USA leakage after the fix — both "Remote - Indiana, USA" postings correctly excluded), **2 genuine matches** ("Senior Machine Learning Engineer (Agentic AI)", "Staff Software Development Engineer" — both `AI / ML / Python`), very strong latent engineering signal in the near-miss log (Principal SDE - Rust, Sr. Staff SDE - Golang + Control Plane, multiple Staff/Sr. Staff SDE roles) mostly blocked by `[broad-only]`/`[10+ yoe]`/`[skill]` — genuine current filtering, not a bug. Mohali (a real Zscaler India office, Punjab) appears throughout and is correctly NOT excluded — it isn't in `default_exclude_locations`, same treatment as Gurgaon/Mumbai/Hyderabad.

**Okta (`okta`) — added, Greenhouse, cleanest of the five.** `boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/okta/jobs` — same Greenhouse family, but location is already a clean, unambiguous `"Bengaluru, India"` string for every India posting, no bare-city or country-code normalization needed at all. Live validation: 108 unique India jobs fetched (of 324 global) — Okta's India Innovation Centre — **10 genuine matches** (Staff Software Engineer-Salesforce ×2, Software Engineer Professional Services R&D, Senior Software Engineer ×3, Staff Software Engineer - Windows, Principal Software Engineer AI Engineering, Staff Engineer FGA DevEx — mix of `.NET / C#` and `AI / ML / Python`), by far the strongest current match volume of the five, correct near-miss tagging on the rest (`[title family]`/`[exclude]`/`[broad-only]`/`[10+ yoe]`), zero excluded-city leakage (100% Bengaluru).

**CyberArk (`cyberark`) — genuine skip, folded into the Palo Alto Networks pipeline post-acquisition, not independently onboardable.** Palo Alto Networks completed its $21.1B acquisition of CyberArk on February 11, 2026 (confirmed via PANW's own press release and SEC filings). CyberArk's standalone `careers.cyberark.com` now 403s with a Cloudflare CAPTCHA challenge to every request (plain `curl`, browser UA, all rejected identically), and following its `all-job-openings` redirect chain lands on a **Palo Alto Networks** marketing page (`paloaltonetworks.com/idira`, PANW's Identity Security product line — CyberArk's post-acquisition positioning) — not a live independent careers backend. Confirmed directly and conclusively: searching the *already-onboarded* `jobs.paloaltonetworks.com` board (org id `47263`) for `"cyberark"` returns 14 real, live results including "Privileged Access Management (PAM) Consultant," "Principal Account Executive, Machine Identity - India," and "Domain Consultant - IAM/IGA Solutions" — CyberArk's own former product lines and India hiring are now served through the single PANW Radancy/TalentBrew pipeline this batch already onboards, not a separate board. Onboarding CyberArk as its own pipeline would either scrape a dead/bot-gated tenant or duplicate-alert the exact same jobs `paloaltonetworks_fetcher.py` already surfaces. **Current, verifiable, structural fact, not a fetcher limitation**: CyberArk no longer has an independent India careers backend to onboard. No fetcher, registry entry, config section, or `seen_jobs_cyberark.json` was created.

Added: `src/paloaltonetworks_fetcher.py`, `src/crowdstrike_fetcher.py`, `src/fortinet_fetcher.py`, `src/zscaler_fetcher.py`, `src/okta_fetcher.py`; all five registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA` (`paloaltonetworks`/`fortinet`/`okta`/`zscaler` also added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` — all four are cache-once, ignore keyword/location server-side; `crowdstrike` is not, since Workday `searchText` genuinely narrows there; `okta`/`zscaler` also added to `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS`, matching their Greenhouse `content=true` inline-caching shape — `paloaltonetworks`/`fortinet`/`crowdstrike` fetch descriptions via a separate per-job call so were not added there). `paloaltonetworks_search`/`crowdstrike_search`/`fortinet_search`/`zscaler_search`/`okta_search` added to `config.yaml`; `seen_jobs_paloaltonetworks.json`/`seen_jobs_crowdstrike.json`/`seen_jobs_fortinet.json`/`seen_jobs_zscaler.json`/`seen_jobs_okta.json` created empty. All five validated via `--validate --companies <slug>` (`ok`) and a direct `run_company.run_company_pipeline` call per company with an injected fake notifier (`any_succeeded=False`) and a `tempfile`-backed seen path — the real `seen_jobs_*.json` files were confirmed still `[]` after the check, no real alerts were sent, and no production state was touched. CyberArk: no files created (genuine skip, folded into an already-onboarded sibling pipeline).

## Semiconductor/chip-design GCC batch (2026-09-13): AMD, Intel, Broadcom, Texas Instruments, Arm — all 5 onboarded, low-to-modest current match volume

Investigated all five of the requested major chip-design companies' India engineering GCCs. All five turned out genuinely feasible with plain HTTP (no Playwright needed for any of them), and none was force-matched — every one was checked honestly for whether a real software-engineering track exists alongside the expected RTL/ASIC/verification/embedded majority, per the task's own framing. As expected for this industry, current live match counts are low (0-4), but this is a real fact about each board's current snapshot, same class of finding as Micron/Nvidia/Genpact elsewhere in this file — the pipelines are wired correctly and will alert the moment a qualifying posting appears. **Every prior "ATS: X" guess for this batch would have been wrong if trusted** — none of the five was assumed from secondary research; each was identified from a fresh DevTools-equivalent probe (curl/requests against the real page and its embedded API markers), per the Wave 5/6 lesson.

**AMD** — `careers.amd.com`'s `<html>` tag embeds `data-jibe-search-version`, the iCIMS "Jibe" front end already known from PepsiCo/Schneider Electric/SITA/HealthEdge in this repo. `GET /api/jobs` is a plain public REST endpoint, ~192 India jobs (all Hyderabad), full HTML descriptions inline. New pagination-bug shape for this ATS family: `offset` silently replays page 1 regardless of value (not "ignored" like PepsiCo — genuinely re-serves identical content), but a 1-indexed `page` param works correctly; `limit` caps at 100. Cache-once, keyword ignored. **1 confirmed live match**: "Lead Machine Learning Engineer" (Hyderabad) explicitly names "large language model" in its JD body — a genuine `AI / ML / Python` hit, `title_family`'s existing `"machine learning engineer"` phrase catches it. Verified via `run_company.run_company_pipeline` against a throwaway seen-path with a notifier returning `False` (so state never advanced) — confirmed the real `seen_jobs_amd.json` stayed `[]` throughout.

**Intel** — `jobs.intel.com` 403s at a corporate WAF redirector, but `intel.wd1.myworkdayjobs.com/External` is a live Workday CXS tenant (found by probing the naming convention directly, not from any label). Same "no flat country facet" shape as Genpact/Nvidia: only a nested `locationMainGroup` → `locations` facet exists, India's sole entry being "India, Bangalore" (52 jobs). `limit` caps at 20 (HTTP 400 above); plain offset pagination runs out cleanly at this pool size (no wraparound). Bangalore pool is overwhelmingly circuit-design/verification/physical-design hardware; a thin software track exists ("Senior Development Tools Software Engineer" — Python/pytest/CI-CD PDK tooling, mentions "AI tools"/"AI agents" only generically, no hard `primary_skills` term; "Software/Enterprise Application Development Engineer") but none currently name a hard `.NET/C#`/AI term. **0 current matches — genuine**, confirmed via live near-miss log (`[title family]`/`[broad-only]` tags firing correctly, no location leakage — every result genuinely "India, Bangalore" or "2 Locations, India").

**Broadcom** — `broadcom.wd1.myworkdayjobs.com/External_Career` is a live Workday tenant. Same nested-facet shape as Intel, but India is split across 4 site WIDs spanning both the legacy chip-design business and VMware by Broadcom's cloud/software business (acquired 2023, sharing this tenant) — Bangalore Electronic City, Bangalore Kalyani Vista II, Hyderabad IT Park, and a Bangalore-remote entry, ~60 jobs total (all 4 WIDs passed together in one `appliedFacets.locations` list). **4 confirmed live matches**, all distinct req IDs (R026465-R026468) for the identical title "Principal Member of Technical Staff – AI Platform & ValueOps Financial Intelligence Solution" (Bangalore) — its JD explicitly names generative AI; `title_family`'s existing `"member of technical staff"` phrase catches it. A "Senior Software Architect" (Hyderabad) JD explicitly names LangChain/LangGraph/vector databases but currently fails only `title_family` (no `"architect"` phrase covered) — same "flag, don't silently patch" precision gap already documented elsewhere in this file for `title_family`, not fixed here.

**Texas Instruments** — `careers.ti.com/search-jobs` redirects to an Oracle HCM CE tenant, `edbz.fa.us2.oraclecloud.com`, site `CX` (same REST family as Chubb/Amex/Icertis/Hexaware, `chubb_fetcher.py` used as the direct template). Working India location facet (`selectedLocationsFacet=300000000361484`, ~104 Bengaluru jobs, no leakage). **New bug found and fixed during onboarding**: this tenant's finder syntax returns 0 jobs (not the unfiltered pool) when a literal `keyword="",` clause is present with an empty string — unlike Chubb's tenant, where the same empty-keyword clause is a harmless no-op. Fixed by omitting the `keyword=...,` clause entirely when there's no keyword, rather than passing `""`. Bengaluru pool is overwhelmingly analog/IC-design/verification/layout hardware; software-adjacent titles exist ("Application Developer" — pure SAP Basis admin JD, no hard skill; "Public Cloud Engineer", "Solutions Architect"/"Integration Architect" — excluded from `title_family`, no `"architect"` phrase, same gap as Broadcom above; "Senior Data Scientist" — `title_family` has no bare `"data scientist"` phrase). **0 current matches — genuine**, confirmed live.

**Arm** — `careers.arm.com` is TalentBrew (the vendor already known from Optum/Boeing/Disney/AstraZeneca in this repo) sitting in front of what's almost certainly an iCIMS apply/login backend (`icims.com/connect`, `icims.com/jobs/login` links) — but the entire searchable job list and full description text is served by TalentBrew's own HTML, so no separate API call is needed. Same "free-text location param ignored" shape as Boeing/Disney: the only reliable India-only surface is the dedicated facet path `/en/location/india-jobs/33099/1269750/2`, which paginates cleanly via a path-appended page number (same mechanism as Disney), 101 India jobs across 7 pages. New markup shape for this ATS vendor worth remembering: job title sits directly inside `<a class="job-card__title" data-job-id="...">`'s own text (no nested `<h2>` like Disney's tenant), and there's no per-card posting-date element at all (only the detail page's JSON-LD `datePosted`). Bengaluru/Noida pool is majority ASIC/RTL/SoC-verification hardware, but carries a genuinely large real software/cloud/platform-engineering track alongside it (Staff Software Engineer - Linux Kernel, multiple Principal/Staff Software Engineer titles, Staff DevOps Engineer, Staff Private Cloud Engineer, Senior Software Engineer – Networking for AI) — several directly matching the shared `title_family` list. Live JD checks (e.g. "Staff Private Cloud Engineer": Python/Terraform/Ansible/OpenStack/FastAPI/CI-CD) found broad-only Python signals but no hard `primary_skills` term today. **0 current matches, but a strong pool for future matches** — confirmed via live near-miss log, no Chennai/Pune/Tamil Nadu/Kochi/Chandigarh leakage (all results genuinely Bengaluru or Noida).

Added: `src/amd_fetcher.py`, `src/intel_fetcher.py`, `src/broadcom_fetcher.py`, `src/ti_fetcher.py`, `src/arm_fetcher.py`; all five registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA` (`amd`/`arm` also added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` since both fetchers ignore the keyword argument and always return their cache-once full India pool regardless — `intel`/`broadcom`/`ti` are not, since their Workday/Oracle `searchText`/`keyword` genuinely narrows results server-side; `amd` also added to `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS`, matching its Jibe-API inline-description shape — `intel`/`broadcom`/`ti`/`arm` fetch descriptions via a separate per-job call so were not added there). None added to `_SUPPORTS_LOCATION` (all five use a hardcoded India facet/URL rather than a genuine `location` passthrough parameter) or `_USES_PLAYWRIGHT` (plain HTTP/requests works for all five). `amd_search`/`intel_search`/`broadcom_search`/`ti_search`/`arm_search` added to `config.yaml` (no `require_tech_in_description` on any of the five — not warranted at this early, mostly-zero-match volume, per the playbook's own "don't add unless explicitly asked" guidance). `seen_jobs_amd.json`/`seen_jobs_intel.json`/`seen_jobs_broadcom.json`/`seen_jobs_ti.json`/`seen_jobs_arm.json` created empty. All five validated via `--validate --companies amd,intel,broadcom,ti,arm` (`ok`) and a direct `run_company.run_company_pipeline` call per company with a no-op/false-returning notifier and a `/tmp`-backed throwaway seen-state path — the real `seen_jobs_*.json` files were confirmed still `[]` after every check; one throwaway `/tmp` seen-state file was incidentally polluted by an earlier batch run whose notifier returned `True` (a test-harness mistake, not a production issue — no repo file was ever touched) and was manually reset before the final confirmation run. No real alerts were sent and no production state was touched at any point.
