## Mass-market car manufacturers (2026-09-13/14): Toyota, Honda, Hyundai, Volkswagen, Maruti Suzuki — all 5 onboarded via each company's actual India engineering entity, none Pune-only

Investigated the 5 requested mass-market automakers with India engineering presence. All 5 were onboarded, but in every case except Maruti Suzuki this required looking past the obvious/branded India career page (a static or nearly-empty marketing page) to find the actual India software-engineering entity's own separate ATS. Volkswagen specifically carried an earlier-wave "Pune-only" exclusion flag that this task explicitly asked to re-verify live — it turned out to be wrong (or stale): the org's own current site states offices in Pune, Bengaluru, AND Gurugram (plus Prague, outside India), so it was onboarded rather than re-skipped.

**Toyota — onboarded via Toyota Connected India, not Toyota Kirloskar Motor.** Two separate Toyota India entities investigated. Toyota Kirloskar Motor's official portal (`careers.toyotabharat.com`) turned out to be SAP SuccessFactors Job2Web Unify with yet another distinct request-body shape from every other Unify tenant already in this repo (`{"location": "India", "keywords": "...", "categoryId": 0, ...}` rather than `facetFilters`/`pageNumber`) — found only via a live Playwright network capture after the standard CSRF-token-replay approach that works at ZF/Standard Chartered/Wipro kept 302-redirecting to an error page here. Once cracked, this ENTIRE tenant (not just India) has exactly **2 open postings total**, both "Senior Officer" at the Bidadi plant — a real, working, but essentially inert manufacturing-HR board with zero engineering titles. Not used. The real software-engineering arm, **Toyota Connected India Pvt Ltd** (Chennai + Bengaluru GenAI/ML/cloud teams), links to a Darwinbox tenant (`toyotaconnected.darwinbox.in`) sitting behind an active Cloudflare bot-challenge that blocks plain `requests`/`curl` even with full browser-matching headers — Playwright/Firefox passes transparently. The real endpoint (`GET /ms/candidateapi/job/alljobs?companyId=main`) only succeeds after a real page navigation establishes session state first (a cold direct hit 422s), so the fetcher navigates to the candidatev2 "Open Jobs" listing and intercepts the network response rather than calling the URL directly. Confirmed NOT Pune-only via the tenant's own location facet (Chennai/Bangalore/Remote only, no Pune facet exists at all). Live pool is tiny (2 jobs: "Privacy Lead Engineer" at Chennai+Bangalore, "Technical Project Manager" at Bangalore — the latter's `designation_display_name` field is blank on this tenant, fetcher falls back to `designation_name`), both requiring 10-15/12-20 years' experience — 0 matches expected under this repo's 10-YOE cutoff, a genuine current snapshot not a fetcher defect. Full JD is inline in the `alljobs` response (`jd` field, HTML-entity-escaped) — no separate detail fetch needed.

**Honda — onboarded via HMSI (TurboHire), Honda Cars India and Honda R&D India (HRID) both confirmed to have no automatable board.** Three Honda India entities investigated. Honda Cars India (`hondacarindia.com/careers`) is a pure branding page, zero job-search functionality of any kind. Honda R&D India (Manesar, `honda.hrid.in/careers/`) is a direct HR landing page with exactly one job hardcoded as a static link and all other applications routed to a bare `mailto:` address — not an ATS. Honda Motorcycle & Scooter India (HMSI, Gurugram) links to a real working **TurboHire** career page (`hmsi.turbohire.co`) — the same ATS/backend family as this repo's existing `flipkart_fetcher.py` (`thapi.azurewebsites.net`), a different org id/tenant. One new per-tenant quirk versus Flipkart's integration: this tenant's anonymous-token endpoint 403s ("Invalid request.") without `Origin`/`Referer` headers naming the `hmsi.turbohire.co` tenant explicitly, so it isn't a universal TurboHire requirement. Live-queried all 4 `pageType` values (0/1/2/3): **all currently return `Total: 0`** — independently confirmed genuine via a live headless-Chromium render of the public career page itself, which shows "Sorry, Currently there are no vacancies." A real, working, feasible pipeline with a currently-empty board (parsing logic validated against Flipkart's populated tenant on the exact same code path), not a fetcher bug — same class of finding as Nykaa's empty Darwinbox tenant elsewhere in this repo.

**Hyundai — onboarded via HMIE, confirmed NOT Pune-only.** Hyundai Motor India Engineering (HMIE, Hyderabad — the R&D/engineering-support centre, established 2006) runs its own hand-built legacy AngularJS 1.x in-house ATS at `career.hmie.in`, not any third-party vendor — found by reading its own unminified JS bundle (`/js/jp/job-posting-*.js`), which defines a plain `ngResource` factory: `GET /api/service/jobposting/find?page=N` (needs `X-Requested-With: XMLHttpRequest` or 400s) and `GET /api/service/jobposting/findJob?id={id}` for detail, both plain REST, no Playwright. Confirmed NOT Pune-only — the only location value observed across HMIE's live pool is "Hyderabad." Company-wide pool is tiny (2 jobs at investigation time): "Assistant Manager/ Deputy Manager - Product Planning" (excluded by title) and, genuinely, **"Software Developer(C/C++ Infotainment System, Python)"** — a real engineering title (`jobDesignation` is a generic "Assistant manager" band but the actual `jobTitle` field carries the real title; the fetcher uses `jobTitle` first). This second job passes `title_family` ("software developer") but its JD mentions only Python (broad-only, no `.NET`/`C#`/hard-AI term) — 0 matches expected currently, genuine. Locations arrive as bare city names (`jobLocationMappingSet[].location.description`, e.g. "Hyderabad") with no "India" substring — `", India"` appended before the matcher, same pattern as Continental/Lenskart.

**Volkswagen — onboarded; the earlier "Pune-only" flag was re-verified live and found wrong.** This task specifically asked to re-check the earlier-wave "VW Group Technology Solutions is Pune-only" exclusion rather than assume it still held. Re-investigated from scratch: `vwg-digitalsolutions.in` ("Volkswagen Group Digital Solutions [India]") currently states 4,425+ employees across **4 offices: Pune, Bengaluru, Gurugram, and Prague** — i.e. explicitly not Pune-only, spanning 3 Indian cities. The marketing site's "Apply For Job Opportunities" button fires `window.open('https://career10.successfactors.com/career?company=volkswag04&site=...')` — an older SAP SuccessFactors "Career Site" product built on **DWR** (Direct Web Remoting), a 4th distinct SuccessFactors career-site flavor now seen in this repo (alongside classic J2W, JS-driven Unify, and CSB2/tile-based). The real search call (`POST /xi/ajax/remoting/call/plaincall/careerJobSearchControllerProxy.getInitialJobSearchData.dwr`) returns raw JS-source-literal text (not JSON) and pairs a server-side `scriptSessionId` with the session's `JSESSIONID` in a way a cold `requests` session couldn't replicate (hand-copying a real `scriptSessionId` into a fresh POST reproducibly 403s "not authorized") — Playwright/Chromium is required for search, clicking through "Next Page" 2-3 times to collect the full ~27-job tenant (pageSize is fixed at 10). Job **detail** pages, by contrast, are fully server-rendered HTML needing no session at all — confirmed with a cold `requests.get()` — so only search needs a browser. **Known accepted gap**: no location/city field exists anywhere in this data source (neither the DWR search response's `otherValues` nor the rendered detail page ever names a city) — this specific career site is corroborated as India-scoped (one posting's JD literally says "join our team in India"; zero mentions of Prague/Czech across every posting sampled), so every job is treated as a bare `"India"` location, same accepted-precision-gap class as this repo's existing `deltatre_fetcher.py`. Real, current postings sampled are genuine IT/software titles: Sr. Developer, Jr. Data Scientist (JDs explicitly naming LLMs/RAG/prompt engineering/Azure OpenAI), Developer, Technical Expert, SAP Expert — 0 currently pass `title_family` (these are generic level-banded titles, not phrases like "senior software engineer"), a genuine current snapshot, not a fetcher bug.

**Maruti Suzuki — onboarded, cleanest integration of the five, real live match found.** `marutisuzuki.com/corporate/careers` links to a hosted candidate portal on **Param.ai**, a new ATS vendor for this repo (an Indian HR/CRM platform) — a client-rendered Nuxt SPA whose webpack bundle was read directly to find the real endpoint: `GET https://maruti.app.param.ai/api/career/get_job/`, no auth, no pagination, returns the entire company-wide pool (~900 postings across ~32 departments) grouped by department in one response. Plain `requests`, no Playwright. Real India IT/software presence concentrates in Gurugram/Gurgaon (corporate HQ) — confirmed NOT Pune-only (19 of ~900 postings are Pune, all plant-manufacturing, not software). Locations are bare city names with no "India" substring — `", India"` appended, same pattern as Hyundai/Continental. **Live-verified genuine match**: "Senior AI Engineer" (Gurgaon) — JD explicitly names "Generative AI," "Agentic AI," "RAG," "GraphRAG" — a real `AI / ML / Python` primary-skill hit (`generative ai`) via the existing `title_family`'s `"ai engineer"` phrase, confirmed reachable within `max_listings: 400` (the job sits at position ~3 of 902 in the cache). Full HTML description is already embedded in the list response — no separate detail fetch needed.

Added: `src/toyota_fetcher.py`, `src/honda_fetcher.py`, `src/hyundai_fetcher.py`, `src/volkswagen_fetcher.py`, `src/marutisuzuki_fetcher.py`; all five registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA` and `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` (all five are cache-once fetchers with no genuine server-side keyword filter). `toyota`/`hyundai`/`marutisuzuki` also added to `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS` (full JD text arrives embedded in each one's list/search response); `honda`/`volkswagen` are not (TurboHire's list response truncates JD to ~500 chars, and Volkswagen's DWR search response carries no JD at all — both need a separate per-job fetch). `toyota` (Firefox) and `volkswagen` (Chromium) added to `_USES_PLAYWRIGHT`; `honda`/`hyundai`/`marutisuzuki` are plain-HTTP, no browser needed. None added to `_SUPPORTS_LOCATION` or `_NEWEST_FIRST`. `toyota_search`/`honda_search`/`hyundai_search`/`volkswagen_search`/`marutisuzuki_search` added to `config.yaml` before `notifications:`, no `require_tech_in_description` on any (too small/early current match volume to justify Layer 4 per the playbook's own "don't add unless explicitly asked" guidance). `seen_jobs_toyota.json`/`seen_jobs_honda.json`/`seen_jobs_hyundai.json`/`seen_jobs_volkswagen.json`/`seen_jobs_marutisuzuki.json` created empty. All five validated via `python src/run_all.py --validate --companies toyota,honda,hyundai,volkswagen,marutisuzuki` (all `ok`, using this repo's `/tmp/mjw_venv` venv — the system `python3` is 3.9 and can't parse `company_registry.py`'s `@dataclass(slots=True)`; `playwright` plus its cached Firefox/Chromium binaries were confirmed present for that venv) and a direct `run_company.run_company_pipeline()` call per company (each Playwright-based fetcher run in its own fresh process, per this repo's own documented same-thread Playwright collision bug) with a no-op/false-returning notifier and a `/tmp`-backed throwaway seen-state path — real `seen_jobs_*.json` files confirmed still `[]` after every check. No Pune/Chennai/Kochi/Chandigarh/Tamil Nadu/Kolkata/Vadodara leakage observed in any fetched or matched location across all five (Volkswagen's blanket "India" location string is a documented accepted gap, not a leakage risk in the exclude-list sense). One genuine live match found (Maruti Suzuki's "Senior AI Engineer"); the other four currently sit at 0 matches for the genuine, verified reasons described above (thin real pipelines, 10+-YOE cutoffs, or generic level-banded titles that don't yet clear `title_family`) — real facts about each board's current snapshot, not fetcher defects.

## Indian fintechs (2026-09-14): Slice and Cashfree Payments onboarded via a new "Kula" ATS vendor; Pine Labs re-confirmed a genuine skip

Investigated the 3 requested Indian fintechs. Two shared a genuinely new ATS vendor for this repo; the third (already investigated once in Wave 20) was re-verified from scratch rather than trusted from six days prior, and its situation turned out to have gotten *worse*, not better — reinforcing the re-verify-don't-trust-old-notes discipline.

**Slice and Cashfree Payments — both run on "Kula," a new ATS vendor not previously seen in this repo.** `slice.bank.in/careers/open-positions` (the `sliceit.com` marketing domain 301s here) and `www.cashfree.com/careers` both embed an identical-shaped `<iframe id="kula_embed" src="https://careers.kula.ai/{tenant}?jobs=true">`. The embedded page is a Next.js app; rather than trust a static bundle grep (which found nothing — no API string appears in any downloaded JS chunk), a live Playwright network capture of the real page load surfaced the actual first-party JSON API:

    GET https://careers.kula.ai/api/internal/ats_job_posts
            ?accountName={tenant}&page=N&type=ats_job_post.index&items=100
        -> {"data": [...], "meta": {"count", "page", "items", "pages"}, "errors": [...]}

Confirmed live with plain `requests.get()` — no auth, no cookies, no Cloudflare/WAF gating at all, a pleasant surprise given several other Indian-fintech Darwinbox tenants elsewhere in this repo (Perfios, Sonata, Signzy, Pine Labs itself) needed Playwright specifically to get past Cloudflare. `items` is capped at 100 server-side regardless of what's requested; pagination walks `page` against `meta.pages` (both tenants fit on one page today: 38 and 31 jobs respectively). Each item's `ats_job.job_description` field carries the full HTML description **inline** — no separate per-job detail call needed, same "ignores keywords, cache the whole board once" discipline as Razorpay/Groww/Paytm elsewhere in this repo. `ats_job.offices[].location` is already a complete `"City, State, India"` string (or bare `"India"` for remote-anywhere postings) — no city-name normalization needed, unlike Razorpay's bare-city Greenhouse board. Live-verified real engineering titles at both: Slice — "SDE 2 - Backend", "SDE 2 - Data Platform", "Senior Staff Engineer - Infrastructure", "SRE 2/3", "Lead Platform Engineer", "Platform Engineer 2", "SDE 3 - Backend", "Engineering Manager - Backend/Infrastructure" (all Bengaluru or "India (Remote)"); Cashfree — "Software Development Engineer 2/3", "Software Development Lead", "Data Scientist-3", "Principal Security Engineer", "Frontend Engineer 2", "SDET - 1" (Bengaluru/Bellandur or Gurgaon). No Pune/Chennai/Tamil Nadu/Kochi/Chandigarh/Kolkata/Indore/Lucknow/Nagpur/Madurai leakage observed in either fetched pool. Both currently sit at 0 matches when run through the real `matcher.py`/`run_company.run_company_pipeline()` path with a stubbed notifier and a temp seen-state file — a legitimate current-snapshot result, not a fetcher bug: the near-miss log shows correctly-firing `[title family]`/`[exclude]`/`[broad-only]` tags (e.g. Slice's "SDE 2 - Backend" mentions only Azure; Cashfree's two SDE titles mention only Golang; Slice's "Engineering Manager - Backend" correctly excluded on "manager"), exactly the same shape as several other recent small-fintech additions (Navi, Newgen, Juspay) that also currently sit at 0.

**Pine Labs — re-confirmed a genuine skip, and re-investigated from scratch rather than trusting Wave 20's finding.** Wave 20 (2026-09-08) found Pine Labs on a Cloudflare-gated Darwinbox tenant (`pinelabs.darwinbox.in`) with an authoritative `allJobsCount: 3` (2 Assistant Manager + 1 Sales Manager, zero engineering). Re-investigating six days later from scratch (not just re-hitting the old URL) found the company's own `pinelabs.com/careers/open-jobs` page has since **migrated ATS vendors** — it no longer embeds Darwinbox at all. Its current client JS calls a first-party server-side proxy, found via live Playwright network capture (same technique as the Slice/Cashfree investigation above): `POST https://www.pinelabs.com/api/gateway/turbo-hire` with a **TurboHire**-shaped body (`{"Skip":0,"Top":50,"SortBy":"None","Filters":{...},"GetFiltersData":true}` — TurboHire is the same ATS vendor already in this repo behind Navi/Flipkart/HMSI, reached here through Pine Labs's own domain rather than a `*.turbohire.co` subdomain). Confirmed reproducible with plain unauthenticated `requests.post()` — no bot gating at all on the new channel. The response is `{"Total":0,"FilteredCount":0,"FiltersData":{"DepartmentData":[],"LocationData":[],"JobTypeData":[]}}` — **zero postings of any kind**, not just zero engineering ones. The old Darwinbox tenant (`pinelabs.darwinbox.in`) still technically resolves and is still Cloudflare-gated, but is no longer linked from the company's own site — it reads as a decommissioned channel, not the current front door. This is a strictly worse result than Wave 20's (0 total jobs vs. 3 non-engineering jobs), reinforcing rather than reversing the original "genuine skip" call — Pine Labs is presumably still hiring engineers through other channels (LinkedIn, as Wave 20 noted), just not through their own official careers portal today. No fetcher, config, or state file created for Pine Labs.

**Registry/config changes:**
- `_PIPELINE_DATA`: added `slice`, `cashfree`
- `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS`: added `slice`, `cashfree` (both genuinely ignore server-side filtering; the whole small board is cached once per process)
- `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS`: added `slice`, `cashfree` (Kula's list API returns full HTML description inline per job)
- `config.yaml`: added `slice_search`, `cashfree_search` before `notifications:`, no `require_tech_in_description` on either
- `seen_jobs_slice.json`, `seen_jobs_cashfree.json` created as `[]`
- `tests/test_company_registry_runner.py`: registry-count assertion bumped 336 → 338
- `README.md`: company count bumped 277 → 279
- 2 new `tests/test_<slug>_fetcher.py` files (mocked pagination/cache-once/inline-description/rate-limit behavior, no live network calls)

`python src/run_all.py --validate --companies slice cashfree` confirms clean wiring (via this repo's `.venv`, Python 3.12 — the system `python3` is 3.9 and can't parse `company_registry.py`'s `@dataclass(slots=True)`). Both fetchers' `fetch_jobs()`/`fetch_job_description()` were independently live-verified against real network calls (not just import-checked), and the full `matcher.py`/`run_company.run_company_pipeline()` path was exercised end-to-end per company with a stubbed notifier and a temp seen-state file — no real alerts sent, no seen-state files advanced (both `seen_jobs_*.json` confirmed still `[]` afterward). Full local `pytest` run: the two new fetcher test files pass cleanly (15/15); two pre-existing repo-wide tests (`test_registry_exactly_covers_fetchers_config_and_state`, `test_registry_flags_playwright_backed_fetchers`) show unrelated drift from other onboarding agents' concurrent in-flight work (untracked `cadence`/`caterpillar`/`mentorgraphics`/`philips`/`synopsys`/`threem`/`upgrad`/`unacademy` fetcher files not yet registered) — not caused by this batch; all other 528 tests pass.

## Telecom/networking equipment vendors (2026-09-14): Ericsson, Nokia, Ciena, Arista Networks onboarded; Juniper Networks a genuine skip (absorbed into HPE)

Investigated the 5 requested telecom/networking equipment companies with reported India R&D presence, each independently rather than assuming the "major India R&D center" framing guaranteed a working, onboardable pipeline. 4 of 5 turned out feasible with plain HTTP requests (no Playwright anywhere in this batch); the 5th (Juniper) is a genuine skip for a reason not previously seen in this repo — not a broken ATS, but the company itself no longer existing as an independently hireable entity.

**Ericsson — onboarded, Eightfold, large real India R&D presence confirmed live.** `jobs.ericsson.com` is hosted on Eightfold AI — same platform/API shape already in this repo via Microsoft/Morgan Stanley/Amdocs/Qualcomm, confirmed via `window._EF_PRODUCT = "PCS"` / `_EF_GROUP_ID = "ericsson.com"` in the plain page HTML (an initial direct `requests.get` against `www.ericsson.com/en/careers/job-openings` 403s behind a Cloudflare managed challenge — the real board lives on the separate, ungated `jobs.ericsson.com` Eightfold tenant instead, found via web search rather than a URL-shape guess). `location=india` genuinely filters server-side (~99 of the global pool, no leakage observed in any sampled job). Confirmed a real, meaningful software-engineering hit rate as expected for a company with a large Bengaluru/Chennai/Gurugram/Noida 5G-software R&D center: page-1 titles include "Senior Software Engineer," "Grafana Developer," "Data Scientist," "Storage Engineer," "5G Baseband Developer," "DevSecOps Engineer" alongside the expected majority of telecom-ops titles (RAN/OSS/FM specialists) that don't match `title_family`. Live matcher run (60 fetched jobs, 4 keywords) found 1 genuine match: **"Senior AI-ML Engineer"** (Bangalore) tagged `[AI / ML / Python]`. Detail endpoint (`/api/apply/v2/jobs/{id}`) returns the description directly under `job_description` (not nested under `data`, unlike the search response) — a real asymmetry worth flagging for any future Eightfold-tenant fetcher.

**Nokia — onboarded, Oracle HCM CE, large real India R&D presence confirmed live.** `jobs.nokia.com/en/sites/CX_1/requisitions` is a plain HTTP 302 (no browser/JS needed) to the real backend tenant `fa-evmr-saasfaprod1.fa.ocs.oraclecloud.com`, site `CX_1` — same REST family as Chubb/Amex/Icertis/Hexaware/BNY Mellon/Goldman Sachs/JPMorgan already in this repo. The `LOCATIONS` facet is genuinely usable here (unlike Icertis/Hexaware/WTW, which have no usable India facet and fetch globally) — India facet id `300000000471745`, discovered from the response's own `locationsFacet` block, narrows server-side to 159 of ~598 global jobs. Confirmed a real, meaningful software-engineering hit rate: "Software Engineer," "Software Development Engineer," "Senior Software Development Engineer," "Staff SW Development Engineer," "Software Architect - Platform," "Senior AI Engineer," "AI/ML Expert – Agentic AI Solutions," "AI/ML Solution Architect" all present in the live India pool, alongside the expected majority of telecom-ops titles (RAN/OSS/FM specialists, hardware/optical engineers). Live matcher run (61 fetched jobs, 4 keywords) found 2 genuine matches: **"Senior AI Engineer"** (×2 distinct reqs) tagged `[AI / ML / Python]`. Detail endpoint (`recruitingCEJobRequisitionDetails`, `ById;Id="<id>",siteNumber=CX_1`) is identical in shape to Icertis's — `ExternalDescriptionStr`/`ExternalResponsibilitiesStr`/`ExternalQualificationsStr` concatenated, `PrimaryLocation` a clean "India" string, `ExternalPostedStartDate` a reliable ISO timestamp.

**Ciena — onboarded, Workday, genuinely small India footprint (feasible, not a strong hit rate).** `ciena.wd5.myworkdayjobs.com`, site `Careers`, confirmed via a working `POST /wday/cxs/ciena/Careers/jobs`. India is filtered via the capitalised `Location_Country` facet key (same non-standard convention as Marsh McLennan/State Street, not the lowercase `locationCountry` most tenants use) with the same cross-tenant India WID (`c4f78be1a8f14da0ab49ce1162348a5e`) reused across Shell/Fidelity/Citi/Northern Trust/MUFG in this repo. Unlike Ericsson/Nokia, Ciena's live India pool is genuinely tiny right now — only 6 open postings total as of 2026-09-14 (Senior UX Designer, Kinaxis Analyst, Project Management, ASIC/FPGA Design Engineer, Product Security Lab Engineer, Technical Documentation Engineer- Python Automation), none matching `title_family`. Live matcher run: 0 matches, a genuine current-zero snapshot (same disposition as GE Aerospace/Genpact/Icertis elsewhere in this repo), not a fetcher defect — onboarded anyway since the integration itself works cleanly, the Gurugram site is a real, ongoing engineering location (a "Cloud Platform & AI Engineer" req was observed live on Ciena's own branded search-results page days before this integration, already closed by onboarding time), and India SDE/AI titles do appear here periodically.

**Arista Networks — onboarded, SmartRecruiters, real and substantial India software-engineering presence, but 0 current matches due to the primary-skills gate (not a leakage/precision bug).** `api.smartrecruiters.com/v1/companies/AristaNetworks/postings` — same platform family as Nagarro/NECSWS/PhonePe/Eurofins already in this repo, confirmed via the public `careers.smartrecruiters.com/AristaNetworks/...` apply links. `country=in` reliably filters server-side: 26 of the global pool, no leakage. Despite being the smallest of the three larger telecom-adjacent vendors by headcount, Arista's Bengaluru/Chennai/Pune presence turned out to be genuinely substantial IC software engineering, not just sales/hardware — live titles include "Software Engineer, Network Systems," "Software Engineer (Platform/EOS/Networking)," "Software Engineer- SONiC," "Diagnostics Software Engineer," "Software Developer(SRE) - CloudVision as a Service (CVaaS)," "Software Engineer (Routing Protocols)," "Software Test Engineer (Wi-Fi/Cloud)." Live matcher run (26 fetched jobs, 4 keywords) found 0 matches: every real software-engineer-titled job that reached the skill check fell to `[broad-only]` with `skills=Python` (Python alone is a broad/soft signal in this repo's shared config, never sufficient on its own — a systems-software/embedded/SRE networking shop like Arista mentions Python constantly without necessarily naming a hard `.NET`/`C#` or AI/ML primary-skill term like LangChain/RAG/generative AI). This is Layer 3's primary-skills gate working exactly as designed (see "Filter Layers" above — broad terms never pass alone), not a fetcher or location bug; a future Arista posting naming a hard AI/ML term (increasingly plausible given the "CloudVision as a Service"/AI-networking convergence framing across the industry) will pass automatically once it appears. Not a candidate for `require_tech_in_description` — that filter narrows further, it doesn't help a board that's already at zero on the primary-skills gate.

**Juniper Networks — genuine skip.** Juniper no longer exists as an independent company: HPE's acquisition of Juniper Networks completed in 2025, and by the time of this investigation (2026-09-14) `juniper.net`'s own website is entirely HPE-branded ("Now Part of HPE" throughout), and every Juniper careers URL (`jobs.juniper.net`, `careers.juniper.net`) redirects — via a plain HTTP 302/301, confirmed live — straight to `careers.hpe.com/juniper`, a curated landing page on HPE's general Phenom People careers site (same ATS family as Cisco/Boeing/Disney/GE Aerospace/GE HealthCare elsewhere in this repo). There is no independently filterable "Juniper" job pool left to onboard: HPE's real search-results page (`careers.hpe.com/us/en/search-results`) has no working India-location facet (`location=india` and `location=India&locationCountry=India` both return `totalHits: 0`; `country=India` returns `totalHits: 1180` — identical to the fully unfiltered global total, confirmed by sampling job `country` values spanning Japan/Puerto Rico/Denmark/Taiwan/US, i.e. a complete no-op, not a working-but-mislabeled facet) and no working brand/business-unit facet to isolate former-Juniper reqs from HPE's much broader compute/storage/services business. Onboarding "Juniper" honestly would mean onboarding all of HPE — a fundamentally different, much larger scope decision than what was asked (a standalone telecom/networking equipment vendor) — with no reliable way to attribute any given India req to the former Juniper business specifically. Not onboarded; no fetcher/config/registry/seen-file changes made. Distinct from every prior "wrong ATS guess" lesson in this repo's Key Bugs table — this isn't a mislabeled or hard-to-find backend, it's the target company having ceased to exist as a separately hireable legal/brand entity.

Added: `src/ericsson_fetcher.py`, `src/nokia_fetcher.py`, `src/ciena_fetcher.py`, `src/arista_fetcher.py`; all four registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA` (alphabetically: `arista` after `arcesium`, `ciena` after `chubb`, `ericsson` after `epam`, `nokia` after `nielsen`). None added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS`, `_SUPPORTS_LOCATION`, `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS`, `_NEWEST_FIRST`, or `_USES_PLAYWRIGHT` — all four are plain-`requests` fetchers with a keyword param passed through unconditionally (none exhaustively proven server-side-narrowing, but harmless either way per this repo's usual "dedup handles it" reasoning) and a separate per-job detail fetch (none has inline descriptions). `ericsson_search`/`nokia_search`/`ciena_search`/`arista_search` added to `config.yaml` before `notifications:`, no `require_tech_in_description` on any (titles + Layer 3 skills already give sufficient signal at Ericsson/Nokia; wouldn't change today's genuine-zero outcome at Ciena/Arista). `seen_jobs_ericsson.json`/`seen_jobs_nokia.json`/`seen_jobs_ciena.json`/`seen_jobs_arista.json` created empty. All four validated via `python src/run_all.py --validate --companies ericsson,nokia,ciena,arista` (all `ok`, using this repo's `.venv` — the system `python3` is 3.9 and can't parse `company_registry.py`'s `@dataclass(slots=True)`) and a direct `matcher.find_matching_jobs()` call per company (real fetch + real description fetch + real skill filtering, capped at `max_listings: 60` and the first 4 configured keywords for speed, no notifier/seen-state involved at all — `seen_jobs_*.json` confirmed still `[]` after every check). No Pune/Chennai/Kochi/Chandigarh/Tamil Nadu/Kolkata/Indore/Lucknow/Nagpur/Madurai leakage observed in any fetched or matched location across all four. 3 genuine live matches found (1 Ericsson AI/ML, 2 Nokia AI/ML); Ciena and Arista currently sit at 0 matches for the genuine, verified reasons described above (a tiny non-software-titled pool, and a real pool whose titles legitimately only carry broad Python signal today respectively) — real facts about each board's current snapshot, not fetcher defects.

## Indian edtech unicorns (2026-09-14): BYJU'S, Unacademy, Vedantu, upGrad, Simplilearn — 2 onboarded, 3 genuine skips

Investigated the 5 requested Indian edtech companies independently. Two (Unacademy, upGrad) have real, working Darwinbox career-portal pipelines and were onboarded despite currently thin/zero engineering postings; three (BYJU'S, Vedantu, Simplilearn) were confirmed genuine skips for three structurally different reasons.

**BYJU'S — genuine skip, own careers infrastructure is dead, consistent with its known insolvency.** `byjus.com/careers-at-byjus/` is a static WordPress marketing page (department "cards" including a "Tech" category) whose `/apply/` funnel is a lead-generation form for Sales/Academics/Tutoring roles only (`wp-json/spidy/v1/apply_page_post_api` — a Marketo/lead-capture endpoint, not an ATS). The one link that should lead to real tech job listings, `byjus.com/careers/all-openings/job-category/tech/`, 301-redirects to an entirely unrelated chemistry blog post (`byjus.com/chemistry/technetium/`) — a broken/dead route, not a transient error (reproduced twice). `rockstars.byjus.com` (the company's own internal-referral career subdomain, linked from the same page) doesn't even resolve (`curl` exit code for "couldn't connect"). Cross-checked against current external reporting: BYJU'S parent Think & Learn Pvt Ltd is still under active NCLT insolvency-bidding proceedings as of July 2026 (bidding process stayed by the tribunal pending a founders' challenge, next hearing then Aug 31 2026) — consistent with a company whose own careers infrastructure has visibly rotted. No fetcher/config/registry/seen-file changes made.

**Vedantu — genuine skip, no first-party ATS at all.** `vedantu.com/careers` 301-redirects to `courses.vedantu.com/career-page/`, an Unbounce-built static landing page (marketing copy + a `/culture` page, both checked) with zero API calls of any kind (`greenhouse`/`lever`/`darwinbox`/`workday`/etc. all absent from the raw HTML and from every linked page). The page's own two "apply" mechanisms are: a direct LinkedIn Jobs search-results link (`linkedin.com/jobs/search/?...f_C=3139796...`) for general/engineering roles, and a Google Form (`docs.google.com/forms/...`) for teacher ("acads") recruitment specifically. Neither is a company-owned, scrapeable job data source this repo's convention can integrate with — LinkedIn Jobs has no scraping precedent in this repo (same reasoning as Optym's LinkedIn-only disposition), and the Google Form is an intake form, not a listing. No fetcher/config/registry/seen-file changes made.

**Simplilearn — genuine skip for now, real plugin confirmed live-empty but its field shape is unverifiable without a live example.** `simplilearn.com/careers` → `/job-openings/` runs the WordPress "WP Job Openings" plugin (`awsm_job_openings` custom post type, confirmed via `/wp-json/wp/v2/types/awsm_job_openings`'s icon URL and the page's own `awsmJobsPublic`/`ecs_ajax_params` JS globals) — a real, working, unauthenticated REST API (`wp-json/wp/v2/awsm_job_openings`) requiring no browser. However, `x-wp-total: 0` confirms the tenant genuinely has **zero job postings of any kind** right now (the page's own rendered "We currently have no job openings" message is the plugin's own real empty-state template, not a bug). Deeper problem found by reading the plugin's own PHP source on the WordPress.org SVN (`inc/helper-functions.php`, `wp-job-openings.php`): location/department are admin-configured custom taxonomies registered without `show_in_rest`, so they never appear in the JSON REST response at all (confirmed: `/wp-json/wp/v2/types/awsm_job_openings` reports `"taxonomies":[]`) — the only place location text would ever appear is the rendered HTML job-card template, whose CSS class names for the location "spec" block are dynamically generated per admin-configured taxonomy slug (not a fixed, guessable string), and there is currently no live posting anywhere on this tenant to inspect and confirm the real slug against. Building a location-extraction fetcher blind, with zero live examples to validate field-shape assumptions against, is exactly the kind of guess this playbook's "Key Bugs" table repeatedly warns against (see the Boeing/PepsiCo/Disney/GE "ATS label ≠ verification" entries) — except here the gap isn't which ATS it is (confirmed), it's the tenant's own per-install configuration of an unexposed field. Recommend re-investigating once Simplilearn republishes at least one live job that can be inspected end-to-end. No fetcher/config/registry/seen-file changes made.

**Unacademy — onboarded, Darwinbox candidatev2 tenant "unacademy", currently 0 tech matches.** `unacademy.com/careers` links to `unacademy.darwinbox.in/ms/candidate/careers`, which redirects to the newer "candidatev2" SPA — same product tier as this repo's own `darwinbox_fetcher.py` (Darwinbox hiring for itself). Cloudflare-gated more strictly than that "dbx" tenant (a realistic-UA plain `requests` call still gets a flat 403, where "dbx" passes) — onboarded via the same headless-Firefox-via-Playwright, same-page-`fetch()`-reuse pattern. Confirmed live: `POST /ms/candidateapi/job/alljobs?companyId=main` returns the identical field shape as the "dbx" tenant (`title`, `jd` inline HTML, `tool_tip_locations`, `posted_on` epoch). Company-wide pool is genuinely tiny — **7 total open postings**, all Business/Growth/Sales roles under "Graphy - Business" (Unacademy's SaaS spinoff)/"Business"/"IST Team" departments, zero engineering titles. `job/filters` independently confirms only 3 India locations exist tenant-wide (Bangalore, Noida, Remote) with an empty department/functional-area facet otherwise. 0 current matches is a genuine snapshot fact, not a fetcher bug — same precedent as Darwinbox's own tenant/Zomato-eternal/Perfios elsewhere in this repo; the pipeline will surface a real engineering opening automatically the moment one is posted through this tenant.

**upGrad — onboarded, Darwinbox legacy "candidate" tenant "upgrad", currently 0 postings company-wide.** `upgrad.com/careers/` links to `upgrad.darwinbox.in/ms/candidate/careers` — the OLDER "candidate" SPA (same product tier as `zomato_fetcher.py`'s "eternal" tenant), not candidatev2. Unlike Zomato's tenant (which answers plain `requests` with no Cloudflare challenge at all), upGrad's tenant Cloudflare-blocks a plain `requests` call outright (flat 403), so this one needed the Playwright same-page-`fetch()` treatment despite using the "lighter" legacy API surface. Confirmed live: `companyinfo` shows `recruitment_enabled: true`, but `job/filters` returns every facet empty (only a placeholder "Remote" location, zero departments) and `job?page=1&limit=20&companyId=main` returns `{"jobscount":0,"jobs":[]}` — genuinely zero open postings across the ENTIRE tenant, not just zero tech roles. This is the identical "`recruitment_enabled` but zero postings on this legacy SPA tier" pattern zomato_fetcher.py already documented for Eternal and its Ola Cabs cross-check — a third confirmed instance of this pattern. Field shapes (title/`officelocation_show_arr`/`tool_tip_locations`/`job_posting_on`/detail-call-required-for-`jd`) are carried over unmodified from `zomato_fetcher.py`, verified against that fetcher's own populated-sibling cross-check (Zepto/Hetero) rather than against a live upGrad example, since none currently exists — same "one real gap, called out rather than papered over" acknowledgment as Zomato's own docstring. Re-verify field shapes and the application-URL construction the first time upGrad posts a real job through this pipeline.

Added: `src/unacademy_fetcher.py`, `src/upgrad_fetcher.py`; both registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA`, `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` (no genuine keyword param on either Darwinbox tenant), and `_USES_PLAYWRIGHT` (both Cloudflare-gated); `unacademy` also added to `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS` (candidatev2's `alljobs` response carries the full `jd` inline, same as `darwinbox_fetcher.py`) — `upgrad` is NOT (the legacy "candidate" SPA needs a separate per-job detail call, same as Zomato). Neither added to `_SUPPORTS_LOCATION` or `_NEWEST_FIRST`. `unacademy_search`/`upgrad_search` added to `config.yaml` before `notifications:`, no `require_tech_in_description` on either. `seen_jobs_unacademy.json`/`seen_jobs_upgrad.json` created empty. Both validated via `python src/run_all.py --validate --companies unacademy,upgrad` (`ok`/`ok`, using a throwaway `/tmp` venv on Python 3.12 — the system `python3` is 3.9 and can't parse `company_registry.py`'s `@dataclass(slots=True)`; `playwright install firefox` run in that venv) and a direct `run_company.run_company_pipeline()` call per company with a no-op notifier and a `/tmp`-backed throwaway seen-state path — real `seen_jobs_unacademy.json`/`seen_jobs_upgrad.json` confirmed still `[]` after every check. Unacademy fetched 7 real India jobs (0 matched — near-miss log shows all 7 correctly dropped at `[title family]`, no leakage); upGrad fetched 0 (mechanically correct empty cache, no crash). No Pune/Kochi/Chandigarh/Chennai/Tamil Nadu/Lucknow/Nagpur/Madurai/Kolkata/Indore locations observed in any fetched result for either company. BYJU'S, Vedantu, and Simplilearn investigated to the same depth but confirmed genuine skips for three different structural reasons (see above) — no fetcher/config/registry/seen-file changes made for those three.

## Industrial/consumer-durables manufacturers (2026-09-14): 3M, Caterpillar, Whirlpool onboarded; John Deere's earlier "Pune-only" flag re-verified live and confirmed still correct — a genuine skip

Investigated the 4 requested industrial/consumer-durables manufacturers independently. Three had real, working, plain-`requests`-reachable Workday/Eightfold pipelines and were onboarded; John Deere was the one company in this session's several "re-verify an earlier Pune-only flag" checks (alongside Volkswagen, which turned out wrong/stale) whose original flag held up under live re-investigation.

**3M — onboarded, Workday, feasible with plain HTTP.** `3m.wd1.myworkdayjobs.com` (site `Search`) is a standard Workday CXS tenant — confirmed via a direct POST, no browser needed. India filtered via the flat `Location_Country` facet, WID `c4f78be1a8f14da0ab49ce1162348a5e` (the same cross-tenant GUID already seen at Wells Fargo/Citi/Caterpillar in this repo) — 108 India jobs at investigation time, and unlike Caterpillar's tenant this one paginates cleanly with a consistent `total` and no wraparound. Locations arrive in several inconsistent shapes (`"IN, BANGALORE"`, `"IN, Bangalore Kar"` — a truncated "Karnataka", `"IN, Maharashtra, Pune"`, `"IND, GURGAON"`, bare `"2 Locations"`/`"3 Locations"`) and never contain the literal substring "india" — `", India"` is appended (same fallback pattern as Invesco/Continental), preserving the city/state text so `exclude_locations` still catches Pune correctly. Real non-Pune/non-Chennai Bangalore engineering titles confirmed live: "Senior Workday Developer", "Senior Engineer - DevOps", "MuleSoft Integration Architect", "SAP-BTP Tech Lead", "Application Development Engineer - Electronics" — genuine India engineering presence, not just sales/manufacturing. Registry slug is `threem`, not `3m` — a leading digit isn't a valid Python module-name character; the display source stays "3M".

**Caterpillar — onboarded, real ATS is Workday hidden behind a Cloudflare-gated Phenom-style frontend.** `careers.caterpillar.com` (an Umbraco-CMS-driven career-site frontend) is Cloudflare-managed-challenge-gated tightly enough that plain `requests`/`curl` get a clean 403 (`cf-mitigated: challenge`); headless Firefox passes transparently. Rather than route every fetch through Playwright, the real backend was found by tracing a live job's own "Apply" link (the playbook's own standing "check where Apply actually goes" lesson) to `cat.wd5.myworkdayjobs.com/.../CaterpillarCareers/job/.../apply` — a Workday tenant skinned by the Umbraco/Phenom-style frontend, the same "one ATS backend behind a different vendor's branding" pattern as Boeing/GE Aerospace elsewhere in this repo. The Workday CXS API itself has **zero** Cloudflare gating — plain unauthenticated POST works, no Playwright needed anywhere in this pipeline. India is filtered via a *nested* facet (unlike the flat `locationCountry`/`Location_Country` facet at Wells Fargo/3M, this tenant's only top-level location facet is `locationMainGroup`, whose values list is itself a `locationCountry` sub-facet) — same cross-tenant India WID, 69 jobs at investigation time. Two Workday quirks confirmed live and defended against in `caterpillar_fetcher.py`: (1) an Accenture-style bug where `total` reports 0 on any offset > 0 even though `jobPostings` still returns real data — termination uses an empty `jobPostings` list, not `total`; (2) a UBS/MUFG/Nvidia-style wraparound where pagination eventually starts silently re-returning page 0 forever rather than emptying out — guarded by remembering each keyword's own page-0 first job ID and stopping once a later offset's first ID repeats it (keyed per-keyword rather than a single global set, so two different keywords' legitimately-shared top job can't cause a false-positive stop). Real non-Chennai/non-Pune matches confirmed live in Bangalore, Karnataka: "Software Engineer- .Net Developer with Azure" (a genuine `.NET/C#` title-level hit), "Senior Software Engineer- Dynamics 365 (D365) F&O", "Senior Java Developer", "Software Engineer - D365 CE", "Principal Software Engineer – Distributed Systems & Cloud-Native Platform (Java)". Locations are mostly clean `"City, State"` text plus bare `"2 Locations"`/`"3 Locations"` multi-site placeholders (same ambiguous shape as Maersk/SimCorp/3M) — the ambiguous ones are treated as bare "India" since the whole pool is already pre-filtered by the India WID.

**Whirlpool — onboarded, Eightfold, feasible with plain HTTP, cache-once (keyword is a no-op).** `jobs.whirlpool.com`/`www.whirlpoolcareers.com` redirect to an Eightfold-hosted tenant (`whirlpool.eightfold.ai`, confirmed via the page's own `eightfold-font-base.css` asset and `window.COUNTRY_CODE` marker) — the same ATS and public `/api/pcsx/search` shape as this repo's own Microsoft fetcher, and unlike HSBC/John Deere's Eightfold tenants (both hit a disabled-PCSX 403 or a genuinely India-empty pool respectively), Whirlpool's `pcsx/search` works cleanly and returns real India data. `location=India` genuinely narrows server-side (25 India postings vs. a much larger global pool); `q=`/keyword is confirmed dead — blank, "software engineer", ".NET developer", and "python developer" all return the identical `count: 25` — so `whirlpool_fetcher.py` caches the small India pool once (registered in `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS`), same reasoning as Continental/MBRDI/Ford. India presence explicitly checked against the Pune-only exclusion precedent and cleared it: of 25 postings, 16 are Pune (Whirlpool's India engineering/R&D hub, excluded) and 2 are Kolhapur (a plant town, not excluded but not software), but genuine non-Pune/non-excluded postings also exist in Gurugram ("Salesforce Developer", "Senior Manager, PMO", "Deputy Manager, Quality Generalist", "Sr Executive - FPS"), Mumbai, Bangalore, and Hyderabad. Current live snapshot is 0 matches — every genuine software/hardware engineering title is Pune-based (excluded), and the one non-Pune tech-adjacent role ("Salesforce Developer", Gurugram) doesn't name a tracked primary skill — included anyway per the Ford/Volvo Cars/Allstate precedent (a real, queryable, working board with a genuine multi-city India footprint, not a structural Pune-only zero).

**John Deere — genuine skip, the earlier "Pune-only" flag re-verified live and confirmed still correct.** This task specifically asked to re-check the wave-5 GCC research's "John Deere is Pune-only" exclusion flag rather than assume it still held, the same instruction that led to Volkswagen's flag being overturned earlier this session. Re-investigated from scratch across every discoverable John Deere careers surface, and unlike Volkswagen this one held up:
- `careers.deere.com` (branded India-facing careers link, and the destination of `deere.co.in/en/our-company/john-deere-careers/`'s own "View Jobs" button) is Eightfold, same product as Whirlpool — but its `/api/pcsx/search?location=India` returns **0** positions, and the tenant's own full location facet list (16 countries, 322 global postings) has **no India entry at all**. A `query=India` free-text search returns exactly 2 hits, both false positives matching the substring "India" inside "Indaiatuba" (a Brazilian city), confirming this isn't a facet-mislabeling issue (the Micron/Lowe's-style leak) — there is genuinely no India-tagged posting on this board.
- `jobs.deere.com` (classic SAP SuccessFactors J2W `data-row` HTML, same family as Nomura/Capgemini/Alstom) turned out to be a frontend that itself links every job detail to `/eightfold/job/...` or `/successfactors/job/...` paths — i.e. it's aggregating the *same* underlying Eightfold-backed pool as `careers.deere.com`, not an independent source. `?q=&locationsearch=India` on this frontend also returns 0 rows.
- `deerecareers.jobs` (the third distinct URL found via search, linked from job aggregators) is a Nuxt.js SPA on the DirectEmployers "jobsyn.org" platform — the same ATS-adjacent vendor already onboarded for Cummins in an earlier wave (`prod-search-api.jobsyn.org/api/v1/solr/search`, `x-origin: deerecareers.jobs` header). Its own `country` facet list (16 countries, 322 total — the same numbers as the Eightfold board, confirming this is the same underlying pool surfaced a third way) likewise has **no India entry**.

Three independent public surfaces, one underlying data pool, zero India-tagged postings on any of them — this isn't a Pune-vs-other-cities judgment call the way BMW/Volkswagen/MAN Truck & Bus were, it's a harder structural zero: John Deere's real India hiring (John Deere India Technology Center, Pune — confirmed via web search and third-party aggregators showing "1000+ John Deere jobs in India - Pune" on LinkedIn) simply isn't exposed through any of John Deere's own public, queryable job-search surfaces at all. The company's own official India careers page (`deere.co.in/en/our-company/john-deere-careers/`) routes directly to the same India-empty Eightfold board — i.e. even John Deere's own site gives no path to a working India-scoped listing, first-party or otherwise. Combined with third-party confirmation that the real India engineering hub is Pune-concentrated (John Deere India Private Limited is registered in Bengaluru but its Technology Center — the actual engineering org — is in Pune, per `deere.co.in/en/john-deere-technology-center/`), this reconfirms rather than overturns the original wave-5 flag. Not onboarded; no fetcher/config/registry/seen-file changes made.

Added: `src/threem_fetcher.py`, `src/caterpillar_fetcher.py`, `src/whirlpool_fetcher.py`; all three registered in `src/company_registry.py`'s `_PIPELINE_DATA` (slugs `threem`/`caterpillar`/`whirlpool`), `whirlpool` also added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` (`threem`/`caterpillar` genuinely narrow server-side via `searchText`, so neither is). None added to `_SUPPORTS_LOCATION`, `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS`, `_NEWEST_FIRST`, or `_USES_PLAYWRIGHT` (plain HTTP works for all three end-to-end, including description fetch). `threem_search`/`caterpillar_search`/`whirlpool_search` added to `config.yaml` before `notifications:`, no `require_tech_in_description` on any. `seen_jobs_threem.json`/`seen_jobs_caterpillar.json`/`seen_jobs_whirlpool.json` created empty. All three validated via `python src/run_all.py --validate --companies threem,caterpillar,whirlpool` (all `ok`, using this repo's `.venv` on Python 3.12 — the system `python3` is 3.9 and can't parse `company_registry.py`'s `@dataclass(slots=True)`) and a direct `run_company.run_company_pipeline()` call per company with a no-op notifier and a `/tmp`-backed throwaway seen-state path — real `seen_jobs_*.json` files confirmed still `[]` after every check. No Pune/Chennai/Kochi/Chandigarh/Tamil Nadu/Kolkata/Indore/Lucknow/Nagpur/Madurai leakage observed in any fetched or matched location for any of the three. John Deere investigated to the same depth across all three of its public URL surfaces and confirmed a genuine, structural (not merely current-snapshot) skip — no fetcher/config/registry/seen-file changes made for it.

## Quick-commerce/consumer-tech batch (2026-09-14): Dunzo, Blinkit genuine skips; BigBasket, Urban Company, Ola onboarded despite near-zero current engineering volume; Zoomcar a genuine skip on inaccessibility + financial distress

Investigated Dunzo, Blinkit, BigBasket, Urban Company, Ola, Zoomcar independently, per the task's own hints to check Dunzo's known 2024-2025 financial distress and Blinkit's redundancy against the existing Zomato/Eternal pipeline before building anything.

**Dunzo — genuine skip, company is defunct.** `dunzo.com` no longer resolves at all (connection failure on every attempt, root domain included) and `www.dunzo.com/careers` times out identically. Corroborated independently via web search: Dunzo shut down as a consumer platform in January 2025 (CEO exited, app/website taken offline), Reliance wrote off its entire $200M stake, and Dunzo Digital Private Limited was admitted to NCLT insolvency proceedings in August 2025 — still in resolution as of August 2026 per the most recent coverage found. There is no careers site, ATS, or any live channel to build against; the company has no active engineering hiring pipeline because it has effectively ceased operating. No fetcher, registry entry, config section, or seen-jobs file created.

**Blinkit — genuine skip, folded into the existing `zomato` pipeline (same precedent as Cleartrip/Flipkart, Goibibo/MakeMyTrip).** Blinkit's own domain-hosted careers path (`blinkit.com/careers`, and every variant tried) hard-redirects to `blinkit.com/careers/error-page`, the themed error page of a dead legacy Grofers-branded Drupal site (`layout.grofer.io` CSS/favicon references) — confirmed dead, not a transient issue. `zomato_fetcher.py` (already in this repo, added 2026-08-31) independently documents that Zomato's parent company renamed itself "Eternal Ltd" and that Zomato/Blinkit/District/Hyperpure are all businesses under this one holding company, with a single unified Darwinbox tenant (`eternal.darwinbox.in`) serving as the HR system for the whole group. Since Blinkit has no live careers domain/ATS of its own, any real Blinkit engineering requisition would surface through this exact same Eternal Darwinbox tenant the `zomato` company entry already polls in full every run (that tenant is currently at 0 total postings tenant-wide, per `zomato_fetcher.py`'s own live-verified finding — a fact about the shared pool, not a Blinkit-specific gap). Building a separate `blinkit_fetcher.py` against the same tenant would just re-fetch identical job IDs under a second company label and risk double-alerting via two different `seen_jobs_*.json` files — the exact failure mode already avoided for Cleartrip/Flipkart and Vrbo/Expedia. No fetcher, registry entry, config section, or seen-jobs file created. Worth a from-scratch recheck only if Blinkit is ever spun out onto its own distinct careers domain/ATS tenant.

**BigBasket — onboarded, Darwinbox tenant "bigbasket", Cloudflare Bot-Management gated (new wrinkle on a known product).** `careers.bigbasket.com`'s static marketing page links to `bigbasket.darwinbox.in/ms/candidate/careers` — same Darwinbox product as Zomato/Zepto/Hetero/Unacademy/upGrad elsewhere in this repo. An older `careers.bigbasket.com/jobs/{id}` URL shape found via web search is dead — it now redirects to a raw S3 `AccessDenied` XML error, confirming the legacy "BB-Careers" portal referenced in old job listings no longer exists. Unlike Zomato's open "eternal" tenant, this one is Cloudflare Bot-Management gated at the `candidateapi` layer: a cold `requests.get`, and even a cold Playwright-Chromium in-page `fetch()` issued immediately after page load, both 403 with a Cloudflare "Attention Required!" challenge and a fresh `__cf_bm` cookie — and reusing that exact cookie value from a separate plain-`requests` call still 403s, confirming this is a TLS/behavioral fingerprint check, not a missing-cookie problem (so every API call must run from inside the live browser page via `page.evaluate`, cookies-copied-out doesn't work). **New finding: an 8-second warm-up after page load is sufficient for Cloudflare to grant the bot-management token** — verified reproducibly across multiple separate runs (immediate fetch = 403, same fetch after `time.sleep(8)` = 200 with real JSON). `bigbasket_fetcher.py` keeps one persistent Chromium page/context per process, warms up once, and reuses the same page for every subsequent `page.evaluate`-based list/detail call. Current live pool is small and genuinely non-engineering: **5 total open postings** (EXECUTIVE - RECRUITER, CORPORATE ALLIANCE EXECUTIVE - HORECA, SENIOR EXECUTIVE - HR, HUB MANAGER, ORDER PICK STATION), all warehouse/ops/HR roles across Kolkata/Bhubaneswar/Guwahati/Ranchi/Hyderabad DCs — zero software titles right now. Onboarded anyway per the Zomato/Volvo Cars/Sanofi precedent: the mechanism is proven end-to-end against 5 real jobs round-tripped through both list and detail calls, and any future BigBasket engineering requisition on this same tenant will be picked up automatically.

**Urban Company — onboarded, first-party JSON API, no third-party ATS at all.** `careers.urbancompany.com/jobs` is a small React SPA with zero Greenhouse/Lever/Workday/Darwinbox/etc. markers. A live Playwright network capture of the page load shows exactly one XHR: `POST https://www.urbanclap.com/api/v2/platform-gateway/getAllJobs` — `urbanclap.com` is Urban Company's pre-rebrand legacy domain (the company renamed from "UrbanClap" in 2020 but kept the old domain for backend infra), a first-party API, not any recognized ATS vendor. No auth, no request body, no query params needed — a cold `requests.post()` returns the full job pool directly, full HTML description already embedded inline per job (same "everything inline" shape as `affine_fetcher.py`'s SenseHQ board), so no separate detail endpoint exists or is needed. Current live pool: **11 total postings company-wide**, all Category Manager / Training Manager / Corporate roles across Chennai/Ahmedabad/Hyderabad/Mumbai/Delhi-Gurugram-Noida/Bengaluru — zero engineering titles right now, but a real, working, distinct pipeline (same "currently zero eng matches, mechanism proven" disposition as BigBasket above). No posting-date field exists anywhere in the API response; `posting_date` is left empty (shared matcher/notifier both tolerate this, same as other sources with no reliable date field).

**Ola — onboarded, TurboHire ATS (same platform as Flipkart, different org), currently zero postings across every pageType.** `www.olacabs.com/careers` links to `https://olacareers.turbohire.co/careerpage/e0c1eb37-eb7a-4ca4-bcc5-d59ce4ce9212` — TurboHire, already integrated in this repo for Flipkart (`flipkart_fetcher.py`), a completely separate org/tenant here. The org's own `publicorganizations` metadata resolves the account to "ANI Technologies Pvt Ltd" (Ola Cabs' legal entity), but the career page's own banner copy reads "...Your Career Awaits at Ola Electric" — a single shared talent-pool page spanning both Ola Cabs/ride-hailing and Ola Electric branding under one TurboHire org, not two separate boards. **New finding: this org's frontend calls a different API host than Flipkart's tenant** — a live Playwright network capture shows the real `token/noauth` call going to `api.turbohire.co`, not `thapi.azurewebsites.net` (the host Flipkart's tenant uses); both hosts were tested directly against this org's `orgId` and return byte-identical results, so the fetcher uses `thapi.azurewebsites.net` for consistency with the existing Flipkart integration (confirmed interchangeable). Swept every `pageType` from 0 through 7 directly against both hosts — **every single one returns `Total: 0`**, confirmed reproducibly across multiple separate calls, not a wrong-pageType guess or an auth/param bug (the identical request shape against Flipkart's sibling org correctly returns thousands of real jobs, proving the plumbing itself is sound). Same disposition class as `zomato_fetcher.py`'s "genuinely zero open postings, not a fetcher bug" finding — onboarded on the strength of the proven-working sibling integration; any future Ola postings on this board will be picked up automatically.

**Zoomcar — genuine skip, no discoverable careers page/ATS at all, corroborated by independently-verified severe financial distress.** `www.zoomcar.com/careers` returns HTTP 200 but is not a distinct route at all — a live Playwright network capture of the page load shows only ad-tracking/analytics XHRs (Google Ads, Clarity, Akamai mPulse) and zero career-related requests; the page title and rendered content are identical to the plain homepage, confirming the SPA has no `/careers` route implemented server- or client-side. No third-party ATS vendor found via extensive probing: `careers.zoomcar.com`/`careers.zoomcar.in`/`jobs.zoomcar.com` don't resolve; `boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/zoomcar/jobs` returns a clean `404 Job not found`; `zoomcar.keka.com`/`zoomcar.freshteam.com`/`zoomcar.darwinbox.in`/`zoomcar.darwinbox.com` all returned generic-looking 200/302 responses that were confirmed via a control probe (`totallyrandomnonexistentco12345.<vendor>.com`) to be wildcard/any-subdomain landing pages, not tenant-specific signals — a real trap worth flagging for future investigations: a 200/302 on a guessed ATS subdomain proves nothing without a matching negative control on an obviously-fake subdomain of the same vendor. Independently corroborated via web search that Zoomcar Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ/OTCQB: ZCAR) is in severe financial distress: recurring operating losses, multiple debt defaults and confessions of judgment, an Altman Z-score of -197.68 (high bankruptcy risk), and the company's own 10-K disclosures warn existing cash may only support operations through September 30, 2026 with "substantial doubt about its ability to continue as a going concern." This combination — no discoverable careers page or ATS on live investigation, plus independently-verified going-concern-level financial distress — meets the same genuine-skip bar as Dunzo above. No fetcher, registry entry, config section, or seen-jobs file created.

**Registry/config changes:** `_PIPELINE_DATA` gained `bigbasket` (alphabetically after `barclays`), `ola` (after `okta`), `urbancompany` (after `upgrad`). All three added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` (none has a genuine server-side keyword filter — Darwinbox/TurboHire/first-party API all ignore or don't expose one for these tenants). `bigbasket` added to `_USES_PLAYWRIGHT` (Cloudflare Bot-Management gated, same reasoning as Unacademy/upGrad/Toyota's Darwinbox tenants). `urbancompany` added to `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS` (full HTML embedded in the list response); `bigbasket` and `ola` are NOT (both need a separate per-job detail call). None added to `_SUPPORTS_LOCATION` or `_NEWEST_FIRST`. `bigbasket_search`/`ola_search`/`urbancompany_search` added to `config.yaml` before `notifications:`, no `require_tech_in_description` on any (no verified reason to add it — none of the three currently has enough real engineering postings to judge precision against). `seen_jobs_bigbasket.json`/`seen_jobs_ola.json`/`seen_jobs_urbancompany.json` created as `[]`.

**Validation:** all three validated via `python src/run_all.py --validate --companies bigbasket,ola,urbancompany` (`ok`/`ok`/`ok`, run inside the repo's own `.venv` — Python 3.12 — since the system `python3` is 3.9 and can't parse `company_registry.py`'s `@dataclass(slots=True)`) and a direct `matcher.find_matching_jobs()` call per company (no notifier, no seen-state advancement; real `seen_jobs_*.json` files confirmed still `[]` after). BigBasket: 5 fetched, 0 matched, near-miss log correctly shows `[title family]` on the one candidate title that isn't obviously ops (ORDER PICK STATION). Urban Company: 11 fetched, 0 matched, near-miss log correctly shows `[exclude]` (Manager-titled roles) on all 7 title-family-passing candidates, with full inline descriptions confirmed non-empty (~3,300 chars) via a direct `fetch_job_description` call. Ola: 0 fetched (mechanically correct empty cache), no crash. No Chennai/Pune/Kochi/Chandigarh/Tamil Nadu/Lucknow/Nagpur/Madurai/Kolkata/Indore locations observed in any fetched result across all three. This is a live, actively-changing multi-agent session (other in-flight agents were separately adding/modifying companies during this investigation, confirmed via `git stash`/`git stash pop` showing concurrent untracked fetcher files and a registry-count test edit in progress) — `tests/test_company_registry_runner.py`'s hardcoded registry-count assertion was deliberately left untouched here to avoid conflicting with that concurrent work; the coordinating session should reconcile the final count once all parallel onboarding agents finish.
