## Semiconductor EDA/design-software batch (2026-09-14): Cadence, Synopsys, Siemens EDA (Mentor Graphics) onboarded; Ansys and Dassault Systèmes genuine skips

Investigated the 5 requested EDA (Electronic Design Automation) / design-software majors with major India R&D presence. As the task's own framing anticipated, this is a software-heavy peer group of the chip-design GCC batch (AMD/Intel/Broadcom/TI/Arm, 2026-09-13) — 3 of 5 onboarded with a real, non-trivial `.NET/C#`/AI-ML-Python software-track hit rate, one genuine redundancy (a 2025 acquisition folding into an already-onboarded tenant), and one genuine structural Pune-only skip.

**Cadence Design Systems — added.** `cadence.wd1.myworkdayjobs.com/External_Careers` is a live Workday CXS tenant (confirmed via a direct POST, not guessed) — the same cross-tenant India `Location_Country` WID (`c4f78be1a8f14da0ab49ce1162348a5e`) seen at Wells Fargo/Citi/TI narrows the ~601-job global pool to 188 India jobs (Noida/Bengaluru/Pune/Hyderabad — not Pune-only), `searchText` genuinely narrows server-side. **Bug found and fixed during onboarding**: this tenant's `locationsText` never contains the word "India" at all (just a bare site label like `"NOIDA"`, `"BANGALORE 05"`, or `"2 Locations"`) — an initial `if "india" not in loc.lower(): continue` safety-net check (copied from Wells Fargo's tenant, where `locationsText` does include the country) silently dropped every single result, a 0-jobs-fetched bug caught immediately by the live pipeline check, not a code-review guess. Fixed by appending `", India"` unconditionally instead of gating on a substring that this tenant's data never contains (results are already server-filtered by the India facet, so this is safe, same reasoning as the Alstom/Micron/Lowe's "middle ground" location-string entries elsewhere in this file). Live-validated via a direct `run_company.run_company_pipeline` call (no-op notifier, throwaway `/tmp` seen-state path): 141 unique jobs fetched, 1 confirmed genuine match — "Lead Software Engineer" (Noida, tagged `AI / ML / Python`) — near-miss log shows `[title family]`/`[exclude]`/`[skill]`/`[broad-only]`/`[10+ yoe]` firing correctly across a large genuine "Software Engineer II"/"Lead Software Engineer"/"Principal Software Engineer" population (much higher software-track density than the chip-design GCCs already in this repo, consistent with Cadence's own product being EDA software rather than physical chip design). No Pune/Chennai/Tamil Nadu/Kochi/Chandigarh leakage; real `seen_jobs_cadence.json` confirmed still `[]` after.

**Synopsys — added.** `careers.synopsys.com` is TalentBrew (org 44408 — the same vendor already confirmed for Arm/Boeing/Disney/AstraZeneca), a Bengaluru/Hyderabad/Pune/Noida GCC (not Pune-only). Free-text location params are unreliable on this tenant (same shape as Arm), so the fetcher uses TalentBrew's canonical India-facet URL (`/en/location/india-jobs/44408/1269750/2` — same cross-customer geonames/facet-type IDs as Arm's tenant), 117 India jobs across 8 pages, path-appended pagination. Markup differs from Arm's tenant (`<li class="search-results-list__list-item">` → `<a class="sr-job-link">` with a nested `<h2>` title and sibling `<span class="job-location">`, closer to Disney's nested-`<h2>` shape than Arm's flat title text) — a new card shape for this vendor worth remembering. Live-validated: 1 confirmed genuine match, "R&D Engineering, Staff Engineer (Saber Power Electronics Solutions)" (Bengaluru, tagged `.NET / C#`) — checked the actual JD text to rule out a template-boilerplate false positive (the Walmart "languages we also use" class of bug elsewhere in this file): confirmed genuine, explicitly requires "Strong proficiency in C#, .NET Framework, and WPF, including XAML, data binding... MVVM" for desktop EDA-tool GUI development, not incidental boilerplate. Near-miss log also surfaced a real precision gap already flagged elsewhere in this file for Broadcom/TI: several genuine software/AI roles ("Principal Engineer, Agentic AI (CAD/EDA)" — explicitly builds LLM/agent orchestration for EDA workflows; "Senior Staff R&D Engineer – C/C++, Python..."; "AI, Sr Architect") don't pass `title_family` today because bare "R&D Engineer"/"Architect" titles aren't covered — not fixed here, same "flag, don't silently patch" backlog. No Pune/Chennai/Tamil Nadu/Kochi/Chandigarh leakage; real `seen_jobs_synopsys.json` confirmed still `[]` after.

**Siemens EDA (Mentor Graphics) — added, as a genuinely separate pipeline from the existing `siemens_fetcher.py`.** Mentor Graphics was rebranded "Siemens EDA" after Siemens' 2017 acquisition and now sits under the "Siemens Digital Industries Software" business unit, whose careers site (`jobs.sw.siemens.com`) is confirmed **not** the same portal as `jobs.siemens.com` (the generic Siemens AG iCIMS portal this repo already scrapes for Buildings/Mobility/Smart Infrastructure roles — same "check for redundancy first" precedent as the Siemens Mobility investigation, but with the opposite result this time: genuinely distinct, not redundant). `jobs.sw.siemens.com` is a client-rendered Nuxt.js SPA with no server-side job data in its raw HTML — plain `requests`/`curl` see an empty shell — so its real backend had to be found via a headless-browser network trace: `prod-search-api.jobsyn.org`, a "jobsyn.org"/NLX job-distribution API, a vendor never seen before in this repo. The request requires an `x-origin: jobs.sw.siemens.com` header (found in the page's own CSP `connect-src` directive) — a plain `Origin`/`Referer` pair alone gets `{"errors": "Mismatched origin."}`. `GET /api/v1/solr/search?page=N&location=ind` (ISO-3166 alpha-3) returns full JSON with plain-text descriptions embedded inline (no separate detail fetch needed) and a real EDA business-structure facet confirming genuine Siemens EDA postings are present (12 of 57 India jobs). India pool spans Noida/Bengaluru/Pune/Hyderabad/Chennai/Mumbai (not Pune-only) and is majority genuine software engineering — much higher density than the chip-design GCCs, consistent with the task's own framing. Live-validated: 1 confirmed genuine match, "Senior Software Engineer, Simcenter X Platform" (Bengaluru, tagged `AI / ML / Python`). No Pune/Chennai/Tamil Nadu/Kochi/Chandigarh leakage (Chennai postings present in the raw pool were correctly excluded downstream); real `seen_jobs_mentorgraphics.json` confirmed still `[]` after.

**Ansys — genuine redundancy, no new fetcher created.** Synopsys completed its ~$35B acquisition of Ansys in July 2025. Confirmed live: `ansys.com/careers` redirects to `ansys.synopsys.com/careers`, which serves the **same TalentBrew org 44408 tenant** the new `synopsys_fetcher.py` already covers — confirmed by Ansys product names ("STK", "ODTK" — Ansys's satellite toolkit products) appearing directly in job titles returned by that same India-facet query (e.g. "Lead Aerospace Applications Engineer - STK & ODTK", Bengaluru). Same disposition as the Siemens Mobility/Siemens AG precedent (2026-09-13): a fully-merged sub-brand whose jobs already live inside an already-onboarded tenant's pool. No registry/config/fetcher changes needed or made for Ansys.

**Dassault Systèmes — genuine skip, structural Pune-only GCC.** `www.3ds.com/careers/jobs` is a client-rendered Angular/JS SPA; its real backend (found via headless-browser network capture) is `www.3ds.com/apisearch/card_search_api` — a proprietary Exalead/CloudView-based search engine (Dassault's own "3DEXPERIENCE"-adjacent CloudView product, a new-to-this-repo vendor), filterable via `card_content_categories:("country/India")` in its `q` query param (the exact filter syntax was captured from a live browser interaction with the site's own country-select dropdown, not guessed). Returns 36 India jobs with full title/location/apply-URL metadata inline (`content_title`/`content_info_2_value`/`content_cta_2_url` in each hit's `metas` array — a technically clean, feasible integration). However: 34 of the 36 India postings are Pune (`India, MH, Pune`) — the only 3 non-Pune postings are "Customer Trust Manager" (Bangalore), "GRC and PMO Compliance Officer" (Bangalore), and "CATIA Brand Sales Specialist" (Gurgaon), none of them engineering roles. Every genuine R&D/software title in the pool ("Front End Developer / AI Enablement", "Geometry Reconstruction C++ Software Engineer", "Software Engineer - .NET Full Stack (7-12 yrs)", "Principal Engineer (.Net Full Stack- 12-16 yrs)") is Pune-only — meaning `exclude_locations` would structurally zero out every addressable match regardless of ATS feasibility, the same "Pune-only GCC" disposition already established for John Deere/Mercedes-Benz R&D (pre-MBRDI)/VW Group Technology Solutions elsewhere in this file. Not onboarded; no fetcher, registry entry, config section, or seen-jobs file created.

**Registry/config changes:** `_PIPELINE_DATA` gained `cadence` (after `browserstack`), `mentorgraphics` (after `mbrdi`), `synopsys` (after `synchrony`). `synopsys` and `mentorgraphics` added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` (both TalentBrew/jobsyn tenants ignore keyword params server-side, confirmed empirically); `cadence` is NOT (Workday `searchText` genuinely narrows). `mentorgraphics` added to `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS` (full description embedded in the jobsyn solr search response); `cadence`/`synopsys` are not (both need a separate per-job detail call — Workday CXS detail JSON for Cadence, JSON-LD on the job-detail page for Synopsys). None added to `_SUPPORTS_LOCATION` (all three use a hardcoded India facet/filter rather than a genuine `location` passthrough) or `_USES_PLAYWRIGHT` (plain HTTP/`requests` works for all three at runtime — Playwright was used only during discovery to capture Siemens EDA's and Dassault's real XHR endpoints, not needed by the shipped fetchers). `cadence_search`/`mentorgraphics_search`/`synopsys_search` added to `config.yaml` before `notifications:` (no `require_tech_in_description` on any — not warranted at this early match volume, per the playbook's own "don't add unless explicitly asked" guidance). `seen_jobs_cadence.json`/`seen_jobs_mentorgraphics.json`/`seen_jobs_synopsys.json` created empty.

**Validation:** all three validated via `python src/run_all.py --validate --companies cadence,synopsys,mentorgraphics` (`ok`/`ok`/`ok`, run inside the repo's own `.venv` — Python 3.12 — since the system `python3` is 3.9 and can't parse `company_registry.py`'s `@dataclass(slots=True)`) and a direct `run_company.run_company_pipeline`/`matcher.find_matching_jobs` call per company with a no-op/false-returning notifier and a `/tmp`-backed throwaway seen-state path. Real `seen_jobs_cadence.json`/`seen_jobs_synopsys.json`/`seen_jobs_mentorgraphics.json` confirmed still `[]` after every check. No real alerts were sent and no production state was touched at any point. This was a live, actively-changing multi-agent session (concurrent edits to `PLAYBOOK.md`/`tests/test_company_registry_runner.py` from other in-flight onboarding agents observed mid-task) — `tests/test_company_registry_runner.py`'s hardcoded registry-count assertion was deliberately left untouched here to avoid conflicting with that concurrent work; the coordinating session should reconcile the final count once all parallel onboarding agents finish.

## Industrial/electronics GCC batch (2026-09-14): Philips, ABB, Rockwell Automation onboarded; GE Vernova onboarded as the distinct remaining GE entity

Investigated 4 companies flagged for reported India engineering/GCC presence. All 4 turned out feasible and were onboarded — no genuine skips this round.

**General Electric corporate check (done first, per task instructions).** `src/geaerospace_fetcher.py` and `src/gehealthcare_fetcher.py` already cover the two GE business-unit GCCs split out of the original conglomerate. The remaining major GE entity is **GE Vernova** (the energy-focused company spun off in 2024) — a genuinely distinct legal entity/careers site/Workday tenant, not a duplicate of either existing fetcher. There is no separate "GE Digital" or bare "General Electric" corporate careers site left to onboard beyond these three business units; GE Vernova is the complete remaining GE surface. Onboarded as `gevernova`.

**Philips** — `careers.philips.com`'s public frontend is Phenom People (refNum "PHILUS", same CDN family as GE Aerospace/GE HealthCare/Cisco in this repo), but every job's own SSR-embedded `applyUrl` points at `philips.wd3.myworkdayjobs.com` — the real backend is Workday, tenant "philips", site "jobs-and-careers" (confirmed live via `POST /wday/cxs/philips/jobs-and-careers/jobs`). Country facet is nested two levels deep (`locationMainGroup` → `locationHierarchy1`, same shape as Nvidia's tenant) with a tenant-specific India WID (`6e1b2a934716103c2adde1d57e7700ea`, not the shared cross-tenant GUID) — ~63 India jobs at investigation time, mostly Bangalore. `limit` > 20 returns a clean HTTP 400 (Northern Trust-style cap), clamped defensively. Onboarded as `philips`; fetcher at `src/philips_fetcher.py`.

**ABB** — `careers.abb`'s public frontend is also Phenom People (refNum "ABB1GLOBAL"). Its own `/widgets` search API returns `{"status":"failure"}` for a plain unauthenticated POST — the same broken shape documented for Morningstar in an earlier wave. Unlike Morningstar, though, ABB's Phenom search-results page still server-side-renders a real `"jobs":[...]` array directly into the page's `phApp.ddo` blob, and every job record there carries a real `applyUrl` pointing at `abb.wd3.myworkdayjobs.com` — exposing the actual backend: Workday, tenant "abb", site "External_Career_Page" (confirmed live via `POST /wday/cxs/abb/External_Career_Page/jobs`, ~2000+ jobs globally). This tenant has a clean `locationCountry` facet using the standard cross-tenant India WID (`c4f78be1a8f14da0ab49ce1162348a5e`, same as Adobe/Citi/Rockwell/Barclays) — ~217 India jobs, many genuine Bangalore software-engineering titles. Onboarded as `abb`; fetcher at `src/abb_fetcher.py`.

**Rockwell Automation** — `rockwellautomation.com/careers` links directly to `rockwellautomation.wd1.myworkdayjobs.com/External_Rockwell_Automation` — a plain, un-skinned Workday tenant (no Phenom/TalentBrew frontend layer at all), confirmed live via `POST /wday/cxs/rockwellautomation/External_Rockwell_Automation/jobs`. Clean `locationCountry` facet, standard cross-tenant India WID, ~56 India jobs including genuine matches (Data Engineer, Test Automation Engineer, Software Engineer - DevOps, IFS Cloud Developer). Onboarded as `rockwell`; fetcher at `src/rockwell_fetcher.py`.

**GE Vernova** — `careers.gevernova.com` links to `gevernova.wd5.myworkdayjobs.com/Vernova_ExternalSite`, confirmed live via `POST /wday/cxs/gevernova/Vernova_ExternalSite/jobs` (~2107 jobs globally). Like GE Aerospace, this tenant has **no country-level facet** — only a flat 424-entry city list under `locationMainGroup` → `locations`. Found India by scanning that facet's own descriptors for known India city names (Bengaluru, Chennai, Gurugram, Hyderabad, "Hyderabad TS IN 26", Mumbai, Noida, Pune, Vadodara) rather than assuming any "India" substring exists — each city can carry multiple distinct facet IDs (22 total India-city WIDs, all hardcoded and applied via `appliedFacets.locations`). Because filtering happens via specific city WIDs (not a generic country facet), ambiguous "N Locations" postings that match this facet are already confirmed India — no per-posting detail-page resolution needed (unlike SimCorp's ambiguous postings, which were NOT pre-filtered by any facet at all). ~303 India jobs at investigation time. Onboarded as `gevernova`; fetcher at `src/gevernova_fetcher.py`.

**New shared quirk confirmed across all 4:** every one of these tenants returns a clean HTTP 400 for `limit` > 20 (the "Northern Trust-style cap" pattern already documented in this file) — all four fetchers clamp `num` to 20 defensively via a `_MAX_LIMIT` constant, following the same discipline as Shell's silent-no-op variant.

**Philips near-miss finding, not fixed (flagged per this file's "flag, don't silently patch" discipline):** Philips titles its engineering ICs "Software Technologist I/II" and "Senior Software Technologist" rather than "Software Engineer" — `title_family` doesn't recognize "technologist" as a synonym, so genuinely strong matches (e.g. "Software Technologist - C# .NET Full Stack", "Lead Software Technologist - .Net Fullstack") are dropped at Layer 2 as `[title family]` near-misses instead of reaching the skill check. Confirmed live in the near-miss log. Not fixed here (a global `title_family` change affects all 280+ companies and needs the same deliberate review as the standing `exclude_terms`/"Sr."/abbreviation backlog already tracked in this file) — recorded so it isn't rediscovered as a surprise.

**Registry/config changes:** `_PIPELINE_DATA` gained `abb` (before `abbott`), `philips` (near `phonepe`), `rockwell` (after `roche`), `gevernova` (after `genpact`). None added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS`, `_SUPPORTS_LOCATION`, `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS`, `_NEWEST_FIRST`, or `_USES_PLAYWRIGHT` — all four genuinely narrow server-side via `searchText`, need a separate per-job Workday CXS detail call (not inline), return relative-dated results (no verified newest-first guarantee), and work over plain `requests` (no Playwright needed). `abb_search`/`philips_search`/`rockwell_search`/`gevernova_search` added to `config.yaml` before `notifications:` — none has `require_tech_in_description` (not warranted at this match volume, per the playbook's own default). `seen_jobs_abb.json`/`seen_jobs_philips.json`/`seen_jobs_rockwell.json`/`seen_jobs_gevernova.json` created empty.

**Validation:** all four validated via `python src/run_all.py --validate --companies abb,philips,rockwell,gevernova` (all `ok`, run inside the repo's own `.venv` — Python 3.12 — since system `python3` is 3.9 and can't parse `company_registry.py`'s `@dataclass(slots=True)`), plus a direct `find_matching_jobs`/`_apply_description_filter` call per company (no notifier invoked, no seen-state path touched — all four `seen_jobs_*.json` files confirmed still `[]` after every check). Live current-snapshot match counts: **ABB 4** (all genuine Bangalore Software/R&D/Full-Stack Engineer titles, tagged `.NET / C#`), **Philips 3** (Bangalore, tagged `.NET / C#` — see the "Software Technologist" title-family gap above for why more weren't caught), **Rockwell 1** (Bangalore, tagged both `.NET / C#` and `AI / ML / Python`), **GE Vernova 10** (Bengaluru/Noida mix, split across both tags including genuine "Lead Engineer - AI Engineering"/"Staff Software Engineer-Python, Gen AI,LLM" roles). No Chennai/Pune/Tamil Nadu/other excluded-city leakage observed in any matched result across all four. This was a live, actively-changing multi-agent session (concurrent edits to `company_registry.py`/`PLAYBOOK.md` from other in-flight onboarding agents observed mid-task, e.g. `bigbasket`, `cadence`, `cashfree` entries appearing between reads) — no attempt was made to reconcile registry-count-based test assertions; the coordinating session should do that once all parallel onboarding agents finish.

## Gaming companies batch (2026-09-14): EA, Ubisoft, Zynga onboarded; Dream11, MPL, Gameskraft, Nazara genuine skips

Investigated 7 gaming companies (EA, Ubisoft, Zynga, Dream11, Mobile Premier League, Gameskraft, Nazara Technologies) as an independent batch alongside other concurrent onboarding agents. **3 added, 4 genuine skips.**

**Electronic Arts (EA) — added.** New ATS vendor discovery for this company (but not for this repo — same platform as Macquarie): EA's careers site (`www.ea.com/careers` → `jobs.ea.com`) runs on **Avature**, confirmed by `avature.wizard.registrars` JS globals in the page source. A newer Avature theme than Macquarie's (`portalpacks/web/js/wizard-legacy...` bundle vs. Macquarie's plainer classic skin), but the underlying `GET .../SearchJobs/{keyword}?jobOffset=N` HTML endpoint works identically — plain `requests`, no session/cookie dance, no Playwright. Keyword search genuinely narrows results server-side (`search=pune` → 0 results, confirming EA's entire India footprint is its Hyderabad studio — no Pune-only concern). No posting-date field exists anywhere on this theme (search results or detail page), same acceptable gap as IBM's "no posting-date field exposed anywhere". Live-verified 2 genuine current matches: "Software Engineer III" (C# in body, tagged `.NET / C#`) and "Data Engineer II" (LangChain + LangGraph in body, tagged `AI / ML / Python`) — both real Hyderabad SDE roles, confirmed by reading the actual description text, not just relying on the tag. `require_tech_in_description` intentionally left off. Files: `src/ea_fetcher.py`, `ea_search` in `config.yaml`, `seen_jobs_ea.json` (empty), registry entry added before `ebay` in `_PIPELINE_DATA` (not added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS`/`_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS`/`_USES_PLAYWRIGHT` — keyword genuinely filters, detail needs a separate per-job HTTP call, plain `requests` suffices).

**Ubisoft — added, currently zero matches by design (all-Pune snapshot).** Ubisoft's branded React SPA careers page never exposes its API base URL in the shipped JS bundle; found the real backend via live web search referencing `careers.smartrecruiters.com/Ubisoft2` — **SmartRecruiters**, same ATS/API shape already integrated for Nagarro/NECSWS/PhonePe (mirrors `necsws_fetcher.py` almost exactly). Company identifier confirmed live as `Ubisoft2` (not the more obvious `Ubisoft`). `country=in` is a reliable, zero-leakage server-side filter. **Current India footprint (2026-09-14) is 100% Pune** — all 3 open India postings ("R&D Engineer (C++)", "R&D Engineer", "Technical Architect", all Pune Test studio roles) are excluded by this repo's standing Pune exclusion, so 0 matches today — confirmed live via `find_matching_jobs` (3 fetched, 0 matched, no near-miss noise since the exclusion fires before the near-miss log). Same "feasible ATS, temporarily/structurally zero matches" shape as Icertis/BNY Mellon (both onboarded anyway per standing playbook precedent) rather than a reason to skip — Ubisoft has 1,000+ India employees per its own careers-locations page and posts new India reqs regularly, so a non-Pune or non-testing opening is a realistic near-term event. Files: `src/ubisoft_fetcher.py`, `ubisoft_search` in `config.yaml`, `seen_jobs_ubisoft.json` (empty), registry entry added before `uipath`.

**Zynga — added, currently zero matches (non-engineering current postings, not a fetcher defect).** `www.zynga.com` and its `jobs.zynga.com` redirect target were unreachable over plain HTTP from this environment throughout the investigation (connection timeouts on every attempt, including via a separate server-side fetch tool on a different network path, despite DNS resolving fine) — a network-level block/outage unrelated to the ATS itself, not a reason to give up. Found the real ATS via live web search results referencing `job-boards.greenhouse.io/zyngacareers` job-detail URLs — **Greenhouse**, board token `zyngacareers` (a separate `zyngaearlycareers` board exists for interns/new grads only, not used). The public Greenhouse Job Board API (`boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/zyngacareers/jobs?content=true`) is reachable and reliable independent of the branded-domain issue — confirmed HTTP 200, 43 total postings, 7 in "Bengaluru, India" (Zynga's real, substantial India studio per Zynga's own "Zynga Expands its India Studio" blog post; Zynga is a Take-Two Interactive subsidiary but retains its own Greenhouse board). Same cache-once pattern as `groww_fetcher.py`/`razorpay_fetcher.py`. **All 7 current India postings are non-engineering** (Lead Game Designer, Lead Producer, Principal Data Analyst, Producer, Product Manager II ×2, Senior 2D Animator) — 0 matches today on a churning 43-job board, confirmed genuine via live near-miss log (all correctly dropped at `[title family]`/`[exclude]`, no false positives). A genuine SDE/AI-ML Bengaluru opening is a realistic near-term event on a board this size and active. Files: `src/zynga_fetcher.py`, `zynga_search` in `config.yaml`, `seen_jobs_zynga.json` (empty), registry entry added after `zomato`; added to `_IGNORES_KEYWORDS` and `_INLINE_DESCRIPTIONS` (same as Groww — keyword ignored server-side, full board cached, description embedded inline in the list response).

**Genuine skip (re-confirmed live): Dream11.** This repo's very first onboarding wave (2026-08-31) already flagged Dream11 as infeasible — its Lever board (`jobs.lever.co/dreamsports`) had gone dead. Re-verified from scratch rather than trusting the old note (per this session's own standing lesson that a prior "infeasible" flag can go stale, as Volkswagen's Pune-only claim did): `jobs.lever.co/dreamsports` still 404s today (also tried `dream11`/`dreamsportsgroup`/`fancode`/`dreamsports-group` slugs against the raw Lever API directly — all 404). More importantly, **DreamSports's own official careers page (`www.dreamsports.group/careers`, redirecting to `/lifeatdreamsports`) still literally links to the same dead `jobs.lever.co/dreamsports` URL** — the company's own site hasn't been updated to point anywhere else. Also checked `dream11.freshteam.com` and `dreamsports.keka.com` (both resolve but return "invalid domain"/"tenant not found" placeholder pages — dead ends, not real tenants). Live web search confirms current hiring activity is real but routed entirely through third-party staffing marketplaces — Uplers (17 open positions, Sep 2026) and Instahyre — the same disposition as the original finding, not a new fetchable ATS. No fetcher/config/state file created. Worth another recheck if DreamSports's own site is ever seen linking to a live, different board.

**Genuine skip: Mobile Premier League (MPL).** No discoverable first-party ATS. `www.mpl.live` has no `/careers`, `/jobs`, or similar path (all 404) and no "career" string anywhere in its homepage HTML. Systematically probed every ATS vendor seen elsewhere in this repo against MPL's likely tenant names — `mplsport.keka.com` (tenant-not-found placeholder), `mpl.freshteam.com` (invalid-domain placeholder), `jobs.lever.co/mpl` (404), `boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/mpl/jobs` (404, and the token collision risk with an unrelated "mpl" board makes this doubly moot) — all dead ends. Live web search confirms MPL is real (Bengaluru HQ, ~800 employees) and does have a small number of open roles (4 per Uplers, ~12 per Naukri as of mid-2026), but every source is a third-party aggregator (LinkedIn, Uplers, Instahyre, IIMjobs, Naukri) — no official company-run job board was found anywhere. No fetcher/config/state file created.

**Genuine skip: Gameskraft.** `gameskraft.com` (bare domain) and `www.gameskraft.com` (the domain search results list as canonical) are both **parked lander pages** (`window.LANDER_SYSTEM="PW"`, a GoDaddy/Wix-style parking-lot page) — confirmed by fetching both directly and by following a real job-detail URL surfaced in search results (`www.gameskraft.com/careers/apply/?p=<base64 job params>`), which itself redirects into the same parking lander. Root cause found via web search: Gameskraft's real-money-gaming business was shut down following India's **Promotion and Regulation of Online Gaming Act, 2025** (which banned real-money online games), triggering layoffs of 400+ employees and an organization-wide restructuring — the company's own web presence has been abandoned/parked, not merely redesigned. This is a stronger and more definitive infeasibility signal than any prior "genuine skip" in this table (Optym/Dream11/Temenos/Intellect all had a live-but-empty or third-party-only presence; Gameskraft's own domain is dead). No fetcher/config/state file created.

**Genuine skip: Nazara Technologies.** Nazara's careers page (`www.nazara.com/careers`) is a React SPA; inspected the full shipped JS bundle directly rather than guessing an ATS from the URL shape (per this repo's standing "URL guess ≠ verification" lesson) — it contains **no job-listing data or API endpoint at all**. The page's only careers-related content is a single `<a href="https://gn2h0l10.forms.app/applicationform">EXPLORE MORE</a>` link to a generic third-party form-builder (forms.app) intake form, confirmed by fetching that form directly: it asks candidates to freely type the "Position" they're applying for rather than presenting any list of open roles. There is no per-job ID, title, location, or description to enumerate — structurally incompatible with this repo's per-company fetcher model (there is no board to fetch, not merely a hard-to-parse one). Nazara's actual game studios (several subsidiaries were visible in the bundle's asset references — Nextwave Multimedia, Fusebox Games, Curve Games, etc.) may have their own separate hiring pipelines, but "Nazara Technologies" itself has none. No fetcher/config/state file created.

**Validation:** EA/Ubisoft/Zynga all validated via `python src/run_all.py --validate --companies ea,ubisoft,zynga` (all `ok`, run inside a fresh Python 3.12 venv since system `python3` is 3.9 and can't parse `company_registry.py`'s `@dataclass(slots=True)` — same version constraint noted by the concurrent ABB/Philips/Rockwell/GE Vernova onboarding wave above), plus a direct `find_matching_jobs` call per company with no notifier invoked and no seen-state path touched (`seen_jobs_ea.json`/`seen_jobs_ubisoft.json`/`seen_jobs_zynga.json` all confirmed still `[]` after every check). No Chennai/Pune/Tamil Nadu/other excluded-city leakage observed in any matched or near-miss result. This was also a live, actively-changing multi-agent session — several other in-flight onboarding agents' registry/config/PLAYBOOK.md entries (`abb`, `philips`, `rockwell`, `gevernova`, `cadence`, `cashfree`, `slice`, and others) appeared mid-task; no attempt was made to reconcile registry-count-based test assertions (`test_registry_exactly_covers_fetchers_config_and_state`, already failing before this batch's changes for the same reason) — left for the coordinating session once all parallel onboarding agents finish.
